*Zhaozong Wang,Guibin Sun,Jinyong Chen,Rui Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 

Method: 

Result: 

Conclusion: 

Abstract: Multi-robot systems can accomplish tasks that are difficult for a single robot by organizing into task-specific formations. Different from existing studies on multi-robot shape formation, we here study the task-oriented formation decision problem, with a focus on the herding task. This task is challenging due to the attackers' superior maneuverability and their unknown strategies. To address these challenges, we propose the following novel results. First, we encode the formation shape using a low-dimensional parameter vector. This parametric representation reformulates the formation decision as a parameter optimization problem, thereby resolving the limited flexibility of predefined shapes. By optimizing these formation parameters, the defenders' maneuverability disadvantage is mitigated through a formation shape that continuously adapts to task requirements. Second, we develop a reinforcement learning-based policy to regulate the formation parameters. Trained offline in simulations covering diverse attacking strategies, the learned policy can effectively handle adversarial unpredictability during online deployment. Comparative simulations against three baselines demonstrate that our method can successfully accomplish challenging herding tasks. Additional scalability simulations further verify its applicability to simulated scenarios involving dozens of robots. We also validate the practical feasibility of our method on a physical robotic platform with 3 attackers and 7 defenders.

</details>


### [23] [Ultra-Low-Impedance Robotic Gripper for High-Bandwidth and Transparent Physical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09198)
*Joon Lee,Ari Choi,Seokhwan Jeong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种9自由度、三指差动直驱（DDD）机械手，通过直驱电机与1:2低传动比差动机构结合，在无需外部力传感器的情况下兼顾抓取力、低惯量、低机械阻抗与运动灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机械夹爪依赖高传动比机构和外部力传感器获得较大抓取力，但高传动比会增加摩擦、折算惯量和机械阻抗，外部传感器则增加系统复杂度。因此，需要一种兼具较高输出力、物理透明性和运动灵活性的机械结构。

Method: 设计并实现9自由度三指DDD机械手。采用直驱电机将执行器质量集中在基座，以降低运动连杆惯量；采用1:2低传动比差动机构，使两个电机并联驱动，在手指屈曲时实现扭矩增益。同时通过原型实验评估抓取力、指尖力、系统惯量贡献和断电状态下的被动机械阻抗。

Result: 原型可产生约18 N的标称抓取力和4.7 N的指尖力。电机对系统惯量的贡献仅为0.236%，电机未通电时测得的最大被动机械阻抗为50.1 N/m，表明该机构具有较低的运动惯量和较高的物理透明性。

Conclusion: DDD结构有效缓解了输出扭矩、机械透明性与运动灵活性之间的矛盾，为高带宽人机交互以及基于本体感知的无传感器力估计提供了可行硬件基础。

Abstract: Conventional robotic grippers often use high-ratio transmissions to generate grasping torque and external force sensors to measure physical interaction. High-ratio transmissions increase friction, reflected inertia, and mechanical impedance, while external sensors add hardware complexity. To address these trade-offs, this study proposes a novel 9-DOF, three-fingered Differential Direct-Drive (DDD) gripper that combines DD motors with a low-ratio (1:2) differential transmission. The mechanism centralizes actuator mass at the base to minimize moving-link inertia, while the differential architecture couples two motors in parallel to amplify torque during flexion. Experiments show that the prototype delivers a nominal grasping force of approximately 18 N and a fingertip force of 4.7 N, while maintaining a low motor contribution to system inertia (0.236%) and low passive mechanical impedance, with a maximum measured value of 50.1 N/m when the motors are unpowered. The proposed hardware addresses the trade-offs among torque, physical transparency, and kinematic dexterity, providing a foundation for high-bandwidth interaction and sensorless proprioceptive force estimation.

</details>


### [24] [SAIN: Structure-Aware Interactive Navigation with Active Dialogue Grounding for Mobile Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09196)
*Yuhao Cao,Xiao Liu,Yang Xie,Lu Liu,Haoyao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAIN，一个零样本交互式实例目标导航框架，将主动对话获得的答案转化为持久化、结构化的导航状态，而非仅作为一次性文本提示，从而提升模糊指令下的实例搜索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航通常假设指令完整且明确，但真实人类指令可能存在歧义、信息不足或缺失。交互式实例目标导航要求智能体通过主动提问定位特定目标实例；现有对话方法往往只利用答案辅助当前决策，缺乏可持续的空间与对象级记忆，难以支持长程导航。

Method: SAIN将对话答案编译为目标证据、路径走廊记忆和对象候选标签，并分别存储于结构化的价值记忆、房间记忆、图记忆和对象记忆中。统一策略利用这些持久状态进行前沿区域排序和最终目标接近，实现对话信息向导航状态的转换，且无需针对任务进行策略训练。

Result: 在VL-LN IIGN基准上，SAIN相较于已报告的最强对话导航基线，将成功率SR从20.2提升至25.4，将路径加权成功率SPL从13.07提升至14.17。该方法为零样本框架，不需要任务特定的策略训练。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，将主动对话转换为持久化、结构化的导航状态，能够有效增强模糊指令下的长程交互式实例导航。SAIN的主要贡献在于统一利用空间、路径和对象级记忆，但摘要未说明不同记忆模块的独立贡献、统计显著性及额外计算或交互成本。

Abstract: Most existing vision-language navigation tasks assume that instructions are complete and unambiguous. However, real-world robots often encounter natural human instructions that are ambiguous, underspecified, or incomplete, requiring them to resolve such uncertainties through active questioning. Interactive Instance Goal Navigation (IIGN) requires an embodied agent to find the specific instance under an ambiguous category-level instruction through active dialogue. However, existing dialogue-enabled methods often consume oracle answers as transient textual context for immediate decisions, rather than persistent spatial or object-centric structured state. We present SAIN, a zero-shot framework that turns active dialogue into persistent navigation state. Instead of consuming oracle answers as one-step text hints, SAIN compiles them into target evidence, route-level corridor memory, and object-candidate labels. These states are stored in structured value, room, graph, and object memories, then consumed by a unified policy for frontier ranking and final target approach. On the VL-LN IIGN benchmark, SAIN improves SR from 20.2 to 25.4 and SPL from 13.07 to 14.17 over the strongest reported dialogue-enabled baseline, while requiring no task-specific policy training. The results support dialogue-to-state conversion as an effective zero-shot mechanism for long-horizon interactive instance navigation. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [25] [High Fidelity Capture, Reconstruction, and Transfer of Human Demonstrations for Robot-Assisted Bathing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09127)
*Arjun S. Lakshmipathy,Jonathan P. King,Ethan Zuo,Rohit Satishkumar,Hongyi Chen,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Dan Ding,Zackory Erickson,Nancy S. Pollard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以接触区域为核心处理单元的人体沐浴示范采集、理解与迁移框架，并据此构建高质量多模态数据集，控制装配于机械臂的灵巧软手在人体模型上完成沐浴任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向沐浴等高价值临床任务，机器人需要进行持续、复杂且高度接触密集的人机交互，但现有系统在安全性、可靠性和示范数据获取方面仍不足。动态人体接触行为难以通过运动与触觉传感器有效采集、解析和迁移，因此需要一种能够保留接触结构与交互细节的方法。

Method: 研究以接触区域作为数据处理的关键原语，采集受训临床人员对人体受试者实施沐浴的示范数据，形成同步记录运动、形状、接触和力信息的数据集。随后将数据用于机械臂末端灵巧软手的任务设计与控制，并分别采用开环和闭环策略，将人体示范迁移至人体模型上的沐浴操作。

Result: 构建了首个面向持续、接触密集人际交互、同时包含高质量同步运动、形状、接触和力信息的沐浴示范数据集。实验表明，所提出的迁移策略能够在机器人系统的多个层级有效利用这些数据，使软手在人体模型上完成沐浴任务。相关数据、方法和材料计划公开发布。

Conclusion: 该框架为复杂接触型人体示范的结构化采集、表示和机器人迁移提供了简洁而有效的方案，验证了接触区域在物理人机交互中的实用价值，并为安全可靠的临床服务机器人研究提供了数据与技术基础。

Abstract: Despite the demand for robots in high-value clinical tasks like bathing, contemporary systems still lack the safety and reliability required for complex, sustained physical interaction with humans. A key challenge hindering the development of such systems is that collecting, understanding, and effectively transferring highly dynamic, contact-rich human bathing demonstrations is difficult, even with modern motion and tactile sensing equipment. We present a straightforward, but effective framework for doing so with high fidelity by utilizing contact regions as a key processing primitive. We use our framework to build a dataset of bathing demonstrations performed by trained clinicians on human subjects. We then use this dataset to design and control an arm-mounted dexterous soft hand to perform bathing tasks on a mannequin using open- and closed-loop strategies. Our dataset is the first to provide high quality synchronized motion, shape, contact, and force during sustained, contact-rich human-human interaction, and our transfer strategies demonstrate effective use of these data across multiple levels of the robotics stack. All relevant materials will be publicly released to enable further advancements in physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) research.

</details>


### [26] [Trajectory Divergence Horizon Decision for Reliable Dual-Arm Surgical Subtask Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09125)
*Mingwu Su,Guankun Wang,Jinsong Lin,Rulin Zhou,Ziyi Hao,Zhiwei Fang,Huxin Gao,Jiewen Lai,Jiazheng Wang,Fan Zhang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TDHD，一种面向手术机器人视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的测试时自适应执行时域决策方法，通过检测轨迹发散并及时触发重规划，提高复杂操作中的执行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型控制器通常针对单一任务训练，复用性有限；VLA模型虽能统一视觉、语言与动作，但多采用固定长度的开环动作序列，面对场景变化时容易产生误差累积，尤其在手术操作的后期阶段带来执行风险。因此，需要根据当前动作可靠性动态决定执行长度。

Method: 将VLA部署建模为自适应执行时域决策问题。TDHD在测试阶段对输入施加小幅噪声，利用流匹配模型生成两条动作轨迹，并通过逐步轨迹发散度估计动作可靠性；随后采用双阈值规则决定截断当前动作序列并触发重规划。研究还构建了真实双臂达芬奇类机器人基准，配置同步多视角感知与语言指令，收集600条遥操作示范，覆盖针操作中的到达、抓取和重新抓取，以及组织操作中的到达、提拉和切除任务。

Result: 在真实硬件实验中，每种任务设置进行20次试验。与最新VLA基线相比，TDHD将针操作成功率由55%提升至60%，将组织操作成功率由55%提升至80%。性能提升主要集中在最终操作阶段，表明自适应执行和及时重规划能够缓解开环动作的误差累积。

Conclusion: TDHD为VLA驱动的手术机器人提供了一种无需重新训练的可靠性增强机制。实验结果支持根据轨迹一致性动态调整执行时域，尤其适用于状态变化明显、末端操作精度要求较高的任务。其进一步价值取决于阈值泛化、计算开销、任务规模扩展及更大样本和更复杂临床场景下的验证。

Abstract: Surgical robotic systems are increasingly being adopted as clinical workload rises, motivating autonomous solutions for repetitive manipulation subtasks. Learning-based controllers improve generalization compared with rule-based and analytic approaches, but most are trained for individual tasks and remain difficult to reuse across procedures. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a unified framework that integrates visual perception, language grounding, and action generation, offering a promising path toward more composable surgical autonomy. However, existing VLA policies rely on fixed-length open-loop action sequences, where changing scene conditions can lead to accumulated errors and potential risks in surgical manipulation. To mitigate this issue, we formulate surgical VLA deployment as an adaptive execution-horizon decision problem and propose Trajectory Divergence Horizon Decision (TDHD), a test-time mechanism that estimates step-wise action reliability by measuring the divergence between two flow-matching-generated trajectories under small noise perturbations and truncates execution using a dual-threshold rule to trigger timely replanning. We further establish a real-world da Vinci-like dual-arm benchmark with synchronized multi-view perception and language instructions, and collect 600 teleoperated demonstrations across needle (reach, pick, regrasp) and tissue (reach, lift, resection) manipulation suites. On real hardware with 20 trials per task setting, TDHD consistently improves performance over the latest VLA baselines: success increases from 55\% to 60\% for needle manipulation and from 55\% to 80\% for tissue manipulation, with the largest gains observed in the final manipulation stages. These results highlight the importance of adaptive execution control for reliable deployment of VLA models in surgical robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [27] [ROEVO: Robust Organized Edge Feature-based Visual Odometry Using RGB-D Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09112)
*Mingrui Liu,Xingxing Zuo,Renlang Huang,Minglei Zhao,Jiming Chen,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅使用“组织化边缘”特征的视觉里程计系统，将离散边缘像素聚类并序列化为连续结构，以增强纹理与几何信息的利用，并实现跨帧边缘级关联。系统结合边缘级跟踪、形状保持边缘拟合和组织化边缘Bundle Adjustment，在室内环境中实现了高效跟踪与精确局部建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于边缘的视觉里程计通常以像素为单位处理边缘，难以充分保留边缘的连续结构、纹理信息和几何形状，也不利于跨帧关联与联合优化。因此，本文旨在构建一种能够利用边缘结构信息、提高配准鲁棒性和位姿估计精度的视觉里程计方法。

Method: 首先提出组织化边缘表示，将不连续的边缘像素转换为有序边缘簇，并据此建立跨多帧的边缘级关联和共视图。跟踪阶段采用边缘级残差替代像素级残差，以进行帧间配准。联合优化阶段提出形状保持的边缘拟合方法，以及组织化边缘Bundle Adjustment，将传统BA分解为边缘拟合与边缘配准两个部分，从而保持边缘结构完整性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该系统在室内环境中具有较高的跟踪效率、位姿估计精度和鲁棒性，相较现有先进方法取得更优或相当的性能。研究同时提供了公开源代码，增强了方法的可复现性。

Conclusion: 组织化边缘能够有效弥补传统边缘特征表示在结构保持和跨帧关联方面的不足。结合边缘级残差、形状保持拟合和专用BA优化后，纯边缘视觉里程计可以实现可靠的跟踪与精确的局部建图，验证了结构化边缘特征在VO中的应用潜力。

Abstract: This work presents a visual odometry (VO) system that leverages image edge features. Edges are spatially expressive cues commonly present across diverse environments, offering rich textural and structural information. However, existing edge-based VO methods often fail to fully exploit this potential. To this end, we introduce a novel feature representation termed \textit{organized edges}, which transforms disjoint edge pixels into sequentialized clusters, enabling more effective retention and utilization of the underlying textural and structural information. Another nice property of this formulation is that organized edges can perform edge-level association across multiple frames, enabling the establishment of a co-visibility graph. To achieve precise and efficient pose estimation, we propose a range of particularly designed tracking and joint optimization methods based on the characteristics of organized edges. For tracking, we formulate edge-wise rather than pixel-wise residuals to achieve robust and accurate inter-frame registration. For joint optimization, we introduce a novel shape-preserving edge-fitting method and an organized edge-based Bundle Adjustment (BA) approach, which decomposes the traditional BA problem into fitting and registration to preserve the structural integrity. Based on these novel techniques, we develop a complete VO system that exclusively employs organized edge features, achieving efficient tracking and precise local mapping. Extensive experiments demonstrate its accuracy and robustness in indoor environments, outperforming or achieving comparable performance to state-of-the-art methods. The source code is publicly available at this https URL

</details>


### [28] [UnsDrive: Towards Robust End-to-End Autonomous Driving in Unstructured Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09098)
*Nanxin Zeng,Ruiqi Song,Xiangyu Guo,Baiyong Ding,Yunfeng Ai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出UnsDrive，一种面向非结构化矿区场景的端到端自动驾驶规划器，通过显式建模占据、可通行与未知空间，提升部分可观测环境下的规划安全性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端规划方法主要针对结构化城市道路，在矿区中容易受到道路结构弱、地形遮挡、能见度下降和大范围未观测区域的影响，导致泛化能力与行驶安全性不足。

Method: UnsDrive利用多帧可见性线索构建未知感知占据表示，并以流匹配规划器为基础生成多模态未来轨迹；同时引入占据轨迹一致性损失和不确定性感知轨迹评分器，抑制轨迹进入不可通行或未知区域。研究还构建了MineLoop闭环仿真器，用于评估不规则道路、低能见度、重型车辆交互及矿区运行约束下的自动驾驶性能。

Result: 在开放环和闭环实验中，UnsDrive均优于具有竞争力的基线方法，在轨迹精度、碰撞规避能力和长时域驾驶鲁棒性方面表现更佳。摘要未提供具体数值或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 显式推理未知空间能够有效增强非结构化矿区环境中的端到端规划安全性与泛化能力。UnsDrive及MineLoop为面向复杂工业场景的自动驾驶规划与评测提供了具有应用价值的方案。

Abstract: End-to-end planning has shown strong promise for autonomous driving, but most existing methods are designed for structured urban roads and generalize poorly to unstructured mining environments. In such settings, weak road structure, terrain-induced occlusions, degraded visibility, and large unobserved regions make safe planning particularly challenging. To address these challenges, we propose UnsDrive, an end-to-end planner designed for unstructured mining scenes. UnsDrive builds an unknown-aware occupancy representation that explicitly models occupied, free, and unknown space using multi-frame visibility cues, and conditions a flow-matching planner on this representation to generate multimodal future trajectories. To improve safety under partial observability, we further introduce an occupancy trajectory consistency loss and an uncertainty-aware trajectory scorer that penalize trajectories entering non-traversable or unobserved regions. We also present MineLoop, a mining-oriented closed-loop simulator for evaluating autonomous driving under irregular road geometry, degraded visibility, heavy-vehicle interactions, and mining-specific operational constraints. Experiments in both open-loop and closed-loop settings show that UnsDrive consistently outperforms strong baselines in trajectory accuracy, collision avoidance, and long-horizon driving robustness. These results demonstrate the value of explicit unknown-space reasoning for autonomous driving in unstructured mining environments.

</details>


### [29] [Latent World Models with Monotone Planning Costs for Image-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09073)
*Amirhosein Chahe,Siwei Cai,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向图像目标导航的潜在世界模型，通过改进多步预测与规划代价排序提升采样式规划性能，并在GNM数据集和实体机器人上取得较强结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型即使能够准确预测未来表征，也可能无法可靠地对候选动作序列进行代价排序，从而误导交叉熵方法（CEM）等采样式规划器。本文旨在缩小训练与多步规划之间的分布差异，并直接提升规划代价的排序可靠性。

Method: 模型采用冻结的DINO系列编码器构建潜在世界模型，并结合两类训练目标：一是自回归滚动损失，用于降低训练轨迹与多步规划滚动轨迹之间的差距；二是单调代价排序（MCR）损失，约束扰动程度更高的动作序列获得更高的规划代价。此外，论文分析了基于InfoNCE的动作对比学习，发现时间置换负样本会扭曲潜在空间几何结构并损害规划性能。规划代价定义为预测未来表征与目标表征之间的余弦距离。

Result: 在GNM导航数据集上，该方法优于NWM、DINO-WM、OmniVLA和NoMaD，达到图像目标导航领域的先进性能。与采用相同编码器的DINO-WM基线相比，方向误差降低了2.7倍。实体机器人零样本实验表明，该模型能够在未见过的室内和室外环境中沿目标导向路径行进。

Conclusion: 论文表明，潜在世界模型的规划效果不仅取决于未来状态预测精度，还高度依赖规划代价的排序质量。自回归滚动训练与MCR损失能够有效改善代价校准和导航性能，而不恰当的时间置换负样本可能破坏潜在表征结构。该方法兼具数据集性能与零样本实体部署能力，但摘要未说明其计算开销、对环境变化的鲁棒性及长期导航失败案例。

Abstract: Image-goal navigation with latent world models requires not only accurate future prediction, but also a planning cost that reliably ranks candidate action sequences. We define the cost as the cosine distance between the predicted future embedding and the goal embedding, and show that poor cost ordering can mislead sampling-based planners such as Cross-Entropy Method (CEM). To address this, we propose a latent world model built on a frozen DINO-family encoder and train it with two complementary objectives. An autoregressive rollout loss reduces the gap between training and multi-step planning rollouts, while a Monotone Cost Ranking (MCR) loss directly encourages increasingly perturbed action sequences to receive higher planning costs. We also study InfoNCE-based action-contrastive training and find that temporal permutation negatives distort the latent geometry and degrade planning performance. On the GNM navigation dataset, our method outperforms Navigation World Models (NWM), DINO-WM, OmniVLA, and NoMaD, achieving state-of-the-art image-goal navigation performance while reducing orientation error by $2.7\times$ over the same-encoder DINO WM baseline. We also deploy the model zero-shot on a physical robot, where it follows goal-directed paths in unseen indoor and outdoor environments.

</details>


### [30] [Personalized Lower-limb Exoskeleton Assistance via Preference-based Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09015)
*Xiao-Yin Liu,Guotao Li,Weiqun Wang,Zeng-Guang Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于偏好贝叶斯优化的个性化外骨骼辅助控制方法，在较少人机交互下快速学习用户偏好的控制参数，并实现不同任务下的实时助力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于用户偏好的外骨骼参数优化方法依赖大量在线人机交互，优化速度较慢，容易导致用户疲劳并降低优化效果。因此，需要一种具有更高样本效率、能够以最少交互完成个性化参数寻优的方法。

Method: 提出偏好贝叶斯优化（PbBO）框架，通过利用候选参数集采样分布的先验知识提升样本效率。该方法针对六个控制参数进行优化，并结合分层控制器，为不同运动任务生成个性化关节力矩，同时实现实时力矩跟踪。

Result: PbBO经过20次迭代即可收敛到用户偏好的参数，验证准确率达到90.7%。跑台和户外实验表明，相较于无辅助行走，优化后的辅助参数可使代谢率降低14.5%至15.4%、心率降低6.3%至7.6%、肌肉激活水平降低6.7%至31.5%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够显著减少个性化外骨骼控制所需的人机交互次数，并在保证实时控制的同时有效降低行走生理负担，证明了偏好贝叶斯优化在高效、舒适的个性化辅助参数学习中的应用潜力。

Abstract: A significant challenge in exoskeleton robotics is the need to dynamically adapt control profiles to individual motion preferences, thereby ensuring both efficient and comfortable assistance. Currently, since user experience can serve as a comprehensive metric for evaluating the effectiveness of assistance, user preference-based optimization methods have been widely studied for parameter tuning. However, the existing methods rely heavily on extensive human-robot online interactions and suffer from slow optimization speed, which not only induces user fatigue but also compromises optimization effectiveness. Therefore, this paper aims to explore an efficient preference-based optimization framework for personalized exoskeleton assistance that can learn optimal parameters with minimal interaction. We propose a preference-based Bayesian optimization (PbBO) approach that can improve sample efficiency by leveraging knowledge about the sampling distribution of candidate sets. For optimizing six control parameters, PbBO can converge to user-preferred parameters with 90.7% validation accuracy via 20 iterations. Moreover, the hierarchical controller is designed to generate personalized torque for different tasks and achieve interaction torque tracking in real time. The results of treadmill and outdoor experiments demonstrate that the optimized parameters can reduce metabolic rate by 14.5%-15.4%, heart rate by 6.3%-7.6%, and muscle activation by 6.7%-31.5% compared to unassisted walking.

</details>


### [31] [SHRIMP: Iterative Refinement of Robot Task Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08884)
*Mya Schroder,Yuna Hwang,Callie Y. Kim,Leqian Cheng,Jeffrey Li-cheng Liu,Chenchen Zheng,Xinning He,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SHRIMP是一种基于自然语言的交互式机器人任务规划系统，支持层次化计划生成、显式纠错、仿真验证及实体机器人执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作机器人通常要求用户具备专业编程知识，而自然语言虽然降低了使用门槛，却存在语义歧义和生成过程不透明等问题，用户难以在执行前验证机器人计划。

Method: 系统利用大语言模型将自然语言指令转换为层次化机器人原语计划；用户可通过再次提示和显式纠错迭代修改计划，并在每次修订后通过仿真进行验证，确认满意后再部署到实体机器人。

Result: 在35名参与者完成桌面厨房任务的用户研究中，SHRIMP提升了用户对机器人行为的感知控制，并增强了机器人计划与行为的透明度。

Conclusion: SHRIMP为非专业用户提供了自然语言驱动且可验证的机器人编程流程，通过迭代修订和仿真预览缓解了语义歧义与模型不透明问题。

Abstract: As collaborative robots have entered domains such as manufacturing, agriculture, and healthcare, programming or adapting robot behavior typically requires robotic expertise that most end users lack. Natural language lowers this barrier. Recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) have made it feasible to translate natural language into robot task plans. However, language-based task specification suffers from semantic ambiguity, and generative models lack transparency for how language instructions become robot actions, making it difficult for users to validate the plan before execution. To address these issues, we introduce SHRIMP, a system that allows users to automatically generate a hierarchical robot primitive plan using natural language and iteratively revise their plan through re-prompting and explicit correction. At each revision, SHRIMP allows users to validate their plan in simulation, and once satisfied, execute it on the physical robot. Through a user study involving participants planning tabletop kitchen tasks (n=35), we validate that SHRIMP improves perceived control and enhances robot transparency. System videos and source code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [32] [Hierarchical Topology-Aware Planning and Control of Underwater Vehicle-Manipulator Systems in Confined Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08871)
*Mohamed Abdelwahab,Ruggero Carli,Damiano Varagnolo,Alberto Dalla Libera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MANTA，一种面向狭窄、拥挤且部分已知水下环境的自主水下航行器-机械臂系统（UVMS）分层规划与控制框架，将通道可通行性、机械臂操作可行性和闭环执行统一起来。实验表明，该方法在任务成功率、碰撞安全裕度、机械臂运动量及轨迹跟踪误差方面优于或改进了基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UVMS在洞穴、管道和复杂水下结构中作业时，受到机动性差、通道狭窄、环境信息不完整及执行不确定性的影响，容易进入无法恢复的区域。现有方法通常难以同时处理全局通行性、底座与机械臂协同避碰以及实际执行阶段的跟踪与恢复问题。

Method: MANTA采用三层层次化架构：第一层在保守的降维底座空间中进行全局连通性推理，提取通向任务区域的候选可通行走廊；第二层联合优化底座连续运动与机械臂轨迹，生成无碰撞的底座-机械臂协同轨迹；第三层通过基于高斯过程模型的强化学习方法MC-PILCO学习到达并保持目标操作状态的底座控制策略，实现轨迹跟踪与定点保持。执行过程中持续监测地图更新，并在当前通道失效时触发恢复和路径修复。

Result: 在120组匹配规划查询中，MANTA相比完整状态空间采样式基线取得了更高的任务成功率、更大的安全间隙和更小的机械臂运动量。基于MC-PILCO学习的控制策略在训练管状参考轨迹和未见过的管状参考轨迹上均降低了位置与偏航跟踪误差。实验覆盖了受限空间UVMS规划和闭环跟踪任务。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，MANTA能够有效整合通道可达性、操作可行性与闭环控制，提升UVMS在复杂水下环境中的安全自主干预能力。其分层结构和数据高效的模型学习机制适用于洞穴、管道及拥挤海底结构等场景，但摘要未提供更广泛环境、长期运行稳定性或真实水下部署规模的验证。

Abstract: This paper addresses autonomous intervention with an underwater vehicle--manipulator system (UVMS) in confined, cluttered, and partially known environments, where poor maneuverability, narrow passages, and uncertain execution may cause the robot to enter unrecoverable regions. We propose MANTA, a three-layer hierarchical planning-and-control framework that couples passage accessibility, manipulation feasibility, and closed-loop execution. The first layer performs global connectivity reasoning in a conservative reduced base space to extract traversable corridor candidates toward the task region. The second layer refines each candidate corridor by jointly optimizing the continuous base motion and arm trajectory, producing a collision-free base--arm trajectory. The third layer learns a reach-and-hold base policy using Gaussian-process model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) through MC-PILCO, enabling trajectory tracking and station keeping at the planned manipulation state. During execution, the framework monitors map updates and can trigger recovery and route repair when the active passage becomes infeasible. MANTA is evaluated in confined UVMS planning and closed-loop tracking experiments. Across 120 matched planning queries, it achieves higher task success than full-state sampling-based baselines while producing larger clearance margins and lower arm motion. The learned MC-PILCO policy further reduces position and yaw tracking errors on both training and unseen tube-like references. These results show MANTA as a structured and data-efficient framework for safe autonomous underwater intervention in caves, tubes, and cluttered subsea structures.

</details>


### [33] [PEEL: Parallel Extraction for Long-Horizon Disassembly Planning via Scale-Invariant Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08773)
*Servet B. Bayraktar,Andreas Orthey,Zachary Kingston,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PEEL算法，用于高效规划多部件物体的长时域拆卸过程，能够在狭窄逃逸通道和碰撞约束下计算可执行的拆卸动作序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域多部件拆卸要求机器人同时解决单个部件的无碰撞移除运动规划，以及部件拆卸顺序的确定。传统方法在狭窄空间、复杂几何约束和较长拆卸链条下效率不足，因此需要一种兼顾采样效率、规划鲁棒性和整体顺序优化的方法。

Method: PEEL首先在预热阶段估计物体尺度，并利用尺度不变的采样方案生成单部件拆卸运动。随后，将不同采样器集成到多臂老虎机快速探索随机树（MAB-RRT）规划器中，根据采样获得的奖励信号动态选择更有效的采样策略。在此基础上，PEEL并行运行多个规划器，构建表示部件拆卸顺序的有向图，并将规划结果提供给机械臂执行。

Result: MAB-RRT在76个装配体的单部件拆卸任务中达到100%的成功率，并表现出较强的参数鲁棒性。集成MAB-RRT后的PEEL在Fetch机械臂上完成了4个长时域拆卸任务，每个任务包含10至17个独立部件。

Conclusion: PEEL通过尺度感知采样、基于奖励的采样器选择和并行规划，有效提升了复杂长时域拆卸的规划能力。实验结果表明，该方法能够处理狭窄逃逸空间和多部件顺序约束，并具备在真实机器人平台上执行的可行性。

Abstract: Long-horizon multi-part object disassembly requires robots to compute feasible sequences of collision-free removal motions, even in the presence of tight, narrow escape corridors. To efficiently solve such disassembly problems, we propose Parallel Extraction for Long-Horizon Disassembly (PEEL), an algorithm which efficiently computes disassembly motions for object assemblies and feeds them to a robot manipulator for execution. PEEL uses sampling-based motion planning to compute single-object motions through the use of a scale-invariant sampling scheme, where the object scale is estimated in a burn-in phase and a subsequent directional sampler exploits the scale. This sampling scheme is integrated into a multi-arm bandit rapidly-exploring random tree (MAB-RRT) planner, which switches between different samplers depending on the reward signal received. Using MAB-RRT, the PEEL algorithm runs a batch of planners in parallel to obtain an ordered graph specifying the sequence in which object parts have to be removed. We show that MAB-RRT can efficiently solve single-part disassemblies with 100 percent success rate on 76 assemblies, and that it is robust to its parameters. By integrating MAB-RRT into PEEL, we solve four long-horizon disassembly problems using the Fetch manipulator robot involving 10 to 17 individual object parts.

</details>


### [34] [OnEvoMemory: Evolving Memory through Online Robot Rollouts for Pretrained Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08749)
*Zhongxi Chen,Shenqi Zong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OnEvoMemory，一种面向预训练机器人策略的价值引导记忆模块，通过保留近期上下文、高价值经验和显著状态转移，提升长时程机器人操作中的任务阶段跟踪与子任务管理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作任务要求策略记忆已完成的子任务和关键交互事件，但现有记忆机制通常依赖外部模型或人工预定义更新规则，难以根据任务结果自适应筛选有用经验，并可能重复执行已完成的子任务。

Method: OnEvoMemory结合三类记忆内容：近期上下文、高价值经验和显著状态转移。首先利用离线示范建立记忆先验，再根据成功与失败的在线轨迹结果学习经验保留策略，持续演化记忆选择，从而辅助预训练VLA策略识别任务阶段转换并调整行为。

Result: 在长时程机器人操作基准上的实验表明，OnEvoMemory能够提升基础VLA策略的性能；离线记忆初始化和在线记忆演化均对性能改进具有贡献。摘要未提供具体数据、基准名称或统计显著性。

Conclusion: OnEvoMemory通过结果驱动的记忆筛选，将离线示范先验与在线经验更新结合，为预训练机器人策略提供了一种无需完全依赖外部模型或固定规则的自适应长时程记忆机制。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation requires policies to track completed subtasks and critical interaction events. However, existing memory mechanisms heavily rely on external models or predefined update rules. To address this, we propose OnEvoMemory, a value-guided memory module for pretrained robot policies. It maintains recent context, high-value experiences, and salient transitions, while learning which experiences should be retained from trajectory outcomes. Offline demonstrations initialize the memory prior, whereas successful and unsuccessful online rollouts refine memory selection, helping the policy recognize task-stage transitions and avoid repeating completed subtasks. Experiments on long-horizon manipulation benchmarks show that OnEvoMemory improves the performance of the base VLA policy through both offline initialization and online memory evolution.

</details>


### [35] [WA-SpecDec: World-Aware Speculative Decoding for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08725)
*Zikang Wen,Yuning Zhang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WA-SpecDec是一种面向视觉-语言-动作策略的世界感知推测解码框架，通过在预填充阶段引入世界模型的物理场景信息，提高宽松动作令牌接受机制下的安全性、任务成功率与解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略以自回归方式生成机器人控制指令，重复执行目标模型前向计算导致闭环延迟较高。现有推测解码虽能并行验证动作令牌，并允许一定令牌差异，但采用场景无关的固定容差，无法区分自由空间与接触邻近区域的风险，可能引发碰撞或抓取失败。

Method: WA-SpecDec在VLA预填充阶段注入世界模型推导的物理场景信息，生成供草稿动作提议与目标模型验证共享的世界感知预填充状态，同时保留现有的宽松接受规则，不修改令牌接受判定机制。

Result: 在三种先进的宽松接受方案上，WA-SpecDec在更宽松的接受条件下保持更高任务成功率，并支持更长的接受前缀。在相近任务成功率的运行点，相比单独使用VLA推测解码，获得1.5倍的匹配成功率加速；相对于对应推测解码基线，近接触失败率平均降低18.6%。

Conclusion: 将世界模型的物理场景感知融入推测解码预填充状态，可以在不改变宽松接受规则的前提下提升VLA策略的效率与可靠性，尤其能够降低接触邻近场景中的控制失败风险。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies generate robot controls autoregressively, making closed-loop latency dominated by repeated target-model forward passes. Speculative decoding reduces this cost by verifying blocks of draft action tokens in parallel, and recent VLA methods further relax token-level acceptance because small differences in action-token space often map to similar continuous controls. However, this relaxation remains scene-agnostic. A fixed token-distance tolerance treats the same action-token deviation as equally safe across states, although deviations that are harmless in free space can cause collisions or grasp failures near contact. We propose WA-SpecDec, a world-aware speculative decoding framework that injects world-model-derived physical scene awareness during the VLA prefill stage, producing shared world-aware prefill states for draft proposal and target verification without changing the relaxed acceptance rule. Across three state-of-the-art relaxed acceptance schemes, WA-SpecDec preserves higher task success under looser relaxation and enables longer accepted prefixes. At comparable-success operating points, WA-SpecDec achieves a 1.5x matched-success speedup over VLA speculative decoding alone and reduces near-contact failure (NCF) by 18.6% on average relative to the corresponding speculative baselines.

</details>


### [36] [Anchor-Based AI Approach for Pre-Crash Object Detection Utilizing Micro-Doppler Signatures in Automotive Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08701)
*Patrick Zaumseil,Rainer Engert,Dagmar Steinhauser,Jonathan Wache,Soumya Dewangan,Thomas Brandmeier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合高分辨率汽车雷达微多普勒特征与锚点式人工智能模型的预碰撞目标检测方法，并通过雷达图像膨胀增强稀疏点云中的局部模式，从而提升近场目标检测和运动参数估计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶安全性依赖于约束系统的及时可靠触发，而预碰撞场景中的目标检测和运动状态估计容易受到雷达点云稀疏、波动、多径反射及虚假目标的影响。现有跟踪方法在动态场景和近场区域可能存在漏检，因此需要利用高分辨率雷达及微多普勒信息提高检测可靠性。

Method: 构建面向高分辨率雷达数据的锚点式AI模型，显式提取和利用微多普勒特征进行预碰撞目标检测及运动参数估计。在特征输入通道上引入雷达图像膨胀技术，以增强局部雷达模式。研究还采集了采用量产传感器的雷达数据集，覆盖与预碰撞相关的多种场景，并将所提模型与先进汽车雷达跟踪方法进行对比。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提锚点式AI模型整体优于既有雷达跟踪方法，尤其在动态场景下能够更准确地估计目标运动参数，并降低关键近场区域的漏检率。该模型对不同数据集具有较好的处理能力，同时在存在多径反射和虚假目标时保持较高的检测可靠性。

Conclusion: 微多普勒特征能够有效补充传统雷达目标信息，雷达图像膨胀则有助于增强稀疏点云中的局部结构。二者结合的锚点式AI方法适用于预碰撞目标检测和参数估计，有望为智能约束系统的提前、可靠触发提供更稳健的感知基础。

Abstract: Advanced automated driving presents significant potential to improve modern automotive safety systems, but it depends highly on the reliable activation of restraint systems. Forward-looking sensors are crucial for immediate and precise object detection. Recent developments in automotive radar technology enable detailed environment detection and the recognition of high-resolution features, such as micro-Doppler signatures. Combined with advanced AI techniques, these features significantly enhance object detection and improve the accuracy of kinematic parameter estimation. This is essential for the early and reliable activation of irreversible safety systems, such as smart airbags and adaptive seat belts. Therefore, an anchor-based AI model is presented, designed to process high-resolution radar data with an explicit focus on micro-Doppler signatures to improve pre-crash object detection. Furthermore, these signatures can improve the accuracy of kinematic object parameter estimation and reduce false negatives, especially in the critical near-field. To address the challenges of sparse and fluctuating radar point clouds, an innovative radar-image dilation technique on the feature input channels was developed to amplify local radar patterns, like micro-Doppler features. Therefore, this approach increases the system's reliability and increases its ability to detect objects in pre-crash scenarios despite radar multipath reflections and ghost objects. In order to investigate the applicability and compare the model's performance with advanced automotive radar tracking methods, a radar data set using series sensors and pre-crash relevant scenarios was recorded. The results demonstrate the advantages of the anchor-based AI model over established tracking approaches. It excels at estimating object parameters in dynamic scenarios and underscores its ability to process different data sets effectively.

</details>


### [37] [Knowledge-Distilled End-to-End Reinforcement Learning for Smooth 6-DOF Thrust Control and Rapid Adaptation to Ocean Currents in Remotely Operated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08598)
*Tiankuang Wen,Huiping Li,Gang Liu,Yong Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TSRCA-PPO端到端强化学习控制方法，通过两阶段蒸馏学习、改进奖励函数和特权多编码器架构，实现受海流扰动下的高精度、快速、节能且平滑的遥控水下航行器控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端强化学习控制方法难以在海流扰动下同时兼顾低稳态跟踪误差、快速瞬态响应、低能耗和控制力平滑性，缺乏统一的综合控制框架。

Method: 提出推力平滑快速海流适应近端策略优化方法（TSRCA-PPO）。该方法采用两阶段蒸馏学习框架获取近似最优策略，并以专门设计的奖励函数协调多项控制目标，同时引入特权多编码器架构增强对扰动和状态信息的建模能力。通过消融实验分析各模块的有效性，并与传统级联P-PID控制器进行仿真对比。

Result: 仿真结果表明，TSRCA-PPO在所有评价指标上均优于P-PID控制器。其稳态位置误差、稳态姿态误差、调节时间、能量指标和推力平滑性指标分别降至P-PID对应值的42.7%、76.5%、10.6%、93.5%和15.9%，表明跟踪精度、响应速度、能耗和输出平滑性均得到改善。

Conclusion: TSRCA-PPO能够在海流扰动环境下实现多目标协调控制，相较P-PID具有更低误差、更快响应、更高能效和更平滑的推力输出。奖励函数设计与特权多编码器架构是性能提升的关键组成部分。

Abstract: With the continuous improvement of computational capabilities, end-to-end reinforcement learning has been rapidly developed for remotely operated vehicles control. Nevertheless, existing end-to-end reinforcement-learningbased methods still face challenges in achieving optimal control under oceancurrent disturbances. In particular, there remains a lack of a unified control framework that can simultaneously achieve low steady-state tracking error, rapid transient response, energy-efficient operation, and smooth controlforce outputs under disturbances. To address the issue, this paper proposes the thrust smoothness rapid current adaptation proximal policy optimization (TSRCA-PPO) method which learns a near-optimal strategy by a twostage distillation learning framework. The core innovations of this work lie in the reward-function design and the privileged multi-encoder architecture. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of each module. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed TSRCA-PPO method consistently outperforms the conventional cascaded P-PID controller across all evaluation metrics. Specifically, TSRCA-PPO reduces the steady-state position error, steady-state attitude error, settling time, energy index, and thrustsmoothness index to 42.7%, 76.5%, 10.6%, 93.5%, and 15.9% of the corresponding P-PID values, respectively.

</details>


### [38] [Vid2WAM: Distilling Video Diffusion Priors into World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08558)
*Chenhao Qiu,Ruixiang Wang,Runyi Zhao,Sixu Lin,Songen Gu,Shufeng Nan,Guiliang Liu,Kui Jia,Yanwei Fu,Simo Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Vid2WAM是一种离线蒸馏框架，将大规模视频基础模型的视觉扩散先验迁移到紧凑的世界动作模型（WAM）中，以减少对目标任务专家示范的依赖，并提升机器人策略的泛化性、数据效率和部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM同时学习未来视觉动态与动作，但高度依赖昂贵的目标任务专家轨迹，导致扩展性和新任务泛化能力受限。该工作探讨未来监督是否必须来自目标任务专家示范，并尝试利用通用视频模型提供可迁移的视觉预测知识。

Method: Vid2WAM采用离线知识蒸馏。首先，利用视频基础模型生成与任务和语言指令条件相关的未来视觉轨迹，直接监督WAM的未来预测分支；其次，通过逆动力学模型从视觉轨迹中恢复与具体机器人本体相关的伪动作，用于动作学习。为融合合成监督与真实监督，方法引入源感知残差动作适配：在共享动作骨干上学习不同数据源的特定修正，以减轻噪声伪动作造成的干扰。推理阶段移除视频教师模型和逆动力学模型，仅保留WAM学生模型。

Result: 仿真和真实机器人实验表明，在专家示范有限的条件下，Vid2WAM能够提升新任务泛化能力和数据效率，同时保持较低的推理延迟。摘要未提供具体数据集、基线或定量提升幅度。

Conclusion: 该工作证明，WAM的未来视觉监督不必完全依赖目标任务专家轨迹；借助视频基础模型和逆动力学生成的跨来源监督，可以有效训练紧凑且高效部署的WAM。源感知残差适配是缓解伪动作噪声和监督冲突的关键机制。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot policy learning by jointly modeling future visual dynamics and actions. However, their scalability and generalization remain constrained by their reliance on costly expert demonstrations. We challenge this by asking whether future supervision for WAMs must originate from target-task expert trajectories. In this paper, we propose Vid2WAM, an offline distillation framework that transfers visual diffusion priors from a large video foundation model into a compact WAM student. Given an observation and language instruction, Vid2WAM distills supervision through two complementary channels: task-conditioned future rollouts directly supervise the student's future prediction branch, while an inverse dynamics model recovers embodiment-specific pseudo-actions for action learning. To robustly integrate synthetic and real supervision, we introduce source-aware residual action adaptation that learns source-specific corrections around a shared action backbone and mitigates interference from noisy pseudo-actions. During inference, both the video teacher and inverse dynamics model are discarded, leaving only the WAM student for efficient deployment. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that Vid2WAM improves novel-task generalization and data efficiency under limited expert demonstrations while preserving low-latency inference.

</details>


### [39] [EsaacSim: A Multimodal Event Camera Add-on for NVIDIA Isaac Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08522)
*Ignacio Bugueno-Cordova,Malte Kuhlmann,Nicolás Navarro-Guerrero,Miguel Campusano,Rodrigo Verschae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EsaacSim 是面向 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 的多模态事件相机仿真扩展，可在线生成灰度与 Bayer RGGB 事件流，并同步输出 RGB、APS、事件、深度和 IMU 数据。实验表明，该系统在多种分辨率下具备较好的实时性能和较低的 GPU 额外内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件视觉适用于机器人高速感知，但其应用受到真实传感器获取困难以及缺乏与现代机器人平台集成的仿真工具限制。该研究旨在提供一种可配置、可在线运行且支持多模态同步输出的事件相机仿真方案。

Method: 论文将事件相机功能集成为 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 的附加模块，支持多种分辨率、灰度事件和 Bayer RGGB 事件生成，并通过原生 ROS2 接口同步发布 RGB、APS、事件、深度和 IMU 数据。此外，采用运动引导的帧间隔合成策略提高有效时间分辨率，同时保持与 Isaac Sim 渲染流程的兼容性。

Result: 系统在五种事件相机分辨率下实现了 240–960 Hz 的有效事件频率。灰度事件生成耗时 6.98–27.28 ms，Bayer RGGB 事件生成耗时 7.58–29.16 ms；在 NVIDIA RTX 4060 GPU 上，额外 GPU 内存占用低于 400 MB。实验展示了代表性机器人场景中的多模态同步仿真和在线运行能力。

Conclusion: EsaacSim 为机器人研究和合成数据生成提供了较完整的在线多模态事件相机仿真工具，具有较好的分辨率扩展性、时间分辨率和计算效率。不过，论文目前主要报告早期版本的架构与性能，后续仍需进一步验证其在更复杂场景、不同硬件平台及更大规模数据生成任务中的稳定性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Event-based vision is becoming an increasingly important sensing paradigm for robotics, yet its adoption remains limited by sensor availability and the lack of integrated simulation tools for modern robotics platforms. This paper presents EsaacSim, a multimodal event camera add-on for NVIDIA Isaac Sim that enables online simulation of configurable event cameras with grayscale and Bayer RGGB event generation. The framework supports multiple event camera resolutions and provides synchronized RGB, APS, event, depth, and IMU outputs through native ROS2 interfaces. A motion-guided frame-gap synthesis strategy further increases the effective temporal resolution while preserving compatibility with the Isaac Sim rendering pipeline. Experimental evaluation demonstrates synchronized multimodal simulation across representative robotic scenes and efficient online performance over five event camera resolutions at effective event rates from 240 to 960Hz. Event stream generation requires 6.98--27.28ms for grayscale events and 7.58--29.16ms for Bayer RGGB events while using less than 400MB of additional GPU memory on an NVIDIA RTX~4060 GPU. These results show that EsaacSim enables supports online multimodal event-camera simulation for robotics research and synthetic data generation. We release an early version of the simulator and report its current architecture and performance.

</details>


### [40] [Stochastic Physics-Informed Neural Networks on Lie Groups for Learning Underwater Vehicle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08356)
*Evan F. Palmer,Ross L. Hatton,Geoffrey A. Hollinger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种遵循物理与几何约束的随机物理信息神经网络，用于学习水下航行器的随机动力学，并在仿真和港口实测场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下航行器运动受复杂流体效应和不确定性影响，传统基于物理的方法难以准确刻画其动力学。为支持自主巡检、科学采样及安全的基于模型控制，需要更准确且具有鲁棒性的动力学模型。

Method: 方法基于Euler–Poincaré动力学和李群几何，构建随机物理信息神经网络架构，以满足水下航行器的物理和几何约束。模型结合保持结构的随机积分方法，并利用矩匹配和有限维匹配机制，保证训练过程中的几何一致性。

Result: 在仿真环境以及港口中绕码头桩航行的水下航行器场景中进行评估。结果表明，该方法能够学习准确、鲁棒的随机动力学模型，并适用于复杂海洋环境下的安全基于模型控制。

Conclusion: 将随机建模、物理信息学习与李群几何结构相结合，可以有效提升水下航行器动力学建模的准确性、鲁棒性和物理一致性，为自主水下任务和安全控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate models of underwater vehicle motion are needed for autonomous execution of marine tasks like infrastructure inspection and scientific sampling. However, such motion is challenging to characterize using traditional physics-based methods. This paper presents a novel data-driven framework for learning stochastic underwater vehicle dynamics. Using Euler-Poincaré dynamics and the geometry of Lie groups, we develop a stochastic physics-informed neural network architecture that respects the physical and geometric constraints of underwater vehicles. Our approach leverages structure-preserving stochastic integration and builds upon moment matching and finite dimensional matching to ensure geometrically-consistent training. We evaluate our approach in simulation and on an underwater vehicle navigating dock pylons in a harbor environment. The results demonstrate that our method learns accurate and robust dynamics models, enabling safe model-based control in challenging marine environments.

</details>


### [41] [MPPI Planning with Gaussian Based Human Cost Function for Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08323)
*Chinmay Mundane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出预测高斯交互场（PGIF），将行人未来轨迹建模为沿运动方向扩展的各向异性高斯排斥场，并与MPPI结合，以提升拥挤环境中的机器人导航安全性。实验显示，PGIF-MPPI在所有测试密度下均达到0%碰撞率，同时保持实时规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MPPI常将行人简化为当前位置的静态点障碍物，无法充分反映行人未来运动，因而低估动态场景中的潜在风险，尤其是机器人进入行人前进路径时的风险。

Method: 提出PGIF时空代价模型：将行人预测结果传播至完整规划时域，并构造沿行人运动方向排列的各向异性高斯排斥场。高斯场的前向扩展程度随行人速度增加，形成运动锥危险区域；机器人进入行人前方路径时受到更强惩罚。该方法具有闭式表达，并可在不同采样轨迹之间完全并行计算。

Result: 在三种人群密度、共300个随机场景中，PGIF-MPPI在每种密度下均取得0%碰撞率，而原始MPPI的碰撞率最高达到82%。同时，PGIF未引入可测量的额外计算开销，并维持实时规划能力。

Conclusion: PGIF有效弥补了静态障碍物建模对行人未来运动预测不足的问题，能够以较低计算代价显著提升MPPI在动态拥挤环境中的安全性。该结果支持其作为实时预测式机器人导航代价模型的可行方案，但仍需通过更多真实环境和更复杂行人行为验证泛化能力。

Abstract: Safe robot navigation in crowded spaces requires planning that accounts for where people will be, not only where they are now. Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is an effective sampling-based planner, but many implementations encode humans as static point obstacles at their current positions, underestimating risk in dynamic scenes. We propose Predictive Gaussian Interaction Fields (PGIF), a spatiotemporal cost formulation that propagates pedestrian predictions forward over the full planning horizon and encodes them as anisotropic Gaussian repulsive fields aligned with each pedestrian's direction of motion. The forward spread of each field grows with the pedestrian's speed, creating a motion cone danger zone that penalises robot trajectories entering the pedestrian's path of travel more strongly than those approaching from behind. The formulation is closed-form and fully parallelisable across rollouts, adding no measurable computational overhead. Evaluated over 300 randomised crowd scenarios at three density levels, PGIF-MPPI achieves a 0\% collision rate at every density level, compared with up to 82\% for vanilla MPPI, while maintaining real-time planning performance.

</details>


### [42] [Diminishing Returns of Intelligence: The Non-Linear Relationship Between LLM Scale and User Perception in Short-Duration Open-Ended Social Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08320)
*Amanda Rasille Røn Volf,Morten Roed Frederiksen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究考察不同参数规模的Qwen3-VL模型在短时开放式人机社交互动中的用户感知差异。结果显示，30B模型相较于4B和8B模型没有形成显著整体优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型被广泛用于具身社交智能体，但模型规模是否能在短暂的人机互动中显著提升用户体验尚不明确。研究旨在检验参数规模与感知智能性、自然性、愉悦度及幽默感之间的关系。

Method: 采用被试内实验设计，邀请19名参与者分别与由4B、8B和30B参数Qwen3-VL模型驱动的机器人脸部界面互动。互动结束后，参与者从感知智能性、自然性、愉悦度和幽默感等维度进行评价，并分析交互频率与模型评价之间的关系。

Result: 30B模型未获得相较小模型的显著整体偏好；在感知自然性和智能性方面，30B相较4B也没有显著优势。对于30B模型，参与者的AI使用频率与智能性排名存在显著关系，表明经验较丰富的用户可能更容易察觉模型能力差异。

Conclusion: 在短时、开放式社交人机交互中，单纯扩大模型参数规模可能带来有限收益。对用户体验而言，对话流畅性、响应及时性和社会行为恰当性可能与原始模型能力同等重要。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to drive embodied social agents, yet it remains unclear whether larger models improve user perception during brief human-robot encounters. This paper examines the effect of LLM parameter size on short-duration, open-ended social interactions with a robot interface. In a within-subjects study, 19 participants interacted with robot faces driven by Qwen3-VL models at 4B, 8B, and 30B parameters. Participants evaluated the interactions in terms of perceived intelligence, naturalness, enjoyment, and humor. Results showed no significant overall preference for the 30B model over the smaller variants, including no significant advantage over the 4B model in perceived naturalness or intelligence. A significant relationship between AI interaction frequency and intelligence rankings for the 30B model suggests that more experienced users may be more sensitive to differences in model capability. Overall, the findings indicate diminishing returns from model scaling in brief open-ended social HRI, where conversational flow, responsiveness, and socially appropriate behavior potentially matter as much as raw parameter count.

</details>


### [43] [Scalable High-Speed Lateral Control for Single-Body and Articulated Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08293)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的横向控制框架，用于单体式和铰接式自动车辆的高速鲁棒路径跟踪，并通过自适应前视、轨迹分类及灵活跟踪点选择提升稳定性与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速路径跟踪面临振荡、前视距离难以匹配路径几何以及不同车辆结构控制需求不一致等问题。传统控制器通常难以同时适用于单体车辆和铰接车辆，因此需要一种统一且可扩展的横向控制架构。

Method: 以基于回旋线的控制器为基础，引入三项改进：集成切向检查与Fréchet距离计算，以优化前视点并抑制振荡；实时分类轨迹片段，动态调整前视搜索范围；根据横向误差和前方路径几何特征，采用双重自适应且具速率约束的前视机制。此外，通过灵活选择跟踪点及曲率—转向角查找表，支持牵引车后轴、铰接点和挂车相关位置的控制，而无需改变核心控制结构。研究使用TruckSim和Simulink对单体及铰接车辆开展双移线和弯道路况仿真。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该框架在高速场景下能够实现稳定路径跟踪，降低横向振荡，并有效调节前视距离。不同车辆配置下均获得较小的横向误差和可接受的横向加速度，说明其对车辆结构和路径几何变化具有较好的适应性。

Conclusion: 研究验证了统一横向控制架构从单体自动车辆扩展至铰接式自动车辆的可行性。所提出的自适应前视、轨迹分类和灵活跟踪点机制能够提升高速路径跟踪的鲁棒性与通用性，但摘要中的结论主要基于高保真仿真，仍需通过实车或硬件在环试验进一步验证。

Abstract: This paper presents a scalable lateral control framework for robust path tracking of single-body and articulated autonomous vehicles at high speeds. A clothoid-based controller is extended with three key adaptations: 1) an integrated tangential check and Frechet distance method for optimized lookahead and oscillation mitigation; 2) real-time trajectory segment classification for dynamic adjustment of the lookahead search range; and 3) a dual-adaptive, rate-controlled lookahead mechanism responsive to cross-track error and upcoming path geometry. To support different vehicle configurations, the framework also incorporates flexible tracking point selection and curvature-to-steering lookup tables, enabling control of points such as the tractor rear axle, hitch point, or trailer-related locations without changing the core control architecture. The controller is evaluated using high-fidelity TruckSim and Simulink simulations for both standalone and articulated vehicles across dual lane changes and winding-road scenarios. Results demonstrate stable high-speed path tracking, reduced oscillations, effective lookahead adaptation, low cross-track errors, and acceptable lateral acceleration across different vehicle configurations. The results support the use of a unified lateral control architecture that can scale from single-body to articulated autonomous vehicles.

</details>


### [44] [Spatiotemporal Context-dependent Personalized Movement Compensation in Delayed Telemanipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08200)
*Sai Jiang,Zonghe Chua*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向个体的运动缩放方法，根据通信延迟、运动方向和目标距离定制缩放参数，以降低远程操作中的过冲并提升精度。实验结果表明，该方法相较于无辅助条件可使关键性能指标提高约20%–25%，且在较长延迟下效果更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信延迟会破坏远程机器人的视觉—运动协调，导致过冲、终点误差增大和操作精度下降。传统运动缩放通常采用统一增益，无法适应不同操作者及不同任务情境，因此需要一种能够结合个体和上下文因素的自适应方法。

Method: 研究招募20名参与者，在虚拟仿真器中完成具有延迟的到达任务。针对不同延迟、运动方向和目标距离组合，通过仿真计算使平均过冲最小化的个性化运动缩放增益。随后在仿真环境和远程操作机器人上进行评估，并使用过冲、终点误差、轨迹平滑度、运动经济性及综合误差—时间指标衡量性能。

Result: 运动缩放在整体上优于无辅助操作，在关键指标上最高带来约20%–25%的性能提升。收益在较长通信延迟下最为显著。个性化缩放进一步提升了中等延迟、短距离向内到达任务中的准确性。研究同时覆盖了多种延迟、距离和运动方向，显示方法具有较好的情境适应性。

Conclusion: 个性化、延迟—方向—距离相关的运动缩放能够有效缓解通信延迟造成的操作误差，并提升远程操作的精度与效率。该方法可作为更高级自适应辅助框架的基础，未来可进一步融合实时上下文信息，以增强远程操作过程中的安全性和精确性。

Abstract: Communication delay remains a central challenge in telerobotics, where it disrupts visuomotor coordination and reduces task precision. Motion scaling is an effective countermeasure to delay-induced overshoot, yet typical deployments rely on uniform gains that neglect individual and contextual variability. We propose a human-centered method that fits personalized delay-, direction-, and distance-specific scaling parameters for each participant. We conducted experiments with twenty participants who performed delayed reaching tasks in a virtual simulator. Scaling gains were computed to minimize mean overshoot in simulation in each combination of experimental conditions. Evaluation was done in simulation and on a telesurgical robot to evaluate assistance benefits. Performance was assessed across multiple delays, distances, and movement directions using overshoot, endpoint error, trajectory smoothness, economy of motion, and a composite error-time metric. Motion scaling consistently improved performance relative to unassisted trials, yielding up to 20-25% performance gains in key metrics. Effects were most pronounced at longer delays. Personalization demonstrated additional accuracy benefits for inward reaching at a short distance under moderate delay. The results highlight the potential of personalized scaling as a foundation for more adaptive frameworks that integrate contextual information to improve the safety and precision of teleoperated procedures.

</details>


### [45] [Multi-modal Interactive Control of Robotic Arm based on Offline Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08183)
*Hanxiao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型服务通常依赖商业API，存在持续使用成本高、开放性有限和部署稳定性不足等问题。作者因此探索将开源大语言模型本地化部署，用于构建成本更低、使用更稳定的具身智能控制系统。

Method: 系统以ChatGLM为核心语言模型，结合文本与图像等多模态输入，通过Socratic Models式交互机制生成或协调机器人控制决策，并在PyBullet仿真环境中实现机械臂的交互式操作。摘要未说明具体网络结构、模块连接方式、训练或提示策略、控制器设计及数据来源。

Result: 作者声称该方法在机器人手臂多模态交互控制中表现良好，并显示出处理复杂文本—图像融合、多步骤及长时域机器人操作任务的潜力。但摘要未提供定量实验结果、对比基线、任务成功率、泛化性能、推理延迟或成本分析，因此其有效性尚缺乏可验证的证据。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是探索基于本地开源大语言模型的具身机器人控制方案，并在PyBullet中进行初步实现。研究方向具有应用价值，但当前摘要中的结论主要属于概念验证和潜力性表述，仍需通过严格基准、消融实验、真实机器人测试及资源开销评估加以验证。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly revolutionized the modern society with numerous advanced interactions between humans and AI agents, whereas the usage of most large language models including ChatGPT are not friendly open-sourced and must require the users paying a lot for such AI services continuously. Therefore, deploying open-sourced large language models on local servers can be considered as an efficient approach to design and implement creative embodied AI algorithms with lower cost and more stable free usage. Inspired by this ordinary motivation, we originally propose and implement the "Socratic Models-ChatGLM", which is a well-performed algorithm for multi-modal interactive control of robotic arm based on offline large language models via the facile PyBullet platform, even presents extraordinary potential to address complicated text-image integrated multi-step long-horizon robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [46] [Event-Time Hybrid Optimal Control for Robotic Table Tennis Serves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08157)
*Thomas Gossard,Till Köpff,Andreas Ziegler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于事件时间最优控制的机器人乒乓球发球生成方法，可在满足动力学、空气动力学、碰撞及机器人关节约束的同时，实现高速、多旋转发球。与固定步长方法相比，求解速度提高约4.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人发球需要同时满足高球速、高旋转、机器人运动学与动力学限制，以及球在己方台面弹跳、越过球网并落入目标区域等离散事件约束。传统固定步长方法通常需要额外进行事件根定位，计算效率较低。

Method: 将发球建模为包含非线性飞行与碰撞动力学的混合系统。第一阶段采用事件时间最优控制，同时优化球拍撞击速度、姿态以及台面弹跳、越网和落地时间，并在相应阶段边界直接施加约束。第二阶段通过另一最优控制问题，将撞击速度和姿态转换为满足关节位置、速度和力矩限制的完整机器人运动。

Result: 数值实验和KUKA Agilus实验证明，该方法相较固定步长结合根定位的方法，中位求解时间降低约4.1倍，同时保持相近的落点精度、旋转精度和发球有效性。真实机器人实现了可控落点以及上旋、下旋和侧旋发球，平均落点误差为13.1±7.3厘米，最高旋转速度达到30转每秒。

Conclusion: 事件时间最优控制能够高效处理发球过程中的混合事件、非线性空气动力学和碰撞效应，并生成物理有效且满足机器人动力学约束的多旋转发球轨迹。

Abstract: Robotic table tennis serves require high ball velocity and spin while respecting the robot's kinodynamic limits. Unlike rally strokes, a valid serve must also bounce on the server's side and clear the net, yielding a hybrid system with nonlinear flight and impact dynamics. We formulate spin-controlled serve generation as an event-time \ac{OCP} that optimizes the racket impact velocity and orientation together with the bounce, net-crossing, and landing times, enabling direct enforcement at phase boundaries of table-bounce and net-clearance constraints. The racket velocity and orientation are then converted into a complete kinodynamically feasible motion through a second \ac{OCP} enforcing joint-position, velocity, and torque limits. We evaluate the method numerically and on a KUKA Agilus robot. Compared with a fixed-step formulation with root localization, the proposed event-time formulation reduces the median solve time by a factor of 4.1 while maintaining comparable landing accuracy, spin accuracy, and serve validity. Real-robot experiments demonstrate controlled placement and topspin, backspin, and sidespin serves, with a mean landing error of $13.1 \pm 7.3$~cm and spin rates up to 30~rps. These results show that event-time optimal control efficiently generates physically valid, kinodynamically feasible serves while accounting for nonlinear aerodynamic and impact effects.

</details>


### [47] [SurgWMBench: A Vision-Based Benchmark for World-Modeling Surgical Instrument Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08070)
*Huanrong Liu,Weiliang Huang,Bob Zhang,Weichao Cai,Chunlin Tian,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SurgWMBench，一个面向短时域手术器械运动规划与动力学预测的视觉基准，用于评估模型预测未来器械轨迹及其在连续滚动和输入扰动下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视频理解方法主要识别阶段、动作或流程状态，缺乏对器械运动及其引发的后续视觉状态演化的联合建模。已有器械轨迹预测方法又通常忽略未来视频状态变化。此外，FVD、CD-FVD等生成质量指标无法直接衡量轨迹的几何准确性、时间一致性和下游规划可用性，而公开数据集与标准化评测协议也相对缺乏。

Method: 构建SurgWMBench视觉基准，以术中图像序列和历史器械轨迹为输入，围绕短时域运动规划与动力学预测进行评估。核心评测包括近未来器械运动预测，以及模型在连续滚动预测和输入扰动条件下的稳定性，从而覆盖轨迹精度、时序一致性和鲁棒性等能力。

Result: 摘要明确提出了基准的任务范围和评测维度，但未提供具体数据集规模、基线模型、定量结果或与现有方法的比较结果。因此，当前信息不足以判断SurgWMBench的实际性能提升或评测有效性。

Conclusion: SurgWMBench旨在将手术世界模型的评估重点从视觉生成质量拓展到器械运动预测、动态稳定性和规划可用性，为面向手术规划的模型研究提供标准化基础。其最终价值仍需通过数据集细节、公开基准结果和临床或下游规划验证加以确认。

Abstract: Reliable surgical planning requires models that move beyond recognizing the current surgical step or imitating expert demonstrations, and instead anticipate how instrument motion reshapes subsequent operative states. Most surgical video understanding methods focus on recognizing phases, actions, or workflow states, while providing limited support for explicitly modeling instrument motion. Conversely, existing tool motion prediction methods can forecast instrument trajectories, but they generally do not capture the coupled evolution of future surgical video states. World models offer a natural framework for jointly modeling visual state transitions and instrument motion dynamics. However, existing surgical world model studies remain largely centered on visual generation quality, relying on generation-oriented metrics such as FVD and CD-FVD. These metrics are poorly aligned with instrument motion planning, as they do not directly measure whether predicted trajectories are geometrically accurate, temporally coherent, or actionable for downstream planning. This limitation is partly structural, since the field lacks public datasets and standardized evaluation protocols that provide the benchmarking infrastructure needed to assess motion-centric capabilities in surgical world models. In this paper, we introduce SurgWMBench, a vision-based benchmark for short-horizon surgical motion planning and dynamics prediction. Given intraoperative image sequences and historical instrument trajectory, SurgWMBench evaluates both near-future instrument motion prediction and stability under continuous rollout or input perturbations.

</details>


### [48] [Compiling and Benchmarking Task-State Horizons for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08036)
*Meiqi Wang,Shichao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出任务状态跨度（Task-State Horizon, TSH）这一新指标，用于衡量具身智能体在长时程任务中持续跟踪任务相关世界状态转移的能力，并通过RoboGraph构建相应基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准主要依据动作序列长度和子任务复杂度衡量长时程难度，较少关注由智能体探索、环境动态、突发故障和干预引起的任务相关状态持续变化。本文旨在评估智能体维护、探索和更新这些状态的能力。

Method: 提出RoboGraph机器人任务编译器，将状态转移依赖关系转换为可执行的符号图，并依据空间与时间因果依赖构造不同任务状态跨度的任务。基准包含84个场景、588个回合，覆盖语义闭环和视觉闭环环境；实验评估了15种先进智能体模型。

Result: 多数模型在较高TSH任务上表现明显下降，说明其在长时程执行中难以持续维护任务相关状态、有效探索环境并及时更新状态表示。该基准能够区分不同模型对状态转移跨度的适应能力。

Conclusion: TSH是衡量长时程具身任务难度的重要补充维度。RoboGraph及其基准揭示了当前智能体在动态状态跟踪方面的显著不足，并为后续研究长期记忆、主动探索、状态更新和故障适应提供了评测工具。

Abstract: Frontier agentic models are increasingly deployed as high-level planners for long-horizon embodied tasks. Existing robotic benchmarks have advanced long-horizon evaluation, but primarily characterize difficulty through action-sequence length and subtask complexity, overlooking a distinct challenge: agents must track evolving task-relevant world states induced by both their exploration and environmental dynamics. We define the span of task-relevant state transitions that an agent must track as task-state horizon (TSH). To evaluate how agent performance varies with TSH, we introduce RoboGraph, a robotic task compiler that translates state-transition dependencies into executable symbolic graphs. Specifically, RoboGraph constructs task-state horizons from spatial and temporal causal dependencies, including those induced by unexpected failures and interventions during task execution. Building on RoboGraph, we release a benchmark comprising 588 episodes across 84 scenes with varying TSHs. Experiments evaluating 15 advanced agentic models in both semantic and visual closed-loop environments show that most models struggle with demanding TSHs, revealing substantial gaps in maintaining, exploring, and updating task-relevant state over long horizon.

</details>


### [49] [4D-WAM: Infusing Spatiotemporal Awareness into World Action Models through Trajectory Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08023)
*Lishan Yang,Wenxuan Song,Xi Wang,Pingyue Sheng,Zheng Fang,Ziyang Zhou,Junjie He,Haodong Yan,Jiayi Chen,Nan Sun,Qiao Sun,Lingqiao Liu,Yan Wang,Yuxiang Gao,Feras Dayoub,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 4D-WAM通过将3D轨迹场的时空知识注入世界动作模型，弥合2D视频表示与机器人3D执行空间之间的差距，从而提升空间理解、动作执行精度、鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型主要在2D像素空间中建模视频，难以充分表达机器人动作所处的3D空间关系；已有3D方法又未能充分利用3D结构的动态变化，因此需要学习兼具局部运动信息和长期目标信息的时空表示。

Method: 提出一种与基础模型无关的训练策略4D-WAM，通过表示对齐将3D轨迹场的时空知识注入世界动作模型。该方法包含两个互补目标：运动对齐，对相邻帧间的时间特征变化进行对齐，以学习局部4D运动感知；终点对齐，使模型根据源帧推断最终目标，并最小化源帧与目标帧之间类似注意力的相似度分布差异，以提供长期目标引导。

Result: 在不同基础模型上的分布内和分布外实验表明，4D-WAM在空间理解、动作执行精度、鲁棒性、泛化性和适用性方面均取得改进，并能够学习面向轨迹层面的时空表示。

Conclusion: 4D-WAM通过结合局部运动监督与长期目标引导，有效增强了世界动作模型对3D动态结构和机器人执行空间的建模能力，是一种具有较好模型兼容性和迁移潜力的训练策略。

Abstract: Building on recent advances in world models, World Action Models (WAMs) jointly model video prediction and action generation. However, they typically represent videos in 2D pixel space, creating a representation gap with 3D space in which robotic actions are executed. Recent 3D approaches introduce 3D information, but fail to fully exploit the dynamics of 3D structures. In this work, we propose 4D-WAM, a model-agnostic training strategy that injects spatiotemporal knowledge from 3D trajectory fields into WAMs through representation alignment. To this end, we introduce two complementary objectives: 1) motion alignment, which aligns temporal feature variations across adjacent frames and encourages the model to build local 4D awareness during training, and 2) destination alignment, which guides the model to infer the final destination from the source frame by minimizing the gap between their attention-like similarity distributions. Together, these objectives provide both local motion supervision and long-horizon goal guidance, enabling WAMs to learn trajectory-level spatiotemporal representations. Extensive in-distribution and out-of-distribution experiments across different base models demonstrate the model's improvements in spatial understanding, execution precision, robustness, generalization, and versatility.

</details>


### [50] [A Mixed-Stiffness Anthropomimetic Fingertip Broadens the Operating Range for Coin Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07887)
*Kaigen Go,Yinlai Jiang,Hiroshi Yokoi,Shunta Togo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究表明，在带有刚性指甲约束的软指垫中，将较硬材料布置于两侧、较软材料置于中央，可扩大机器人对硬表面上薄片物体进行斜向旋转捏取的有效操作范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬表面上的硬币等薄物体几乎没有可供手指插入的底部间隙，传统抓取方法往往需要重新定位物体、接触其底面或增加专用指甲机构。已有研究显示刚性指甲可抑制软垫变形并形成几何约束；本研究进一步考察，通过在指垫内部设置不同刚度边界，能否增强并扩展该约束。

Method: 制作仿人机器人指尖：中央采用Shore E10软硅胶，两侧采用Shore A60材料；以均匀Shore E10指尖作为对照。自动化装置执行斜向旋转尖端捏取，使指垫接触硬币侧面并在无底部间隙的情况下将其抬离硬表面。实验改变水平接近距离、垂直手指位移、食指旋转角度及硬币沿手指轴向的位置，并评估几何约束形成和过渡至稳定抓取的成功率；随后针对硬币尺寸进行调参，测试六种日本面额硬币。

Result: 混合刚度指尖在更多测试条件下保持较高成功率，尤其是在几何约束形成及向稳定抓取过渡阶段。无指甲的指尖在实验1-1的36种条件下全部失败。改变硬币沿手指轴向位置时，均匀软指尖反而表现更好，显示出明显的条件依赖性权衡。尺寸调节后，两类指尖均成功抓取六种日本面额硬币。

Conclusion: 薄物体抓取的操作范围不仅取决于指垫整体软硬度，还取决于刚度在带指甲指垫内的空间分布。中央柔软、两侧较硬的边界设计能够扩展部分抓取条件下的有效范围，但其优势并非普遍存在，指尖刚度布局应结合物体位置和具体运动参数进行优化。

Abstract: Robotic grasping of thin, flat objects such as coins on hard surfaces remains challenging because conventional methods require reorienting the object, accessing its underside, or adding a dedicated nail mechanism. We previously showed that a rigid nail arrests soft-pad deformation and thereby forms a geometric constraint that improves precision grasping. Here we asked whether an additional constraint-forming boundary, created within the pad by material choice rather than by anatomy, could extend the conditions under which that constraint holds. We fabricated anthropomimetic fingertips with Shore E10 silicone at the center and Shore A60 at the sides, and compared them with uniformly soft E10 fingertips. An automated apparatus performed an oblique rotational tip pinch in which the pad engaged the coin's lateral surface, lifting it from flush contact with no gap beneath it. Over variations in horizontal approach distances, vertical finger displacements, and index-finger rotation, the mixed-stiffness pair maintained high success rates across more tested settings than the uniform pair during both geometric-constraint formation and the transition to a stable grasp. The nail-free pair failed in all 36 conditions of Experiment 1-1. However, the uniform pair performed better when coin position along the finger axis was varied, a condition-dependent trade-off. After tuning for coin size, both fingertip types grasped all six Japanese denominations. These results suggest that the operating range for thin-object grasping depends not only on pad softness but also on where stiffness is placed within a nail-supported pad, making boundary placement a candidate fingertip design variable.

</details>


### [51] [Enhancing Autonomous Vehicle Navigation with a Clothoid-Based Lateral Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07740)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合缓和曲线与自适应前视距离的自动驾驶车辆横向控制方法，通过平滑曲率变化和动态调整前视距离，提高路径跟踪精度、横向稳定性与乘坐舒适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统横向控制方法在复杂道路几何和车辆动态变化下可能出现跟踪误差、响应滞后及曲率突变问题。研究旨在降低乘坐不适与侧翻风险，并提升车辆在多种工况下的路径跟踪性能，尤其面向铰接式车辆的应用需求。

Method: 以回旋曲线（Euler螺旋线）实现连续、平滑的曲率过渡；依据实时车辆动力学状态和道路几何特征自适应调整前视距离；采用准反馈控制算法在每个时刻构造最优缓和曲线并生成转向输入；引入超前滤波器补偿车辆横向动力学滞后。通过TruckSim与Simulink联合仿真进行验证。

Result: 联合仿真结果表明，所提出控制器在多种驾驶场景下能够改善横向控制性能，包括提高路径跟踪精度、增强稳定性和控制响应性，并有助于减少由曲率变化引起的乘坐不适。摘要未提供具体的定量指标或对比数值。

Conclusion: 缓和曲线、自适应前视机制、准反馈控制和超前补偿的结合，为自动驾驶车辆横向控制提供了有效方案。未来工作将面向更高车速扩展控制器，并进一步降低偏离轨迹误差，重点优化铰接式车辆的控制性能。

Abstract: This study introduces an advanced lateral control strategy for autonomous vehicles using a clothoid-based approach integrated with an adaptive lookahead mechanism. The primary focus is on enhancing lateral stability and path-tracking accuracy through the application of Euler spirals for smooth curvature transitions, thereby reducing passenger discomfort and the risk of vehicle rollover. An innovative aspect of our work is the adaptive adjustment of lookahead distance based on real-time vehicle dynamics and road geometry, which ensures optimal path following under varying conditions. A quasi-feedback control algorithm constructs optimal clothoids at each time step, generating the appropriate steering input. A lead filter compensates for the vehicle's lateral dynamics lag, improving control responsiveness and stability. The effectiveness of the proposed controller is validated through a comprehensive co-simulation using TruckSim and Simulink, demonstrating significant improvements in lateral control performance across diverse driving scenarios. Future directions include scaling the controller for higher-speed applications and further optimization to minimize off-track errors, particularly for articulated vehicles.

Abstract: Computing distances between sets is essential in robotic motion planning and control, where differentiable gradients enable real-time optimization. The Euclidean Signed Distance Function (SDF), however, is not differentiable everywhere, and existing alternatives often sacrifice differentiability, sign information, or computational efficiency. In this letter, we introduce a novel differentiable signed distance between convex polyhedra. To this end, we first propose differentiable versions of the minimum and maximum operators, termed the Hölder minimum and Hölder maximum. We then replace the original min-max operators in the classical SDF formulation, yielding the Hölder signed distance. Unlike prior differentiable distance formulations that rely on iterative algorithms, our approach is computed in closed form, eliminating convergence issues while remaining naturally amenable to GPU parallelization. We validate the practical advantages and computational performance of the proposed distance through runtime comparisons with existing approaches. We also present a robotic manipulator experiment, demonstrating its suitability for applications in control.

Conclusion: TRASER通过结构柔顺性、关节点重定位和质心转移的协同设计，显著突破了传统履带机器人的障碍跨越限制。其跨越性能据称达到履带移动机器人领域已报道结果中的最高水平，体现出在非结构化环境中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Tracked robots are widely used in unstructured environments; however, their obstacle traversal capability is fundamentally limited by a tradeoff between front-end reachability and locomotion stability. This study presents TRASER (Tracked Robot with Articulated Spine for Extended Reach), a reconfigurable tracked robot capable of relocating both its articulation point and internal mass. TRASER employs a tape-spring mechanism that localizes compliance to the bending region while maintaining high stiffness in the remaining body, thereby improving both front-end reachability and center-of-mass (CoM) shifting capability. Geometric and static models are developed to analyze the effects of articulation point and CoM position on step and ditch traversal performances. Experiments demonstrate step traversal, suspended-platform traversal, and ditch traversal of 74\%, 66\%, and 59\% of the robot body length, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, these results represent the highest reported obstacle traversal capabilities among tracked mobile robots.

</details>

*Yanping Zhao,Hang Yu,Yiwei Wang,Chen Ye,Siyu Tian,Di Zhang,Qingjun Wang,Qian Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GWM-VLA是一种面向视觉变化和环境变化的几何感知潜在世界建模框架，通过多视角几何编码、目标视角预测及共享潜在动作表示，提升VLA模型的鲁棒性与机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在视角、环境等分布偏移下性能容易下降；已有潜在世界模型通常独立编码各相机视角，并以整体场景动态预测为主，缺乏对多视角几何关系的显式建模，导致空间结构和局部交互信息利用不足。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的实验表明，GWM-VLA能够有效提升VLA模型的操作性能与环境变化下的鲁棒性，验证了几何感知多视角建模和共享潜在动作表示的有效性。摘要未提供具体数据、基线提升幅度或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 该工作将多视角几何关系、目标视角动态预测与动作监督统一到潜在世界建模框架中，为提升VLA模型在视觉和环境分布偏移下的泛化能力提供了有效方案。其主要贡献在于以较低预测成本保留跨视角几何信息，并通过联合训练加强世界模型与动作生成之间的一致性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong robotic manipulation performance but often degrade under visual and environmental shifts. Latent world modeling offers a promising approach to improving robustness, yet existing methods commonly encode camera views independently and predict holistic scene dynamics without explicitly modeling their geometric relationships. We propose GWM-VLA, a geometry-aware latent world modeling framework for VLA learning. GWM-VLA combines geometry-aware multi-view state encoding, global context-conditioned target-view prediction, and shared latent-action representations grounded by robot-action supervision. Specifically, VGGT-$\Omega$ jointly aggregates multi-view observations at each timestep to construct geometry-aware multi-view states. The latent world model predicts the next-step patch tokens of a selected target view using patch and register tokens obtained after multi-view aggregation, thereby retaining multi-view geometric information without predicting the complete multi-view state. We use the wrist view as the target in our experiments, placing greater emphasis on end-effector motion and local gripper-object interactions. Finally, the shared latent-action representations condition both the latent world model and the flow-matching action head, allowing latent-prediction supervision and ground-truth robot-action supervision to jointly shape the same latent-action representations. Experiments across both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of GWM-VLA.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人抓取方法主要依赖视觉信息，缺乏接触后的触觉反馈与动态调整机制，难以处理精细的手指—物体交互，因此在复杂物体和不同场景下的鲁棒性与泛化能力有限。

Method: 方法构建高效的视觉—触觉统一表征，将物体几何信息与触觉反馈紧密融合，并通过手指身份关联触觉信号。该表征既用于规划阶段的接触感知抓取姿态生成，也用于接触后的触觉引导抓取细化，从而实现对手指—物体接触关系的建模与动态修正。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的综合实验表明，该方法能够显著提高抓取成功率，并增强对多种不同物体的泛化能力。摘要未提供具体提升幅度、基线方法或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 视觉与触觉的统一融合能够有效支持从抓取规划到接触后调整的闭环过程，是提升机器人抓取稳定性、适应性和泛化能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Humans achieve stable and adaptive grasps by seamlessly integrating visual perception and tactile feedback, a capability that remains challenging to replicate in robotic systems. Existing robotic grasping approaches predominantly rely on visual inputs and lack mechanisms for tactile-guided adaptation after contact, limiting robustness and generalization. To address this challenge, we propose a unified visuo-tactile-fusion grasping framework that integrates grasp generation, feasibility prediction, and adaptive refinement. At its core, our method introduces an efficient visuo-tactile representation that tightly fuses object geometry with tactile feedback by associating tactile signals with finger identities. This unified representation supports contact-aware grasp pose generation during planning and tactile-guided refinement after contact, enabling the system to reason about fine-grained finger-object interactions and adjust grasps dynamically. Comprehensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that our approach significantly enhances grasp success rates and generalization across diverse objects.

</details>

*Alexander Htet Kyaw,Miana Smith,Paul Richard,Neil Gershenfeld*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将几何智能嵌入材料系统的机器人建造模块，通过自对齐、可逆连接和三维互锁聚合，实现可重构的家具与建筑结构装配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人建造通常将材料系统与机器人分开设计，主要依赖硬件、传感、运动规划和控制实现智能。本文旨在通过材料几何本身同时满足机器人抓取、自对齐、可逆连接、结构承载及三维聚合等要求，并支持循环建造。

Method: 研究构建了由相连立方八面体—八面体单元组成的复合嵌套晶格模块。立方八面体提供明确的机器人抓取与对齐表面，八面体集成可通过螺钉释放的卡扣连接器及对应 receptor；嵌套布局支持沿x、y、z三个方向的互锁聚合。系统通过机械臂和移动装配设备完成家具及建筑尺度结构的实验装配，并进行压缩测试。

Result: 系统成功构建了座椅、跨度结构、表面和垂直框架等多种形态。复合模块的压缩刚度为4556 N/mm，最大载荷为3445 N，压缩模量为17.5 MPa。模块能够拆卸并用于不同构型，验证了其可重构性和重复利用潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，几何智能可以直接编码于架构化材料中，从而减少对复杂机器人控制的单独依赖，并实现机器人可装配、结构性能与多向聚合的协同。所提出模块适用于可逆、可重构和循环性的机器人建造，但其进一步应用仍需结合尺度扩展、连接耐久性及复杂环境下的装配可靠性进行评估。

Abstract: Robotic construction systems often treat the material system and the robot as separate design problems, locating intelligence primarily in hardware, sensing, motion planning, and control. This project instead investigates how geometric intelligence can be encoded within architected material systems to simultaneously address requirements for robotic grasping, self-alignment, reversible connection, structural performance, and three-dimensional aggregation. We introduce a self-aligning compound nested lattice module composed of conjoined cuboctahedral-octahedral units. The cuboctahedral features of the modules provide defined surfaces for robotic grasping and alignment, while the octahedral features incorporate screw-releasable snap-fit connectors and corresponding receptors. Additionally, we present a nested arrangement that enables interlocking aggregation along the x, y, and z axes. We demonstrate the system through furniture and architectural scale structures assembled using both a robotic arm and mobile assembler. The resulting configurations include seating, spanning structures, surfaces, and vertical frames. Compression testing of the compound module produced a stiffness of 4,556 N/mm, a maximum load of 3,445 N, and a compressive modulus of 17.5 MPa. The modules can also be disassembled and reused across different configurations, supporting reconfigurable and circular construction.

</details>


### [57] [Projection-Retraction MPPI: Exact Constraint-Manifold Control for Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07573)
*Seulchan Lee,Leesai Park,Minhyeong Kang,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PR-MPPI，通过在采样动力学内部执行投影与回缩，使MPPI能够严格满足闭链等式约束及关节限位、障碍物间距等不等式约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MPPI主要将约束作为软代价处理，容易因任务代价过强而违反持续有效的运动约束，尤其难以保证双臂闭链的精确一致性以及关节和碰撞安全限制。

Method: 在每个采样滚动步骤中，将采样速度先投影到满足等式约束的子空间，再投影到该子空间内由不等式约束定义的半空间，从而避免破坏等式约束。由于有限步长会产生一阶近似误差，进一步将输出指令回缩至等式约束流形，使其达到数值容差并降低对任务代价权重的依赖。

Result: 在14自由度双臂系统上的仿真表明，PR-MPPI在关节限位压力测试和随机障碍物规避中均能将闭链等式误差控制在数值容差内。真实Unitree H1-2人形机器人实验显示，双臂能够对移动障碍物进行反应式规避。

Conclusion: PR-MPPI将MPPI的无梯度、并行采样优势与在线约束投影和回缩结合，能够更可靠地处理多类持续运动约束，适用于双臂协同操作及其他受约束的非凸控制任务。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is widely used in manipulation for its gradient-free, parallel handling of non-convex costs. Manipulation tasks, however, often impose constraints that hold throughout the motion: a closed kinematic chain that two grasping arms keep exactly, or joint limits and obstacle clearances that are never crossed. MPPI handles such constraints only through the cost, as soft penalties that hold approximately and fail under a strong task cost. To address this, we propose Projection-Retraction MPPI (PR-MPPI), which enforces the constraints inside the sampled dynamics. At every rollout step, the sampled velocity is projected to satisfy both constraint types: the equality restricts it to a subspace, and each inequality to a half-space within that subspace, so inequality handling never breaks the equality. This projection, however, satisfies the constraints only to first order, and a finite step leaves a small drift off the equality. Therefore, we retract the returned command back onto the constraint to numerical tolerance and independent of task weighting. We validate PR-MPPI on 14-DoF dual-arm systems. In simulation, the returned commands satisfy the closed-chain equality to numerical tolerance through a joint-limit stress test and randomized obstacle avoidance. On real hardware, the arms of a Unitree H1-2 humanoid reactively avoid a moving obstacle. Code and experiment videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [58] [You Don't Need To Stay in The Loop: An Agentic Robotics Loop for Robot-Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07555)
*Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgenticRobotics将代码智能体的闭环架构迁移到机器人策略改进，通过可验证的工具调用、持久化事务和证据门控，实现可恢复、可审计且不依赖后端的自动化控制平面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略训练、评估和数据采集工具经常失败，且工具质量会随底层产物变化而失效。传统智能体架构缺少对工具质量的持续度量、记录、过期管理和故障恢复机制，可能导致错误晋级或状态丢失。

Method: 系统采用LLM控制器驱动一次性工作器，执行持久化的训练—评估—改进事务；使用不可变目标、控制器自有测量、基于提交键的崩溃恢复、按证据分级的技能库，以及标准化并记录调用过程的工具注册表。工具质量在每次调用时测量，并在关联产物变化后失效；策略晋级由证据门控决定，结果和能力质量从历史记录中推导。

Result: 实验表明，强化后的每次运行错误晋级率为0.001，低于直接发布方案的0.005—0.021；在可选停止条件下仍能进行任意时刻有效的决策；注入进程终止故障后没有丢失或重复执行的效果；签名验证器捕获了六类中的全部六类产物篡改。就已测量的一条策略谱系而言，该系统并未优于人工选择更好的检查点。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了机器人策略改进可以通过证据驱动、可恢复和可审计的控制平面实现更可靠的自动化，但其核心贡献是流程可靠性与错误晋级控制，而非策略性能或检查点选择能力的提升。

Abstract: Coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex close the software loop: a main agent manages the loop, subagents analyze and execute, tools do the work. We port this architecture to robot-policy improvement, where one difference dominates the design: robotic tools---trained policies, training pipelines, data collection---fail routinely, so a tool's quality must be measured, recorded at every call, and expired when the artifact behind it changes. AgenticRobotics is a backend-independent control plane in which an LLM controller drives disposable workers through durable train--evaluate--improve transactions: an immutable objective, controller-owned measurement, commit-keyed crash recovery, an evidence-graded skill library, and a tool registry with a standardized, recorded call surface. The title is an operational claim, not a selection claim: the operator can leave because promotion is evidence-gated, state is recoverable, and capability quality is derived from records---not because the loop picks better checkpoints than a human; on the one lineage we measured, it does not. The gates measurably buy false-promotion control (0.001 per run hardened versus 0.005--0.021 shipped), anytime-valid decisions under optional stopping, zero lost or duplicate effects under kill injection, and six of six artifact-tampering classes caught by a signed verifier.

</details>


### [59] [Self Supervised Learning from Automatically Generated Demonstrations for Visual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07553)
*Andres Rivas,Anselmo R. Cukla,Rodrigo S. Guerra,Bruna V. Guterres,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需人工示范和显式相机—机器人外参标定的自监督视觉操作方法，通过仿真自动生成图像—位姿数据，训练网络预测目标相对位姿，并采用粗到细控制完成抓取对准。方法在仿真和UR5e真实机器人上进行了验证，显示出一定的定位精度和抓取成功率。


<details>
