  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人操作通常依赖针对物体的程序设计、人工数据标注、示教或精确的视觉标定流程，部署成本较高。本文旨在降低数据采集与系统配置要求，使机器人能够利用自动生成的示范数据快速学习目标位姿修正。

Method: 系统基于ROS 2和Isaac Sim自动生成带标签的RGB图像—相对位姿数据，不要求显式相机到机器人基座的外参标定。针对平面精调和三维粗略接近分别构建数据集，并训练卷积神经网络从单帧腕部RGB图像回归相对平移与旋转。执行时，机器人先使用包含高度变化的数据进行三维粗定位，再利用平面数据完成末端精调，最终在仿真和配备夹爪及单目相机的UR5e机器人上进行抓取测试。

Result: 仿真中，精调阶段将最终平面离散误差由9.69 mm降至5.38 mm。真实机器人实验覆盖三个实体物体；在不发生物体旋转的条件下，两个物体的端到端抓取成功率分别达到66.6%和63.6%。物体发生旋转时系统仍具备一定鲁棒性，但性能有所下降。

Conclusion: 自动生成的示范数据能够在较少系统配置和人工参与的情况下支持视觉操作，并实现可行的粗到细抓取控制。当前主要限制包括单目视觉下的深度预测能力不足、对物体姿态变化的适应性有限，以及模型在不同物体之间的泛化能力仍需提升。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires object specific programming, manual data annotation, or calibrated perception pipelines, which limits rapid deployment in practical settings. Learning from demonstration offers a more direct alternative, but collecting demonstrations can still demand human teleoperation or kinesthetic teaching. This paper presents a self supervised visual manipulation method in which a robot automatically generates demonstrations around a target pose and learns relative pose corrections directly from wrist mounted RGB images. The proposed pipeline uses ROS~2 and Isaac Sim to collect labeled image-pose pairs without requiring explicit camera to robot extrinsic calibration. Separate datasets are generated for planar refinement and coarse three dimensional approach, and a convolutional network is trained to regress relative translation and rotation from single frame RGB observations. During execution, a coarse to fine controller first approaches the object using models trained with height variation and then refines the final alignment using planar data. The method is evaluated both in simulation and on a real UR5e collaborative robot equipped with a gripper and a monocular camera. In simulation, the refinement stage reduces the final planar dispersion from 9.69 mm to 5.38 mm. In real world experiments, the system performs end to end grasp attempts on three physical objects and reaches success rates of 66.6% and 63.6% for two objects without object rotation, while still maintaining partial robustness under rotated conditions. These results show that automatically generated demonstrations can support practical visual manipulation with limited setup effort, while also exposing remaining challenges in depth prediction and object dependent generalization.

</details>


### [60] [Agentic Harnesses: LLM-Driven Verification Layers for Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09857)
*Rohan Bhagra,Mahantesh Halapannavar,Uddhav Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种由大语言模型驱动的机器人行动计划验证层，作为规划与执行之间的中间件，对计划进行批准、拒绝重写或提交人工复核，从而提升机器人自主系统的安全性与可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人自主研究更重视行动执行，较少验证规划模型提出的行动是否可行、合乎科学伦理且安全。规划模型可能受用户目标偏置、无法持续记忆既有风险，或受到自主系统中的对抗性输入影响，因此需要在计划到达底层控制之前增加独立的安全审查机制。

Method: 构建LLM-as-a-Judge集成框架，将多个模型的链式推理结果视为专家判断，并通过综合机制形成最终评估。该验证层部署在规划模块与MCP服务器及机器人低层控制之间，对每项计划执行门控分类：批准执行、拒绝并要求重新规划，或升级至人工审核。其设计结合了混合专家与自洽性思想。

Result: 系统在批准、升级和拒绝三类决策上的总体精确率接近85%，对对抗性攻击的拦截率达到97%。批准与拒绝之间的误判较少，主要错误集中在批准或拒绝与升级人工审核之间的边界。

Conclusion: 结果表明，LLM集成式验证层能够作为机器人自主系统的有效安全屏障，在不显著干扰执行流程的情况下提升计划审查和对抗风险控制能力。不过，约15%的分类误差及升级边界上的不确定性仍说明该方法需要更严格的基准测试、可解释性评估和人工监督机制。

Abstract: Advances in advanced artificial intelligence tools have sparked research in robot autonomy, but the development of such systems has largely focused on execution rather than verifying the feasibility actions planning models propose. Like general-purpose LLMs, robotics planning models carry risks: biased toward user-specified goals, they may suggest actions misaligned with scientific ethics, they may be unsafe due to an inability to "remember" prior safety risks, or they may be vulnerable to adversarial attacks on the autonomy ecosystem. We propose a LLM-driven verification layer between planning and execution to evaluate action permissibility. Our LLM-as-a-Judge ensemble combines chain-of-thought reasoning across models and synthesizes those expert judge outputs, mirroring a combination of a mixture of experts and self-consistency approach. This layer serves as middleware, gating plans from the server's planning module before they reach the MCP server and therefore the robot's low-level controls: plans are approved, rejected for reformulation, or escalated for human review. With this system, we achieve near 85% precision across accept/escalate/reject categories 97% containment of adversarial attacks, with negligible errors between accepting and rejecting tasks, and errors mostly manifesting at the escalate boundary.

</details>


### [61] [Predictive safety filter enhanced curriculum learning control for efficient vehicle dynamics controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09653)
*Baocong Zhang,Siliang Lu,Chenyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合课程学习控制器与基于物理的预测安全过滤器的方法，用于提升车辆运动与动力学控制的安全性、鲁棒性及控制性能，并在Python-CarSim平台上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型控制方法在复杂控制任务中表现出较强性能，但可能缺乏可验证的安全保证；传统车辆控制方法又存在参数标定工作量大等问题。因此，研究旨在兼顾学习控制的性能与效率，以及车辆控制的稳定性、灵活性和安全性。

Method: 构建课程学习控制器，使控制策略能够逐步适应不同难度的车辆状态控制任务；同时引入基于物理模型的预测安全过滤器，对控制动作进行安全约束和修正，以降低潜在风险并增强鲁棒性。方法在Python-CarSim联合仿真平台上进行测试。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在多种车辆操纵工况下具有更好的控制表现和可扩展性，并在稳定性与灵活性方面优于相关先前方法。摘要未提供具体的定量指标、基线方法或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: 课程学习与基于物理的预测安全过滤相结合，为车辆学习型控制提供了一种兼顾性能和安全性的方案。初步仿真验证支持其有效性，但仍需通过实车测试、形式化安全分析及更充分的定量对比来进一步验证实际应用价值。

Abstract: Recent advances in learning-based control have enabled impressive achievements in solving complex control problems in various domains. However, since learning-based control may not be able to realize safety-guaranties, it is of great importance to enhance safety and robustness while maintaining good performances. Take vehicle motion \& dynamics control as an example, in order to overcome the pain points of traditional methods such as heavy parameter calibration effort and learning-based control to bring better performance and efficiency in stability \& agility over prior work for state-based vehicle control tasks, in this work, our method aims to develop a curriculum learning controller enhanced with physics-based predictive safety filter. The validation is conducted with the Python-CarSim platform, demonstrating better improvements and scalability under various maneuvers.

</details>


### [62] [WorldSimProbe: Diagnosing Simulator Faithfulness in Action-Conditioned World Models for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09298)
*Peterson Co,Sicheng Hu,Chunxuan Jiao,Hongyang Cheng,Yulin Luo,Yijie Xu,Sixiang Chen,Zhongxia Zhao,Zihao Wang,DaFeng Chi,Peidong Liu,YuTong Chen,Henghua Liu,Zhihao Yuan,Huizhu Jia,Yuzheng Zhuang,Tianle Zhang,Liang Lin,Huajie Tan,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldSimProbe，用于评估动作条件世界模型是否具备物理模拟器所需的真实动作—运动—环境响应能力，而不仅是生成视觉上合理的视频。该方法在三个机器人基准、超过18,000个实例和六个开源模型上进行评测，发现模型普遍存在动作实现退化、交互 grounding 和动力学建模缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型评估主要关注视觉质量、任务成功率或粗粒度 rollout 响应，难以直接判断模型是否忠实模拟了动作引起的运动及其环境后果。为弥补这一缺口，论文提出可观测模拟器契约，要求输入动作产生对应的智能体运动，且环境响应必须以该实际运动为基础。

Method: 提出WorldSimProbe，包含五类受控测试：局部控制敏感性、全局轨迹变化、来源多样的动作、交互 grounding 和动力学。对应评估指标包括模拟器相对校准、稠密动作—运动对应关系、虚假交互 grounding 以及基元级动力学表现。实验覆盖RoboTwin、ManiSkill和LIBERO中的六个开源动作条件世界模型，共超过18,000个测试实例。

Result: 实验显示，模型在控制变化增大时出现系统性的动作实现退化；同时存在结构化的交互 grounding 失败和动力学建模错误。WorldSimProbe产生的评估信号与人工判断及下游任务结果具有一致性，说明其能够识别传统视觉或任务指标难以揭示的模拟器缺陷。

Conclusion: 论文将动作条件世界模型的评估从视觉逼真度和任务表现扩展到可观测模拟器能力，提供了透明、标准化且面向诊断的评测框架。其核心价值在于直接检验动作是否被正确实现，以及环境响应是否由真实实现的运动驱动，从而更准确地衡量模型作为物理模拟器的可信度。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models (ACWMs) promise to provide embodied AI with scalable predictive simulators for planning, policy evaluation, and data generation. Realizing this promise requires precise action-conditioned transitions rather than merely plausible outputs. Yet their applicability remains difficult to establish because prevailing evaluations emphasize visual quality, task outcomes, or coarse rollout-level responsiveness without directly testing simulator fidelity. To address this gap, we evaluate ACWMs through the observable capabilities expected of physical simulators. Accordingly, we formalize Observable Simulator Contract, a minimal contract that any action-conditioned physical simulator should satisfy: supplied actions must induce corresponding agent motion, and environment responses must be grounded in that realized motion. To operationalize this contract, we introduce WorldSimProbe, comprising five controlled suites spanning local control sensitivity, global trajectory variation, source-diverse actions, interaction grounding, and dynamics. Suite-specific evaluators assess simulator-relative calibration, dense action-to-motion correspondence, false-interaction grounding, and primitive-level dynamics. We evaluate six open-source ACWMs on more than 18,000 instances across RoboTwin, ManiSkill, and LIBERO. World-SimProbe reveals systematic action-realization degradation across control variation, structured failures in interaction grounding and dynamics, and benchmark signals consistent with human judgments and downstream outcomes. Together, this capability-based framework provides a transparent, and standardized paradigm for diagnosing ACWM simulator fidelity beyond coarse, task-directed evaluation.

</details>


### [63] [SpeedTuning: Speeding Up Policy Execution with Lightweight Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09138)
*David D. Yuan,Tony Z. Zhao,Kaylee Burns,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出SpeedTuning，一种通过强化学习预测动作最优执行速度的方法，可在不额外采集数据的情况下加速模仿学习策略。实验表明其速度提升超过2.4倍，同时保持较高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略的执行速度受机器人硬件和数据采集操作者速度限制，现有针对模仿策略加速的方法不足，且执行速度与任务成功率之间的关系尚未得到充分研究。

Method: SpeedTuning以基础任务策略为核心，通过强化学习学习每个动作的合适执行速度，并将速度预测模块与原策略结合，从而在无需额外示范数据的情况下实现自适应加速。

Result: 在动态和高精度操作任务上进行评估，包括倾倒、投掷和抓取。SpeedTuning实现了超过2.4倍的执行速度提升，并在成功率方面优于或保持在可接受水平，相较原始策略和固定速度线性插值等简单加速方法表现更好。

Conclusion: SpeedTuning能够有效提升真实机器人操作策略的执行效率，并兼顾任务成功率，展现出较好的鲁棒性和实际部署价值。

Abstract: While learned robotic policies hold promise for advancing generalizable manipulation, their practical deployment is often hindered by suboptimal execution speeds. Imitation learning policies are inherently limited by hardware constraints and the speed of the operator during data collection. In addition, there are no established methods for accelerating policies learned via imitation, and the empirical relationship between execution speed and task success remains underexplored. To address these issues, we introduce SpeedTuning, a reinforcement learning framework specifically designed to enhance the speed of manipulation policies. SpeedTuning learns to predict the optimal execution speed for actions, thereby complementing a base policy without necessitating additional data collection. We provide empirical evidence that SpeedTuning achieves substantial improvements in execution speed, exceeding 2.4x speed-up, while preserving an adequate success rate compared to both the original task policy and straightforward speed-up methods such as linear interpolation at a fixed speed. We evaluate our approach across a diverse set of dynamic and precise tasks, including pouring, throwing, and picking, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness in enhancing real-world robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [64] [From Operational Design Domain to Action: A Systematic Behavioral Taxonomy for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08941)
*Chaitanya Shinde,Hadi Hajieghrary,Miguel Hurtado*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向自动驾驶系统的行为能力分类法，将基于ODD的运行条件规范扩展为可验证的行为规范，并覆盖高速公路、城市道路和枢纽三类运行域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ODD主要规定系统允许运行的环境条件，却未充分说明系统进入该域后必须表现出的可验证行为，导致安全论证、行为测试和SOTIF覆盖之间存在缺口。

Method: 基于PEGASUS六层模型化ODD，系统构建21项行为能力，并按高速公路、城市道路和枢纽三类运行域组织。每项能力从纵向控制和横向控制进行分解，并依据安全性、合规性、舒适性和效率四项属性进行刻画。同时，将ODD层参数与行为能力交叉映射，生成可用于系统化行为测试的场景族；分类法参考AVSC00008202111、SAE J3237和SAE J3016，并部署于规则约束的轨迹优化系统中进行验证。

Result: 研究形成了覆盖三类运行域的21项行为能力分类体系，能够将环境参数与具体行为要求关联起来，并支持场景生成及SOTIF覆盖证据构建。系统部署结果表明，该分类法可作为具有操作性的行为规范层。研究还发现，枢纽域在结构上区别于高速公路和城市道路，且当前规范与研究相对不足。

Conclusion: 该研究为连接ODD定义、行为验证和安全论证提供了标准化框架。其主要贡献在于把抽象的运行条件转化为可测试、可度量的行为能力要求；后续应重点完善枢纽域的行为模型、场景库和验证方法。

Abstract: Operational Design Domain (ODD) specifications describe where an automated driving system (ADS) is permitted to operate, but they do not prescribe what the ADS must demonstrably do once deployed within that domain. This gap between operating condition specification and behavioral validation represents a critical unresolved challenge in ADS safety assurance. This paper presents a structured, standards-grounded taxonomy of 21 behavioral competencies organized across three operational domains-Highway (HWY), Urban (URB), and Hub (HUB)-derived systematically from the PEGASUS six-layer model-based ODD. Each behavior is decomposed along longitudinal and lateral control axes and characterized against a four-property framework: Safety (gap maintenance, conflict avoidance, kinematic stability), Compliance (legal rules and behavioral norms), Comfort (rider dynamics and trust), and Efficiency (mission completion and product-level metrics). We further demonstrate that the crossing of ODD layer parameterizations with behavioral competency specifications yields concrete scenario families suitable for systematic behavioral testing and SOTIF coverage evidence. The taxonomy is grounded in AVSC00008202111, SAE J3237, and SAE J3016, and is validated as an operational specification layer through its deployment in a rule-enforced trajectory optimization system. The Hub domain is identified as a structurally distinct, underspecified domain warranting dedicated research attention.

</details>


### [65] [DA-NBV: A Direction-Aware Next-Best-View Planner for Efficient 3D Reconstruction of Ships at Sea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08025)
*Jiaming Chen,Juntao Yang,Zhentao Zou,Qi Ming,Yi Yu,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang,Xue Jiang,Yue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DA-NBV方向感知下一最佳视点策略，通过融合方向观测统计、位置优势场、局部约束动作空间和非线性覆盖奖励，提升海上船舶三维重建的完整性、精度与扫描效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下一最佳视点方法主要关注空间占用状态，忽略观测方向历史，难以处理船舶复杂上层结构和严重自遮挡问题。海上环境中的升沉、横摇和纵摇会持续改变船体姿态与表面可见性，风扰动和有限能源也要求更高的路径与扫描效率。

Method: 提出DA-NBV方向感知策略，将方向观测统计引入传统占用状态；设计可学习的位置优势场PAF，引导视点选择；采用局部约束动作空间限制不必要的大幅移动；构建非线性覆盖塑形奖励以提升扫描效率；同时开发面向船舶的SeaShip-3D数据集和可配置海况仿真环境。

Result: 在不同升沉、横摇和纵摇条件下，DA-NBV使重建完整性提高约3个百分点，Chamfer距离降低43%，并取得更高的路径效率，表明其在动态海况下具有更好的重建性能和扫描经济性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模方向观测历史并结合运动约束与覆盖导向奖励，DA-NBV有效缓解了船舶自遮挡和海况扰动带来的视点规划困难，可为自主海上三维扫描提供更高效、准确且具扩展性的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate 3D reconstruction of ships at sea is important for maritime supervision, damage assessment, and autonomous maritime operations. Although 3D reconstruction has advanced considerably, high-quality data acquisition still largely relies on manually designed trajectories or skilled operators, resulting in high costs and limited scalability. Next-best-view (NBV) planning automates this process by selecting subsequent viewpoints based on the current state. However, existing NBV policies mainly model spatial occupancy while overlooking directional observation history. This limitation is particularly problematic for ships: their complex superstructures and severe self-occlusions require observations from multiple viewpoints, and insufficient directional coverage often yields incomplete reconstructions. These challenges are further amplified at sea, where wave-induced heave, roll, and pitch continuously alter the ship's pose and surface visibility. Meanwhile, wind disturbances and limited onboard power impose stricter requirements on scanning efficiency. To address these challenges, we propose DA-NBV, a direction-aware NBV policy that augments the conventional occupancy state with directional observation statistics. We introduce a learnable Position Advantage Field (PAF) that uses directional information to guide viewpoint selection. The policy further adopts a locally constrained action space and a nonlinear coverage-shaping reward to improve scanning efficiency. We also develop the ship-oriented SeaShip-3D dataset and a configurable sea-state simulation environment. Experiments under varying heave, roll, and pitch conditions show that DA-NBV improves reconstruction completeness by approximately 3 percentage points and reduces Chamfer distance by 43% while achieving higher path efficiency.

</details>


### [66] [Complete, Scalable, and Robust Prioritized Planning for Multi-Robot Ordered Storage and Retrieval at Maximum Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07734)
*William Zhang,Tzvika Geft,Jingjin Yu,Kostas Bekris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究最大容量下有序存取问题，提出一种面向高密度拼图式仓储的在线优先级多智能体路径规划方法，在单边界可达矩形网格中实现无死锁、可扩展的机器人协同存取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拼图式仓储通过取消通道提升存储密度，但高密度布局使多机器人协调面临路径冲突与死锁风险。现有通用集中式规划方法难以同时保证几何可行性、执行效率和规模扩展性。

Method: 针对先满容量存储、再按指定顺序检索的场景，利用无搬移存储布局的结构性质和不变量，设计在线优先级多智能体路径规划算法。该方法结合特定仓储排列约束进行任务协调，并通过鲁棒存储布局应对离开序列的不确定性。

Result: 实验表明，机器人数量增加时，系统完成时间近似线性改善，且在机器人数量不超过网格宽度C时仍具良好扩展性。该方法能够保证完备性并避免死锁；支持鲁棒性的额外算法开销很小，相较非鲁棒基线几乎不降低执行速度。

Conclusion: 本文将仓储几何布局性质与在线多机器人规划相结合，为最大容量拼图式仓储提供了高效且可扩展的无死锁解决方案，并验证了其在确定性和不确定性存取序列下的实用性。

Abstract: Automated warehouses face a fundamental trade-off between maximizing storage density and achieving high retrieval throughput. While puzzle-based storage (PBS) architectures increase capacity by eliminating aisles, coordinating multiple robots in these high-density spaces is computationally challenging due to the potential for deadlocks. This paper introduces a novel multi-robot formulation for the ``ordered storage and retrieval problem at maximum capacity''. The focus is on rectangular grids accessible from a single boundary, where loads need to be first stored up to full capacity and then efficiently retrieved, given a planned departure sequence. This work bridges the gap between geometric feasibility and execution efficiency by leveraging the properties of relocation-free arrangements. These properties guide an online, prioritized multi-agent path-finding algorithm, which is the main contribution of this work. Unlike general centralized planners, the approach exploits the specific invariants of the storage arrangement to guarantee completeness and prevent deadlocks, enabling scalability. Experiments demonstrate that the method achieves near-linear improvement in makespan with respect to the number of robots, up to $m = C$, where $C$ is the grid width. Crucially, the algorithmic overhead of supporting robustness is negligible; the system handles uncertainty in departure sequences using robust storage arrangements with no significant penalty in execution speed compared to the non-robust baseline.

</details>


### [67] [Generalizing deep reinforcement learning across cable-driven parallel robot configurations with actuator-level policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07546)
*Abir Bouaouda,Mohamed Boutayeb,François Charpillet,Dominique Martinez,Rémi Pannequin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向索驱并联机器人（CDPR）的执行器级深度强化学习策略，使同一策略能够适用于不同机器人构型、执行器数量及空间维度，并在真实8电机CDPR上实现了从仿真4电机平面CDPR迁移的三维运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度强化学习控制CDPR通常训练时间长，且策略依赖具体机器人构型、执行器数量和布置方式，跨平台泛化能力有限。同时，直接学习末端执行器控制涉及较复杂的正运动学问题。

Method: 在仿真环境中训练执行器级策略（ALP），由每个电机独立控制对应电缆长度以达到目标值，而不是直接控制整个机器人末端位置。该方法依赖逆运动学，将共享策略应用于不同CDPR构型，并通过仿真到现实迁移实现真实机器人控制。

Result: 实验表明，ALP在鲁棒性和控制精度方面优于传统强化学习方法。经过在仿真4电机二维平面CDPR上的训练，该策略成功迁移至真实8电机CDPR，并实现三维运动控制，表现出对执行器数量、空间维度和安装位置变化的适应性。

Conclusion: 执行器级深度强化学习为CDPR提供了一种与具体构型无关的控制框架，可减少对特定机器人模型的依赖，并提升策略的跨构型泛化能力。该方法验证了从低复杂度仿真系统向真实复杂CDPR迁移的可行性，但其性能边界和对极端构型的适应能力仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Cable-driven parallel robots (CDPRs) present diverse configurations and complex control challenges, which can be addressed by deep reinforcement learning (DRL) by learning their nonlinear dynamics. However, DRL methods often require extensive training time, and the resulting policies do not generalize well to different robot configurations or varying numbers of actuators. In this article, we introduce a novel DRL approach for controlling CDPRs that does not depend on the specific robot configuration. Our method trains an actuator-level policy that controls each motor to achieve its target cable length, in contrast to conventional DRL approaches that learn to control the entire robot to reach a desired end-effector position. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to apply DRL to control CDPRs using an actuator-level policy. This approach offers two main advantages: (i) a single shared policy can be applied to any CDPR configuration, regardless of actuator count, and (ii) reliance on inverse kinematics, avoiding the more challenging forward kinematics problem. Training is performed in simulation, and the learned policy is successfully transferred to a real CDPR. Experimental results show that the actuator-level policy (ALP) surpasses traditional reinforcement learning methods in both robustness and precision. We further control a real 8-motor CDPR with 3D motion using a policy trained on a simulated 4-motor planar CDPR operating in 2D. This illustrates that the proposed method is applicable to any CDPR configuration, independent of actuator number or placement.

</details>


### [68] [Removing Infrastructure Barriers in Human-Robot Collaboration Through Wireless Reconfigurable Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09658)
*Emma Takács,Mátyás Hajós,Ádám Juniki,Ádám Fischer,Zoltán Komáromi,Kristóf Abai,Dániel Horváth,Sándor Máthé,Konstantinos Kousias,Bence Tipary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于5G的无线人机协作实验平台，结合电池供电多传感器装置、边缘计算和计算机视觉，实现可重构工作单元中的实时感知与安全协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 再制造等高混合、低批量工业场景经常需要调整工作单元布局，但传统电力与数据布线限制了系统的模块化和可重构性；同时，适用于实时感知与安全协作的商用无线设备选择有限。

Method: 构建电池供电的多传感器平台，并集成用于目标检测、姿态估计和手部识别的计算机视觉模块。模型采用合成数据与真实数据训练，将高计算量任务通过5G卸载至边缘侧；系统分别在匈牙利和挪威部署，并在公有与私有、独立组网与非独立组网5G环境下进行测试。

Result: 视觉模型在不同光照和背景条件下能够稳定识别定向抓取姿态与人手，mAP@50-95达到97.74±0.10%，平均推理时间为12.5毫秒。网络测试显示，在网络与设备兼容时，往返响应时间最低可达12毫秒，满足安全、自适应人机协作的潜在需求；同时发现当前5G部署存在互操作性限制。

Conclusion: 该系统验证了无线5G、边缘计算与多传感器视觉技术支持灵活、可迁移人机协作工作单元的可行性，适用于再制造、操作员培训和用户研究。其实际推广仍需改进不同5G网络与设备之间的兼容性和互操作性。

Abstract: Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) plays a vital role in dynamic, high mix, low volume industrial scenarios such as remanufacturing, which frequently face workcell rearrangements. Traditional setups are constrained by power and data cabling, restricting modularity and reconfigurations, while the selection of commercial wireless devices suitable for real-time perception and safe collaboration are limited in availability. This paper presents a highly flexible, wireless, 5G-based system that serves as a versatile experimental testbed for applications including remanufacturing, operator training, and user studies. To eliminate infrastructure barriers, the workcell integrates a novel battery-powered, multi-sensor platform prototype. Additionally, to support operator safety and system adaptability across environmental shifts, the system integrates a computer vision module for object detection and pose estimation, further augmented for robust hand recognition. Trained on synthetic and real data, the model reliably detects oriented grasping poses and human hands across varying lighting and background conditions (with an mAP@50-95 of 97.74 +- 0.10% and a mean inference time of 12.5 ms). Offloading these computationally intensive tasks to the edge via 5G, the proposed architecture contributes to resolving the bandwidth-latency trade-off. To demonstrate portability, the system was implemented in both Hungary and Norway, and was evaluated across a combination of public and private, Standalone and Non-Standalone 5G infrastructures. The performed network experiments produced results in round-trip response times down to 12 ms in case of compatible network-device pairings, suitable for safe, adaptive HRC. However, these measurements also revealed practical limitations related to interoperability in current 5G deployments that should be addressed in future works.

</details>


### [69] [FactorDrive: Adaptive Multi-Step Reasoning Driven by Planning-Critical Factors for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09591)
*Guolei Huang,Tengfei She,Yuxuan Lu,Yao Huang,Yuqi Ye,Yongjun Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FactorDrive是一个面向端到端自动驾驶的视觉语言模型框架，通过规划关键因素驱动的自适应多步推理，并结合搜索引导的策略优化提升轨迹规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在自动驾驶规划中对空间与物理证据融合不足，推理适应粒度较粗，难以满足场景特定的规划需求；同时，面向高质量规划的推理路径优化在自动驾驶后训练中研究不足。

Method: 首先进行大规模驾驶领域指令微调，建立基础驾驶知识。随后构建PCF-CoT数据集，将轨迹相关的空间物理证据纳入链式推理，并围绕规划关键因素组织推理，使推理路径的组成与深度能够适应不同场景。进一步提出QS-GRPO，利用轨迹级规划奖励引导蒙特卡洛树搜索发现高质量推理路径，再通过群体相对策略优化更新模型策略。

Result: 在nuScenes开放环基准和面向闭环的NAVSIM基准上进行的大量实验表明，FactorDrive均取得了当前领先的规划性能，说明其在轨迹规划质量方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将规划关键因素、空间物理证据、自适应链式推理与搜索引导后训练相结合，有效增强了视觉语言模型的场景化规划推理能力，为自动驾驶中的推理路径优化提供了新的技术路线。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have advanced scene understanding and enabled explicit reasoning in end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing methods insufficiently integrate spatial-physical evidence into planning reasoning, while reasoning adaptation remains coarse-grained and falls short of scene-specific planning demands. Furthermore, reasoning-path optimization for higher planning quality remains largely unexplored in autonomous-driving post-training. To address these limitations, we propose FactorDrive, an end-to-end autonomous driving framework for adaptive multi-step reasoning driven by planning-critical factors (PCFs). We first perform large-scale driving-domain instruction tuning to establish foundational driving knowledge. Building on this foundation, we construct PCF-CoT, a chain-of-thought (CoT) dataset that grounds planning reasoning in trajectory-relevant spatial-physical evidence and organizes reasoning around scene-specific PCFs, enabling the composition and depth of reasoning paths to adapt to different planning demands. We further introduce Quality Search-Guided Group Relative Policy Optimization (QS-GRPO), which guides Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) with trajectory-level planning rewards to discover reasoning paths with higher planning quality and uses the resulting responses to optimize the policy through GRPO, thereby improving trajectory planning performance. Extensive experiments on both open-loop (nuScenes) and closed-loop-oriented (NAVSIM) benchmarks demonstrate that FactorDrive achieves state-of-the-art planning performance.

</details>


### [70] [VANE: Reliable Test-Time Training for Vision-Language-Action Models via Future Visual Representation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09448)
*Hongjin Ji,Guoyang Xia,Luoyang Sun,Fangxiang Feng,Lei Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VANE，一种面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的可靠测试时训练框架，通过基于当前视觉-语言上下文的提示适配、候选更新隔离及未来视觉结果验证，实现选择性且可逆的在线适应。在SimplerEnv WidowX上，相比对应TTT基线平均成功率提升3.2个百分点，但在Google Robot上的收益依赖具体任务与机器人形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时训练在闭环操作中存在两项主要问题：共享适配空间可能混合相互冲突的任务修正；在线更新可能在动作后果尚未显现前改变后续行为，从而引入不可靠适应。

Method: VANE根据当前视觉-语言上下文进行提示适配，并利用已执行动作产生的未来视觉后果进行学习。候选模型更新不直接作用于在线策略，而是与实时策略隔离；系统根据后续观测评估更新，仅在未来证据支持时提交，从而实现受约束、选择性和可逆的适应。

Result: 在SimplerEnv WidowX基准上，VANE相较对应的测试时训练基线将平均成功率提高3.2个百分点。Google Robot实验表明，部署阶段的性能增益并不稳定，受到任务类型和机器人形态的影响。摘要未提供更多统计显著性、各任务分项结果或计算开销信息。

Conclusion: 该工作证明，基于未来观测证据筛选候选更新，能够提升VLA策略测试时适应的可靠性。其优势主要体现在降低不受控在线更新风险，但泛化收益具有任务与实体依赖性，仍需进一步验证跨环境稳定性、适应成本和长期闭环性能。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) offers a lightweight way to adapt vision--language--action (VLA) policies from unlabeled deployment streams, but it remains difficult to use reliably in closed-loop manipulation. A shared adaptation space can mix incompatible task corrections, while an online update can alter subsequent actions before its consequences are known. We introduce a reliable TTT framework for VLA policies (VANE). VANE conditions prompt adaptation on the current vision--language context and learns from the future visual consequences of executed actions. Candidate updates are isolated from the live policy, evaluated on subsequent observations, and committed only when supported by future evidence, making adaptation selective and reversible. On SimplerEnv WidowX, VANE improves average success by $3.2$ percentage points over the corresponding TTT baseline. Results on Google Robot further show that deployment-time gains remain task- and embodiment-dependent. Together, these results demonstrate a constrained, evidence-based approach to adapting VLA policies during interaction.

</details>


### [71] [SG-WAM: Text-Grounded and Spatial-aware Semantic Guidance for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08839)
*Junjie He,Junfeng Li,Zhide Zhong,Haodong Yan,Ruixin Li,Yangyang Zheng,Jiaguan Zhu,Tianran Zhang,Yuqiao Du,Wen Chen,Shunbo Zhou,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SG-WAM通过视觉语言模型生成文本对齐且具空间感知的语义先验，并将其注入世界-动作模型，从而提升机器人操作中的指令遵循、未来视频预测和动作预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型主要依赖视觉线索生成未来视频与动作，文本编码器通常独立于视觉观测，导致语言指令与预测内容语义错位，进而降低动作预测准确性。

Method: 提出SG-WAM语义引导方法，将视觉语言模型用作语义规划器，预测文本对齐和空间感知的语义前瞻信息。其中，文本对齐前瞻用于识别指令对应的目标物体，空间感知前瞻用于提供精确操作所需的场景几何信息；随后将这些高层语义信息注入世界-动作模型，以共同约束未来视频生成和动作预测。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实世界实验中，SG-WAM均表现出优于现有方法的性能，能够实现更精确的机器人操作，并展现出更强的语言指令遵循能力。摘要未提供具体数据或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: 通过引入由视觉语言模型生成的文本与空间语义引导，SG-WAM有效缓解了世界-动作模型中的视觉-语言语义错位问题，提升了操作规划和执行的可靠性，证明了语义规划器与世界-动作模型结合的有效性。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, most existing WAMs generate future videos and actions by relying mainly on visual cues rather than language instructions, since off-the-shelf text encoders embed instructions independently of visual observations. As a result, the videos predicted by these WAMs are often semantically misaligned with their corresponding language instructions, which degrades the accuracy of the predicted actions. To overcome this limitation, we propose SG-WAM, a semantic guidance method for world-action models that leverages a vision-language model (VLM) as a semantic planner to enhance the instruction-grounding capacity of world-action models. Specifically, we train a VLM-based planner to predict text-grounded and spatial-aware semantic foresight. The text-grounded semantic foresight grounds the instruction by identifying the correct target objects, and the spatial-aware semantic foresight provides the scene geometry for precise manipulation. We then inject this foresight into the world-action model as high-level semantic guidance, ensuring that both future-video generation and action prediction faithfully follow the language instruction. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate the superiority of our semantic guidance method, showcasing precise manipulation and strong instruction-following capabilities.

</details>


### [72] [Action- and Language-Conditioned Video Assessment for Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08273)
*Hwanhee Kim,Jaehyun Jang,Seungmin Cha,Hyeonseo Yun,Donghoon Lee,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ALVA是一种基于视觉、动作序列和自然语言指令的轨迹级评估器，用于判断具身智能体在多步任务中的完成进度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖最终画面匹配或连续视觉嵌入相似度，可能忽略任务执行过程中不可缺少的中间状态转移，因此难以准确评估指令是否完成。

Method: ALVA采用预训练视觉语言模型分两阶段工作：首先结合执行动作总结连续帧之间的视觉变化；随后根据自然语言指令评估该总结，并输出离散的轨迹级进度分数。该分数可作为闭环策略优化中的终止反馈。

Result: 在模拟三维家庭环境中，ALVA表现出较为保守的评估特性，误报率接近于零。用于闭环策略优化时，其反馈效果优于基于静态图像和视觉嵌入的基线方法，并缩小了与真实标签评估器之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 动作与语言条件化的视频评估能够提供更可解释、可靠的轨迹级反馈，适合用于所研究的模拟具身控制任务，但其有效性目前主要在模拟环境中得到验证。

Abstract: Vision-based embodied agents executing multi-step natural language instructions require feedback mechanisms that assess task progress over complete trajectories. Conventional approaches based on final-frame matching or continuous embedding similarity may overlook intermediate transitions that are necessary for determining whether an instruction has been completed. We propose ALVA (Action- and Language-Conditioned Video Assessment), a trajectory evaluator that conditions its assessment on visual observations, the executed action sequence, and the natural language instruction. The method uses a pre-trained vision-language model (VLM) in two stages: it first summarizes frame-to-frame visual transitions conditioned on the executed actions and then assesses the generated summary with respect to the instruction to produce a discrete trajectory-level progress score. In simulated 3D household environments, ALVA exhibits a conservative assessment pattern with near-zero false-positive rates. When used as terminal feedback for closed-loop policy optimization, it provides more effective feedback than the evaluated static image and embedding-based visual baselines and reduces the performance gap to a ground-truth oracle. These results support action- and language-conditioned video assessment as an interpretable feedback mechanism for the evaluated simulated embodied-control tasks.

</details>


### [73] [PhysX-CoT: Structured Physical Reasoning from a Single Image to Simulation-Ready 3D Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08053)
*Jie Huang,Xiaohe Li,Jiahao Li,Fangli Mou,Chen Qian,Yuqiang Fang,Junhao Fan,Kaixin Zhang,Zide Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysX-CoT将单图像3D资产生成建模为显式、结构化的物理推理过程，通过可解析的部件级状态轨迹连接图像理解、几何生成与物理验证，从而提升几何、尺度及物理属性质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常让视觉语言模型直接输出供解码器生成几何和物理场的序列化资产，导致部件位置与局部形状在全局坐标令牌流中耦合，且中间物理状态未被显式监督、条件化或验证，限制了生成质量与可靠性。

Method: 将生成过程拆分为有序且机器可解析的部件级状态轨迹，依次涵盖部件分解、二维与三维定位、部件关系、粗粒度几何和表面属性。对这些状态进行独立监督，并用于条件化几何生成及奖励学习。几何表示采用因子化设计：三维框编码部件位置与尺度，局部编码描述形状；同时使用与推理链对齐的GRPO，联合优化解析有效性、定位、几何、部件放置和物理一致性。所有学习型基线在统一骨干网络、数据和冻结解码器下重新训练，以保证公平比较。

Result: 在统一评测协议下，PhysX-CoT在几何、尺度和物理属性指标上均超过最接近的完整任务基线。Oracle、令牌数量匹配和状态顺序等对照实验表明，显式中间状态具有实际功能，而非仅为表面化的推理链。在Unreal Engine 5中，生成资产表现出较高的解析、碰撞和关节运动有效性。

Conclusion: 单图像3D资产生成的关键改进方向是从输出中心范式转向显式结构化物理推理。将部件级中间状态作为监督信号、条件信息和奖励目标，并解耦位置与形状，有助于提升生成结果的可控性、物理一致性和仿真可用性。

Abstract: Simulation-ready 3D assets are central to robotics and embodied AI. Generating them from a single image is usually framed as a vision-language model that emits a serialized asset for a decoder to turn into geometry and physical fields, leaving the image-to-3D reasoning implicit. We argue the limiting factor is this output-centric view: part placement and local shape are entangled in one global-coordinate token stream, and the intermediate physical states are never exposed for supervision, conditioning, or verification. PhysX-CoT instead casts single-image asset generation as an explicit structured physical reasoning process, an ordered and machine-parseable trajectory of part-level states covering decomposition, 2D and 3D grounding, relations, coarse geometry, and surface cues that we separately supervise, use to condition geometry, and treat as reward targets. Geometry is factorized so that 3D boxes carry placement and local codes carry shape, and CoT-aligned GRPO optimizes parse validity, grounding, geometry, placement, and physical consistency. Under a unified protocol that retrains all learned baselines on the same backbone, data, and frozen decoder, PhysX-CoT outperforms the closest full-task baseline across geometry, scale, and physical-attribute metrics. Oracle, token-matched, and state-order controls show the explicit states are functional rather than cosmetic, and in Unreal Engine~5 the generated assets parse, collide, and articulate at high validity.

</details>


### [74] [Drone-Assisted UAV-UGV Collaboration for Autonomous Navigation in Snow-Covered Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07797)
*Shreyam Gupta,P. Agrawal,Priyam Gupta,R. Gautam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向高海拔积雪地形的UAV-UGV协同导航框架，融合高效U-Net道路分割、EKF定位、YOLOv5视觉跟踪与动态路径规划，实现复杂低能见度环境下的实时导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高海拔雪地存在低能见度、积雪覆盖和地面不稳定等问题，使传统道路识别、定位与路径规划方法难以稳定工作，因此需要一种具备环境适应性和实时性的协同导航方案。

Method: 采用定制高效U-Net进行道路分割，并通过合成积雪数据增强提升模型对雪地场景的适应性；利用融合GPS与IMU的扩展卡尔曼滤波器进行UAV定位；基于UAV RGB-D相机获取的图像和深度信息，结合YOLOv5实现UGV视觉跟踪；根据道路分割结果动态调整路径，以适应积雪堆积和地形变化。

Result: 道路分割准确率达到96.5%；UAV最大观测位置误差约为±0.5米；在遮挡和低能见度测试环境中，系统能够成功完成导航，且路径偏差较小。摘要未提供具体的测试规模、基线方法、运行帧率或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 该框架初步验证了UAV提供空中感知、UGV执行地面导航的协同模式在复杂雪地环境中的可行性。其主要优势在于较高的分割精度、较好的定位性能和动态环境适应能力，但仍需通过更大规模实地测试、与更多基线比较以及对计算效率和鲁棒性的定量评估来进一步验证。

Abstract: This paper presents a collaborative UAV-UGV navigation framework for high-altitude, snow-covered terrain, where reduced visibility and unstable ground render conventional methods ineffective. We introduce a custom efficient U-Net architecture that falls under the computational constraints for real-time road segmentation, utilizing a novel synthetic snow data augmentation technique to achieve 96.5% segmentation accuracy. For UAV localization, we implement an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) fusing onboard GPS and IMU data, achieving a maximum observed positional error of +-0.5 meters. The UGV position is determined via a visual tracking pipeline using YOLOv5 and depth data from the UAV's RGB-D camera. A dynamic path planning algorithm utilizes this segmentation to adjust for snow drifts, enabling successful navigation in obscured test environment with minimal deviation.

</details>


### [75] [LIRA: Local Cross-Layer Information Routing for Vision-Language-Action Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07596)
*Zhewei Zhang,Puyue Wang,Guanren Qiao,Yijie Weng,Jiawei Hu,Guo Li,Lujia Wang,Junyan Wang,Tao Gu,Hongliang Lu,Guiliang Liu,Hong Jia,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LIRA，一种面向视觉-语言-动作模型的局部跨层动作条件机制，通过深度感知的信息路由融合视觉语言模型不同层级的任务信息，从而提升机器人操作性能与分布偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型中的VLM到动作解码器接口存在信息利用不足：有的仅使用较窄的表示层级，有的将每个解码器模块固定对应单一VLM层，难以整合不同深度中互补的任务证据。

Method: LIRA作用于任务令牌特征和由中间VLM状态生成的LIRA Query特征。它为每个并行融合模块分配以对应VLM层为中心的深度对齐局部窗口，聚合邻近层的LIRA Query特征，并与任务令牌特征及本体感觉输入共同融合后预测动作。该方法不改变主干网络、动作解码器和监督训练流程。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN ABC→D及真实世界操作任务中，采用相同的0.5B参数配置时，LIRA整体优于VLA-Adapter基线。在LIBERO-Plus零样本迁移中，平均成功率由59.1%提升至78.0%，绝对提升18.9个百分点。

Conclusion: LIRA有效改善了VLM中间层信息到动作解码器的路由与融合，在不增加架构和训练流程复杂度的前提下提升了VLA模型的任务性能及对受控分布偏移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform representations from pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into robot actions, yet the interface that routes intermediate VLM features into action decoders remains underexplored. Existing designs either expose only a narrow part of the representation hierarchy or rigidly match each decoder block to one VLM layer, restricting access to complementary task evidence across depths. We introduce LIRA, a local cross-layer action-conditioning mechanism that formulates VLM-to-action conditioning as depth-aware information routing. LIRA operates on task-token features and LIRA Query features derived from intermediate VLM states, then assigns each Parallel Fusion Block a depth-aligned local window centered on its corresponding VLM layer. Parallel Fusion Blocks aggregate neighboring LIRA Query features and integrate them with task-token features and proprioceptive inputs before action prediction. This routing interface leaves the backbone architecture, action decoder, and supervised training recipe unchanged. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, and real-world manipulation, LIRA improves the principal aggregate metrics over the VLA-Adapter baseline under the same 0.5B-parameter configuration. In zero-shot transfer to LIBERO-Plus, LIRA increases average success from 59.1% to 78.0%, an 18.9-point gain indicating improved robustness under controlled distribution shifts.

</details>


### [76] [Learning Physical Interaction: A Survey of Tactile- and Force-aware Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07558)
*Shilin Shan,Chuhao Zhou,Ruize Wang,Xinyan Chen,Xiangyu Chen,Xinyu Zhou,Boyu Ma,Iris Yuxuan Hu,Jingliang Li,Celeste Yuxuan Hu,Geng Li,Guohao Chen,Tianrui Zhu,Zhe Li,Yanjie Ze,Haoran Geng,Zhiyang Dou,Jianxin Bi,Yuejiang Liu,Jianshu Zhou,Jiachen Li,Paul Liang,Tatsuya Harada,Robert Katzschmann,Harold Soh,Na Li,Edward Johns,Danica Kragic,Jan Peters,Wojciech Matusik,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jitendra Malik,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于力觉与触觉感知机器人学习的综述，提出TF-ART分类体系，从多模态感知和多阶段系统设计两个维度，统一梳理物理交互操作方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触敏感型操作不仅依赖视觉和运动规划，还需要力调节、触觉反馈与自适应控制。现有综述尚未从统一视角系统分析力觉/触觉感知、多模态融合及多阶段架构之间的关系，因此本文旨在弥补这一研究空白。

Method: 提出TF-ART（Tactile/Force-Aware Robot learning Taxonomy）层次化分类框架，分析不同方法如何组织视觉、力觉、触觉、语言和本体感觉等观测模态，如何编码与融合异构信息，以及如何在高层策略、动作细化模块和低层反应式控制器之间生成、修正并执行动作。同时结合任务场景与基础设施需求，考察算法和实际部署条件。

Result: 本文形成了一个统一的力觉/触觉感知机器人学习方法分类视角，覆盖多模态观测、信息融合、多阶段动作生成与细化，以及学习策略与机器人末端反应式控制的衔接。摘要未报告具体实验数据、性能指标或定量比较。

Conclusion: TF-ART为理解和组织力觉、触觉感知机器人学习研究提供了系统化框架，并将算法设计与物理交互任务的实际基础设施需求联系起来，有助于识别现有方法的设计模式、应用条件与未来研究方向。

Abstract: Physically grounded robot intelligence requires robots to perceive, reason about, and regulate their interactions with the physical world. This capability is particularly critical in contact-sensitive manipulation, where successful task execution depends not only on visual perception and motion generation, but also on force regulation and adaptive control. In this context, recent robot learning methods have made substantial progress by integrating force, tactile, vision, language, and proprioceptive sensing into learned manipulation policies. In parallel, many systems adopt multi-phase architectures that combine high-level policies, action-refinement modules, and low-level controllers to bridge semantic task understanding with reactive physical execution. Despite these advances, existing surveys have not explicitly reviewed force- and tactile-aware robot learning from a unified perspective that jointly captures multimodal sensing and multi-phase system design. This survey addresses this gap by proposing TF-ART, a Tactile/Force-Aware Robot learning Taxonomy for multimodal and multi-phase frameworks, which maps individual methods into a unified hierarchical structure. The framework characterizes how recent works organize observation modalities, encode and fuse heterogeneous sensory inputs, generate and refine actions across multiple phases, and connect learned policies to reactive robot-end control. Building on this methodological view, we further examine the task settings and infrastructure requirements of physical interaction, thereby integrating both algorithmic and practical perspectives on force- and tactile-aware robot learning.

</details>


### [77] [AeroDPO: Unleashing Lightweight UAV Navigation with High-Fidelity Perception and Automated Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07557)
*Peng Xu,Chengcheng Wang,Shaohua Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究面向无人机视觉语言导航的轻量化与鲁棒控制，发现高质量视觉感知比扩大语言模型规模更关键，并提出基于物理仿真回滚的AeroDPO，以低成本提升分布外场景表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端UAV-VLN方法通常依赖数十亿参数的大型语言模型，难以满足边缘设备的低延迟要求；同时，纯行为克隆缺乏负反馈，导致模型难以学习稳健的空间约束，在分布外环境中容易发生碰撞。

Method: 作者开展跨模型规模评估，比较视觉感知质量与语言推理能力的影响；随后提出AeroDPO自动化偏好优化流程。该流程利用确定性物理仿真进行状态回滚，将碰撞前后的错误动作提取为拒绝样本，并通过特权干预生成规避碰撞的偏好动作，再由离线视觉语言检查器过滤视觉歧义，最终对2B模型进行离线DPO训练。

Result: 配备高保真视觉输入的2B模型在总体成功率上达到与7B基线相当的水平。加入AeroDPO后，模型在未映射场景中的成功率提升至49.16%，同时显著降低碰撞率，并取得当前自主空中智能体的最佳性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在UAV-VLN中，提升视觉感知质量可能比扩大语言模型规模更有效。基于仿真回滚的自动偏好数据生成能够以无需人工标注的方式弥补行为克隆的鲁棒性缺陷，为低延迟、可部署的轻量化导航策略提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV-VLN) requires rapid and reactive control in complex 3D environments. Recent minimalist end-to-end paradigms show great promise but typically rely on massive language models containing billions of parameters, incurring prohibitive latency for real-world edge deployment. In this paper, we challenge this parameter-heavy reliance. Comprehensive cross-scale evaluations reveal the critical insight that perception quality fundamentally outweighs language reasoning capacity. We demonstrate that a lightweight 2B model equipped with high-fidelity visual inputs completely matches the overall success rates of massive 7B baselines. However, this minimalist policy exposes a fundamental robustness flaw inherent to pure Behavior Cloning (BC). Lacking explicit negative feedback, the agent fails to internalize robust spatial constraints and exhibits alarming collision rates in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To overcome this vulnerability without relying on unscalable human annotations, we propose AeroDPO, a zero-cost automated Direct Preference Optimization pipeline driven by deterministic physical simulation state rollback. Upon detecting collisions, the system autonomously rewinds the environment to extract causal reasoning errors as rejected actions, applies decoupled privileged interventions to synthesize collision-avoidance preferred maneuvers, and leverages an offline vision language inspector to filter visual ambiguities. By equipping our 2B model with this automated data flywheel, AeroDPO boosts success rates to 49.16% on unmapped scenarios while drastically suppressing collision rates, establishing a new SOTA for autonomous aerial agents.

</details>


### [78] [SC$^{2}$-WM: A Self-Correcting World Model with Closed-Loop Feedback for Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07548)
*Xuan Yao,Yuze Zhu,Junyu Gao,Zongmeng Wang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SC²-WM，一种面向连续环境视觉-语言导航的自校正世界模型框架，通过世界模型预测反馈实现闭环决策，并在必要时于测试阶段选择性更新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN-CE方法多采用开环执行，难以及时发现和纠正推理过程中的内部状态漂移，导致在部分可观测和复杂场景下导航鲁棒性与泛化能力不足。

Method: SC²-WM利用世界模型的前瞻预测生成内部反馈，在执行动作前进行状态级计划细化；当反馈表明世界模型的建模能力不足时，进一步采用条件式世界感知适应，在测试阶段选择性更新世界模型，实现模型级纠正。

Result: 在标准VLN-CE基准上的实验表明，该方法能够提升导航鲁棒性和跨场景泛化能力。摘要未提供具体数据、对比方法或显著性分析结果。

Conclusion: 通过将世界模型预测反馈引入闭环决策，并结合按需测试时适应，SC²-WM能够缓解状态漂移和模型能力不足问题，为连续环境中的视觉-语言导航提供了更可靠的自校正机制。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to make fine-grained navigation decisions under partial observability. However, most existing methods rely on open-loop execution, lacking mechanisms to detect and correct internal state drift during inference. We propose SC$^{2}$-WM, a self-correcting world model framework that introduces internal feedback for closed-loop decision making in VLN-CE. Our method derives feedback from world-model foresight to perform state-level plan refinement before action execution. To handle challenging scenarios, we further introduce conditional world-aware adaptation, which enables model-level correction by selectively updating the world model at test time when feedback indicates model capacity insufficiency. Experiments on standard VLN-CE benchmarks demonstrate improved navigation robustness and generalization. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [79] [RynnValue: Scaling Robotic Value Foundation Models with Temporal Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09853)
*Dongchi Huang,Hongyin Zhang,Bohan Hou,Siteng Huang,Zhian Su,Hang Guo,Tong Lu,Zhaofeng Xu,Jiahao Tang,Jianfei Yang,Donglin Wang,Peixi Peng,Mingxiu Chen,Deli Zhao,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出RynnValue，一种面向机器人操作的开放式价值基础模型，以时间距离作为统一监督信号，在无需偏好标注的情况下学习从当前观测到语言目标的有向代价，并实现跨任务、跨本体和跨视角泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用奖励模型已成为机器人学习扩展的瓶颈。现有方法依赖偏好或归一化进度等任务内部锚点，难以在不同机器人本体和异构数据源之间迁移，且大规模偏好标注成本高。论文旨在寻找可直接从现有数据中构造、具有跨任务一致性的价值学习目标。

Method: 将时间距离定义为从观测到语言指定目标的有向cost-to-go，并利用视频时间戳自动生成监督标签。模型在超过7000小时、约300万条指令条件片段上训练。为提升大规模时间价值学习的可靠性，采用随机时间采样、时间顺序打乱和值隔离注意力，减少模型利用时序捷径并增强其对失败、退化和回归状态的敏感性。随后通过基于势函数的奖励塑形，将预测价值转换为稠密奖励。

Result: 在RBM-EVAL-OOD上，RynnValue的平均Kendall tau_a为0.675，高于完全依赖偏好监督的现有最佳结果0.655，并显著超过仅使用进度监督的模型0.292。模型能够零样本泛化到未见任务、机器人本体和视角。作为稠密奖励后，真实环境策略成功率由52.5%提升至72.5%，离线策略成功率由63.8%提升至82.5%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，时间距离是可扩展且具有跨数据源迁移能力的价值监督目标，也是一种实用的通用机器人奖励接口。RynnValue在减少人工偏好标注的同时提升了价值排序和策略性能，但其效果仍取决于时间戳质量、目标定义以及对复杂长时程任务的进一步验证。

Abstract: General-purpose reward models are increasingly the bottleneck for scaling robot learning, yet the recipe for learning value-related capabilities from large-scale heterogeneous corpora remains underexplored. Existing approaches tie supervision to task-internal anchors such as preferences or normalized progress, none of which transfer cleanly across embodiments and data sources. We introduce RynnValue, an open-source value foundation model for robotic manipulation that replaces these anchors with temporal distance, the directed cost-to-go from an observation to the language-specified goal. Because temporal-distance labels can be derived directly from timestamps, RynnValue scales to over 7,000 hours and roughly 3M instruction-conditioned clips without preference or progress annotations. To make temporal-value learning reliable at scale, we combine random temporal sampling, temporal-order shuffling, and value-isolation attention, suppressing shortcuts that would leave predictions insensitive to failures and regressions. Trained without preference labels, RynnValue attains an average Kendall's tau_a of 0.675 on RBM-EVAL-OOD, surpassing the fully preference-supervised state of the art (0.655) and more than doubling a progress-only counterpart (0.292), while generalizing zero-shot to unseen tasks, embodiments, and viewpoints. Converted into dense rewards via potential-based shaping, it raises real-world policy success from 52.5% to 72.5% online and from 63.8% to 82.5% offline. These results establish temporal distance as a scalable supervision target and practical reward interface for generalist robot policies.

</details>


### [80] [SAFE-CHEM: Uncertainty-Aware Policy Switching for Robust Robotic Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09303)
*Laura Jones,Shazil Shahzad,Ayesha Sana,Gabriella Pizzuto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAFE-CHEM是一种面向化学实验室自主机器人的不确定性感知安全控制框架，通过集成模仿学习策略与规则控制器，在高不确定性情况下自动切换至安全备用控制，从而提升任务成功率并减少安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人可加速化学实验并生成用于人工智能科学发现的数据，但学习策略难以可靠区分安全与不安全动作，容易出现过度自信的外推和严重操作失误。因此，需要一种能够在线识别不确定性并主动规避风险的控制方法。

Method: 框架采用多个基于循环神经网络的模仿学习策略组成的集成模型，以动作预测方差估计在线认知不确定性；随后利用核密度估计刻画成功样本条件下的不确定性分布，并据此校准安全阈值。当不确定性超过阈值时，系统由学习策略切换至确定性的基于规则的备用控制器。

Result: 在三项基础实验室操作任务上的评估表明，与传统单策略基线相比，混合控制策略提高了总体任务成功率，并减少了关键安全违规。此外，该方法成功实现了从仿真环境到实体Frankа Production 3机械臂的零样本迁移，显示出实际部署可行性。

Conclusion: SAFE-CHEM通过将不确定性估计、学习控制与规则安全备份相结合，为高风险化学实验中的自主机器人提供了更稳健的安全机制。研究结果表明，该框架能够在保留学习策略灵活性的同时降低失控风险，但其性能仍可能依赖不确定性校准质量、任务覆盖范围及备用控制器的设计。

Abstract: The deployment of autonomous robotic systems in chemistry laboratories is accelerating experimental workflows and providing the foundational data for AI-driven scientific discovery. However, despite the success of data-driven methods in acquiring dexterous skills, safety remains a primary barrier to their deployment in high-risk domains, such as early-stage materials chemistry experiments. Specifically, learning-based policies frequently struggle to distinguish between safe and unsafe actions, leading to overconfident extrapolation and potentially catastrophic failures. To mitigate these safety risks, we propose SAFE-CHEM, an uncertainty-aware framework designed for robust, learning-based robotic chemists. Our approach leverages an ensemble of recurrent neural network-based imitation learning policies to quantify epistemic uncertainty online through the variance of action predictions. By characterising the success-conditioned density of this variance using kernel density estimation, we introduce a hybrid control architecture that autonomously switches from the learned policy to a deterministic, rule-based backup controller when uncertainty exceeds a calibrated safety threshold. We evaluate SAFE-CHEM across three fundamental laboratory manipulation tasks, where our empirical results demonstrate that this hybrid strategy improves overall task success rates and reduces critical safety violations compared to traditional single-policy baselines. Finally, we demonstrate the practical viability of the framework through zero-shot sim-to-real transfer onto a physical Franka Production 3 robot manipulator.

</details>


### [81] [Particle-Based Conformal Prediction for Contact-Aware Uncertainty Calibration in Stratified Configuration Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09166)
*Luís Marques,Kristian Popov,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CaPTURe，一种面向机器人接触交互场景的几何感知、基于保序预测的粒子集不确定性表示方法，可生成具有概率覆盖保证的未来状态预测区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统在运动规划和执行过程中同时面临随机性与模型失配，尤其在接触障碍物时，未来状态分布可能呈现多峰、非连通或低维流形结构。传统解析模型或学习模型难以准确刻画这些不确定性，可能导致规划不安全或任务失败。

Method: CaPTURe利用任意保真度的粒子模型表示运动不确定性，并基于系统状态转移的校准数据进行局部保序校准。该方法结合几何结构构造预测区域，使其在用户设定的概率水平下覆盖真实未来机器人状态，同时区分并适应接触与非接触运动中的不确定性变化。

Result: 在迷宫中操控弹珠和机械臂高精度插入两个仿真任务上进行评估。与相关基线相比，CaPTURe在接触和非接触场景下均达到用户指定的覆盖率要求，并使任务成功率相较最佳基线最高提升30个百分点。

Conclusion: CaPTURe能够在复杂接触环境中提供具有统计覆盖保证且适应多样状态分布的不确定性表示，从而提升机器人规划与执行的可靠性和任务成功率。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty representation is essential for deploying autonomous systems that interact with their environment, as robots must reason about how uncertainty arising from both stochasticity and model mismatch is impacted by contacts with obstacles (e.g., when navigating through a cluttered environment or inserting a part into an assembly). We propose Calibrated Particle-sets for Trans-dimensional Uncertainty Representation (CaPTURe), a geometry-aware, conformal prediction-based algorithm that generates probabilistically valid prediction regions of the unknown future system configuration using particle-based models of arbitrary fidelity. While calibrated uncertainty predictions are essential for safe and efficient planning, analytical or learned motion models are often inaccurate - due to limited data, simplifying assumptions, unmodeled effects, etc. - which can lead to unsafe executions or task failure. Additionally, when a robot contacts an obstacle, the distribution of its future configurations can become multimodal or disjoint, or lie along manifolds of lower intrinsic dimension than the space of possible robot configurations. Our method uses a calibration dataset of system transitions to locally calibrate motion uncertainty estimates, constructing regions guaranteed to contain the future robot configuration at a user-set probability. Our calibration procedure captures how motion uncertainty varies between contact-rich and contactless motions, leading to sufficient coverage in both cases. We evaluate our method on two simulated planning tasks: controlling a marble around a labyrinth and performing tight-tolerance peg-in-hole insertion with a manipulator. Compared to relevant baselines, CaPTURe achieves the user-specified coverage requirement both in and out of contact and achieves up to a 30% absolute improvement in task success rate over the best baseline.

</details>


### [82] [Curriculum Generation under Structured Parametric Environments for Robust Navigation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08545)
*Prishita Ray*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向连续环境参数的重参数化课程生成框架，通过单向梯度优化自动构建训练课程，并结合分布偏移正则化提升多模态观测下的策略鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主导航策略需要适应转向率、障碍物、摩擦力、坑洼和坡度等连续变化环境。现有课程学习方法在课程自动设计、样本效率及多模态输入表征方面仍存在不足。

Method: 将结构化连续环境参数进行重参数化，并利用单向梯度优化生成课程；针对图像与标量组成的多模态观测，引入分布偏移正则化，以促进更细粒度的潜在表示学习。方法在2D障碍物Car Racing变体和Bipedal Walker变体上进行评估，两个环境均包含共同影响策略性能的耦合参数。

Result: 在五个随机种子下，该方法 consistently 优于普通策略训练、随机参数采样、人工课程、frontier-based方法、SPRL、ALP-GMM和反向课程学习等基线。消融实验验证了重参数化课程机制在两个环境中的有效性，并表明辅助正则化的收益具有环境依赖性。

Conclusion: 重参数化课程生成能够有效提升连续控制策略的训练效率、鲁棒性和跨环境泛化能力；分布偏移正则化具有潜在价值，但其效果取决于具体环境。

Abstract: Robust navigation policies for autonomous agents must generalize across continuously varying environmental conditions such as turn rates, obstacles, friction, pits, and slopes. Curriculum generation provides a principled mechanism for improving generalization by progressively adapting training environments, but designing such curricula in a sample-efficient and automated manner remains challenging. This paper proposes a reparameterized curriculum generation framework for structured continuous environment parameters using unidirectional gradient-based optimization. To improve robustness in multimodal observation spaces consisting of image-based and scalar inputs, a distribution-shift regularization objective is incorporated to encourage the learning of finer-grained latent representations. The proposed method is evaluated across two continuous-control OpenAI Gym environments: a 2D obstacle-based Car Racing variant and Bipedal Walker variant, where coupled environment parameters jointly influence policy performance. Across five random seeds, our method consistently outperforms vanilla policy training, random parameter sampling, manual curricula, frontier-based methods, Self-Paced Reinforcement Learning (SPRL), Absolute Learning Progress with Gaussian Mixture Models (ALP-GMM), and reverse curriculum learning baselines. Ablation studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of the reparameterized curriculum mechanism across both environments, while highlighting environment-dependent benefits of the auxiliary regularization objective.

</details>


### [83] [Auditing Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches in Multimodal Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07895)
*Simon Holk,Ryosuke Takanami,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yueh-Hua Wu,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人视觉-语言-动作策略训练数据中的指令—轨迹错配（ITM）问题，并提出无需训练的多模态概率融合（MMPF）审计框架，用于检测错配并纠正错误标签。实验表明，MMPF在LIBERO基准和真实机器人噪声数据上取得了较强的检测与标签校正效果，并能改善下游策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人示范轨迹可能在行为上看似正确，但与配套语言指令不一致。这类隐蔽错误难以通过传统失败轨迹筛查发现，却会破坏语言与行为之间的对应关系，降低视觉-语言-动作策略的学习效果。

Method: MMPF将不同模态视为独立专家：一方面利用局部邻域一致性估计任务标签分布，另一方面利用全局原型相似度进行估计；随后依据预测熵对各模态进行加权，并通过乘积专家融合得到最终判断。该方法无需额外训练，并支持错配检测与指令标签纠正，同时比较过滤示范和重新标注两种数据清理策略。

Result: 在注入指令错配的LIBERO基准及含噪真实机器人数据上，MMPF取得了总体最优的ITM检测准确率和标签校正准确率。审计后的数据在语言对任务消歧作用较强的场景中，对下游策略学习的促进最明显。真实机器人实验进一步显示，该方法能够提升策略性能，并揭示了过滤示范与重新标注之间的性能权衡。

Conclusion: 指令—轨迹错配是机器人示范数据中容易被忽视但具有实质影响的数据质量问题。MMPF通过融合局部一致性、全局语义相似度和不确定性权重，实现了有效的训练后数据审计。结果支持在策略训练前进行系统化审计，但具体采用过滤还是重新标注，应根据任务对语言信息的依赖程度和数据修复成本进行选择。

Abstract: Robot demonstration datasets used to train vision-language-action policies can contain a subtle but harmful failure mode: trajectories that are behaviorally correct but paired with the wrong language instruction. We study post-hoc auditing of these Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches (ITMs). Unlike failed rollouts, ITMs often look plausible, and can corrupt the language-behavior mapping learned by the policy. We propose Multimodal Probabilistic Fusion (MMPF), a training-free auditing framework that treats each modality as an expert, estimates a task-label distribution from local neighborhood agreement and global prototype similarity, and then fuses modalities with predictive-entropy weighting in a product of experts. Across LIBERO benchmarks with injected instruction mismatches and noisy real-robot data, MMPF achieves the strongest overall ITM detection and label correction accuracy. We also show that auditing improves most downstream policy learning in settings where language is needed to disambiguate the task. We demonstrate in real robot experiments that our method can achieve improved policy performance and show the trade-off of filtering demonstrations compared to relabeling.

</details>


### [84] [CoCoNav: Conformal Control for Safe Robot Navigation in Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07751)
*Cheng Guo,Mingzhe Ni,Zheng Liang,Yihu Ling,Yuan Hu,Michele Caprio,Daniele Pucci,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoCoNav是一种面向人群环境的机器人安全导航框架，通过在线保序校准动态估计行人预测误差，并结合“先松弛、后验证”的规划机制，在保证求解可行性的同时提升碰撞规避、任务成功率与导航效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人运动预测存在不确定性和随时间变化的误差。传统反应式方法可能产生振荡，预测式规划又常将预测视为精确结果或依赖严格的误差模型；若直接把保守不确定性集合作为硬约束，还可能导致模型预测控制不可行。因此，需要一种能够适应误差变化、兼顾安全性与规划可行性的导航方法。

Method: CoCoNav包含两个核心组件：一是面向不同预测时域的保序比例—积分控制器，根据在线观测到的轨迹误差调整误差界限，以维持长期经验覆盖率；二是“先松弛、后验证”规划器，先利用软约束模型预测控制生成名义轨迹，再对名义轨迹及备用机动动作相对于校准后的不确定性边界进行运行时安全认证，确认后才执行。

Result: 仿真与四足机器人实验表明，相较于所评估的基线方法，CoCoNav在碰撞规避、任务完成和导航效率之间取得了更优的综合平衡，说明其能够适应变化中的预测误差并降低因保守硬约束造成的规划失败风险。

Conclusion: 该工作将在线不确定性校准与运行时安全验证结合起来，为人群环境中的机器人预测导航提供了一种兼顾适应性、可行性和安全性的方案。其主要贡献在于不要求固定误差模型，并通过独立验证机制避免将安全约束直接施加为可能导致不可行的硬约束。

Abstract: Safe and efficient robot navigation in crowds requires anticipating pedestrian motion despite uncertain and potentially shifting prediction errors. Existing reactive methods can produce oscillatory behavior, while predictive planners often treat forecasts as exact or rely on restrictive error models. Incorporating conservative uncertainty sets as hard constraints can also render model predictive control (MPC) infeasible. We propose \textit{CoCoNav}, a crowd-navigation framework that combines online conformal calibration with runtime-certified planning. A horizon-specific conformal proportional--integral controller adapts trajectory-error bounds to regulate long-run empirical coverage, enabling the framework to respond to changing prediction errors. A \textit{relax-then-verify} planner preserves solver feasibility by generating nominal trajectories with soft-constrained MPC and separately certifying them, together with contingency maneuvers, against the calibrated bounds before execution. Simulations and quadruped experiments show that CoCoNav achieves a favorable balance among collision avoidance, task success, and navigation efficiency relative to the evaluated baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [verdi: retrieval is not transfer for continual world model optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09537)
*Junyu Wu,Shiqin Nie,Youyi Kou,Baohua Yin,Guocai Yao,Qingyu Chen,Jingheng Ma,Shiji Zhou,Hongyong Song,Mingchen Zhuge,Sen Cui,Changshui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VERDI是一种面向预训练世界模型持续优化的证据许可框架，通过模型指纹、经验检索和目标侧验证，降低优化搜索与计算成本，并显著减少负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型优化通常为每个新模型从头寻找策略，且将已验证策略直接迁移到其他模型，缺乏判断迁移适用性的原则，导致知识复用不足和负迁移。论文强调“检索不等于迁移”：源模型上的成功策略只能作为目标模型上的优化假设，必须经过目标侧实验验证后才能成为可复用知识。

Method: VERDI首先通过统一的推理时探针对世界模型进行表征，构建Optimization Fingerprint；随后根据指纹检索并排序历史经验，将其作为候选优化假设，而非直接方案。每个候选方案都在冻结的目标侧验证器下进行实验验证，只有通过验证才被纳入可复用证据库。当相近指纹之间出现结果矛盾时，框架会触发探针演化，持续改进诊断表示。

Result: 在Ctrl-World、Cosmos系列和RoboCoin上的实验表明，VERDI将搜索成本降低68%，GPU成本降低69%，将负迁移率从0.34降至0.06，并以83%的符号准确率预测迁移结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将世界模型优化中的经验复用从直接配方迁移转变为基于证据的假设验证，兼顾了优化效率、迁移可靠性和诊断表示的持续改进。实验结果支持其在降低成本和抑制负迁移方面的有效性，但其性能仍依赖指纹探针、验证器设计以及实验任务覆盖范围。

Abstract: Foundation world models have made remarkable progress in planning, simulation, and embodied intelligence. However, optimizing a pretrained world model toward a user-specified objective remains difficult: each campaign typically rediscovers optimization strategies from scratch, and the resulting knowledge rarely transfers to the next model. Existing research agents automate the optimization loop but treat successful strategies as directly reusable recipes, without principled safeguards for when transfer is appropriate. We argue instead that retrieval is not transfer: a strategy validated on one model is at best an optimization hypothesis for another, and becomes transferable knowledge only after target-side experimental valida- tion. Guided by this principle, we propose VERDI , a continual framework for evidence-licensed world model optimization. VERDI characterizes each world model through shared inference-time probes to construct an Optimization Fin- gerprint, retrieves relevant prior experience as ranked hypotheses, and validates every candidate under a frozen target-side verifier before admitting it as reusable evidence; contradictions among nearby fingerprints further trigger probe evolution, continually refining the diagnostic representation itself. Experiments on Ctrl-World, the Cosmos family, and RoboCoin show that VERDI reduces search cost by 68%, GPU cost by 69%, and negative transfer from 0.34 to 0.06, while predicting transfer outcomes with 83% sign accuracy.

</details>


### [86] [LLM-Guided Heuristic Design from Simulation Traces: A Case Study in Dynamic Production and AGV Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09343)
*Jinbo Li,Chuanhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合大语言模型、重复仿真与事件级轨迹诊断的仿真优化框架，用于自动发现调度策略瓶颈并进行代码级改进。在动态生产与AGV调度仿真中，该方法显著优于滚动MILP、规则法和元启发式基线，并在故障及不同运行条件下保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统仿真优化通常将模拟器视为黑箱，仅通过聚合得分比较候选策略，难以解释策略失败原因，也难以指导具体逻辑修改。本文旨在利用低分运行的事件级轨迹，为策略改进提供可查询、可解释的诊断依据。

Method: 框架通过多次随机复现实验评估当前策略，并回放得分最低的一次运行以生成事件级轨迹。管理代理基于轨迹提出瓶颈假设，多个编辑代理并行生成代码级策略修订；系统执行代码检查并重新仿真，仅保留优于当前最优解的版本。大语言模型在评估批次之间进行修订，而单次仿真过程中的策略保持固定。实验场景为动态生产与自动导引车调度，并使用多次独立优化、匹配随机种子、随机故障、延长规划时域、变化订单到达间隔及消融实验进行验证。

Result: 使用Gemini-3.1-Pro进行五次独立优化后，最终平均得分为77.51（0—100分）。最高分运行中，基于轨迹的诊断促成主动充电、距离感知的AGV分配和重新平衡的派工优先级，使最优平均得分由62.49提升至78.61。在100个匹配随机种子上，最终策略逐种子优于滚动MILP、规则法和元启发式策略；在随机故障下无需重新优化仍保持优势。针对更长时域和可变订单到达间隔重新优化后，所得策略再次优于所有基线。消融实验表明，移除并行候选生成或轨迹数据库访问都会降低最终平均得分。

Conclusion: 事件级仿真轨迹能够将黑箱式性能评价转化为针对具体失败机制的诊断，并有效指导大语言模型进行代码级启发式改进。该框架在复杂调度任务中表现出较强的性能提升、鲁棒性和跨场景适应能力，但其效果仍可能依赖于大语言模型、轨迹质量及候选修订机制。

Abstract: Simulation-based optimization (SBO) evaluates executable policies under stochastic dynamics, but most methods treat the simulator as a black box: aggregate scores rank candidates without revealing why they fail or which policy logic should change. We present an LLM-guided heuristic design framework that uses repeated simulation for selection and event-level traces for diagnosis. Each incumbent is assessed through multiple replications, while replaying its lowest-scoring one produces a queryable trace. A manager agent formulates bottleneck hypotheses from this evidence, and editing agents implement parallel code-level revisions. After execution checks and repeated evaluation, best-so-far selection retains only improvements. LLM revision occurs between evaluation batches, while a fixed policy controls each simulation run. We evaluate the framework in a discrete-event simulation of dynamic production and automated guided vehicle (AGV) scheduling. Across five independent optimization runs with Gemini-3.1-Pro, final mean scores averaged 77.51 on the simulator's 0-100 scale. In the highest-scoring run, trace-based diagnoses motivated proactive charging, distance-aware AGV assignment, and rebalanced dispatch priorities, raising the best-so-far mean score from 62.49 to 78.61. On 100 matched seeds, the best final policy outscored representative rolling-MILP, rule-based, and metaheuristic policies on every seed and retained its advantage under random faults without re-optimization. After separate re-optimization for a longer horizon and variable order interarrival times, the resulting policies again outscored all baselines. Ablations with two LLM backbones showed that removing either parallel candidate generation or trace-database access reduced final mean scores. These results show that simulation traces can guide targeted code-level policy improvement in complex simulation-based scheduling.

</details>


### [87] [Emotion2Skill: Model-Internal Emotion Signals for Adaptive Skill Selection and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09248)
*Bohan Lin,Hejia Geng,Xinyi Xie,Heng Zhou,Qinghua Xing,Bo Liu,Chen Zhang,Yudong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Emotion2Skill从大语言模型残差流中提取27维内部情绪状态，并将其用于技能选择与技能演化。在WebShop和ALFWorld上，该方法显著提升任务成功率，表明内部表征可作为智能体路由的有效决策信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于技能的LLM智能体主要依赖任务文本、语言反思和经验规则进行技能路由，忽略了模型内部表征。虽然已有研究发现情绪表征能够因果影响模型行为，但其应用主要停留在事后解释或输出控制层面，尚未用于智能体级决策与技能维护。

Method: 在每个决策步骤，从模型残差流中提取27维情绪状态，并通过置信度门控生成摘要，注入技能路由提示以辅助技能选择。同时分析情绪轨迹中的突变，定位可能存在问题的技能调用，并据此进行有针对性的SOP重写，以替代传统较粗粒度的二元结果反馈。研究还通过情绪—技能共激活分析检验信号的语义一致性。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，相比Zero-Shot基线，WebShop成功率提升26.9%，ALFWorld平均成功率提升25.5%，并在两个基准上超过所有比较方法。使用Qwen3-14B时也观察到稳定增益。共激活分析显示情绪与技能之间存在语义一致的配对关系。

Conclusion: LLM内部情绪表征不仅可用于解释模型行为或直接控制输出，还能有效支持技能级路由和技能演化。Emotion2Skill验证了内部状态作为智能体决策信号的可行性，但其有效性仍依赖情绪向量提取、跨模型泛化及提示注入机制的可靠性。

Abstract: Skill-based LLM agents select reusable procedures from an external library to solve complex tasks, yet their routing decisions rely entirely on text-level signals such as task descriptions, verbal reflections, and experience-derived rules, while the model's own internal representational state remains unobserved. Recent interpretability work has shown that LLMs maintain linear emotion representations that causally influence behavior; however, these representations have been exploited only for post-hoc analysis or direct output steering, and have not been used to inform agent-level decision-making. We propose Emotion2Skill, a framework that extracts LLM-internal emotion vectors and incorporates them into both skill selection and skill evolution. At each decision step, a 27-dimensional emotion state is extracted from the residual stream and mapped to a confidence-gated summary injected into the routing prompt. Beyond online selection, emotion trajectories are analyzed for abrupt internal-state shifts to pinpoint problematic skill invocations, guiding targeted SOP rewriting that replaces the coarse binary outcome signal of prior methods. On WebShop and ALFWorld, Emotion2Skill with Qwen3-8B improves over the Zero-Shot baseline by +26.9% success rate and +25.5% average success respectively, outperforming all baselines on both benchmarks with consistent gains on Qwen3-14B. Co-activation analysis further reveals semantically coherent emotion--skill pairings, confirming that the routing improvements reflect meaningful internal-state signals rather than opaque statistical correlations. These results establish LLM-internal emotion representations as an effective decision-level signal for orchestrating agent skill systems, extending their utility beyond interpretability and output steering. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [88] [CRUISE: Vision-Language Model-Guided Uncertainty-Aware Cross-Modal Sensor Fusion for Robust Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09202)
*Junyao Wang,Yulin Xu,Yu Li,Pramod Khargonekar,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CRUISE，一种由视觉语言模型引导、具备像素级不确定性估计和动态跨模态依赖建模能力的多传感器融合框架，用于提升自动驾驶感知在复杂及分布外场景中的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摄像头、LiDAR和雷达在恶劣天气、低能见度等条件下的可靠性差异显著，传统基于简单特征级不确定性估计的融合方法难以准确判断各模态信息的可信度，并且在复杂分布外场景中泛化能力有限。因此，需要更细粒度、更具上下文感知能力的不确定性建模与自适应融合机制。

Method: CRUISE包含两个核心设计：首先，引入视觉语言模型引导的不确定性量化模块，利用视觉语言模型的先验知识和上下文推理能力生成像素级、细粒度的不确定性估计；其次，设计动态自适应机制，显式建模不同传感器模态之间的依赖关系，并据此动态调节跨模态信息融合，以充分利用各传感器的互补性。

Result: 摘要仅描述了方法设计与预期优势，未提供具体实验数据、数据集、评价指标或与基线方法的定量比较。因此，目前只能判断该方法旨在提升复杂环境和分布外场景下的融合鲁棒性，无法据此确认实际性能增益。

Conclusion: CRUISE通过视觉语言模型驱动的像素级不确定性估计和动态跨模态依赖建模，针对现有不确定性感知融合方法粒度不足、泛化能力有限的问题提出了系统性改进。其潜在价值在于实现更可靠的模态选择与信息互补，但仍需通过完整实验验证有效性、计算开销及实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Modern autonomous vehicles are equipped with multiple sensors, such as cameras, LiDAR, and radar, for comprehensive environmental perception. However, robust cross-modal feature fusion remains a critical challenge, as the reliability of each sensor varies significantly across diverse real-world driving conditions, including poor visibility and adverse weather. While uncertainty quantification (UQ) mitigates this issue by allowing models to prioritize reliable signals, existing uncertainty-aware fusion methods typically rely on simple feature-level uncertainty estimates and thus often fail to generalize effectively in complex, out-of-distribution scenarios. To address this limitation, we propose CRUISE, a novel uncertainty-aware cross-modal sensor fusion framework. CRUISE integrates a vision-language model (VLM)-guided UQ module that generates fine-grained, pixel-level uncertainty estimates. By leveraging the VLM's rich prior knowledge and superior contextual reasoning, our approach provides a highly informative guide for the fusion process. Furthermore, we introduce a dynamic adaptive mechanism that explicitly models and captures cross-modal dependencies, ensuring the framework fully exploits the inherent complementary nature of multi-sensor inputs.

</details>


### [89] [Discovering Diverse Planning Policies for Multimodal Embodied Agents with Quality-Diversity Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08523)
*Pengfei Xu,Yong Liu,Xiaoya Nan,Qiang Yang,Peilan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多模态具身智能体的质量多样性（Quality-Diversity，QD）规划框架，通过离线发现并维护多种行为互补的规划策略，在在线执行陷入停滞时回滚并切换策略，从而提升长时程任务的成功率与交互效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大模型的规划器通常依赖单一主导规划风格。当该风格在执行过程中失效时，智能体可能持续进行低效交互而无法取得进展。因此，需要能够发现、保存并动态切换多种规划策略的机制，以增强任务适应性和失败恢复能力。

Method: 将规划策略模板视为可进化个体，并构建基于行为特征的策略档案库。离线阶段利用轨迹提取结构化成功与失败经验，通过重组和经验引导变异生成新策略；再依据交互强度和目标导向性构建行为空间，并在各行为生态位中保留质量最高的策略。在线阶段逐步执行策略并监测任务进展；检测到持续停滞后，回滚至最近检查点，并切换到行为上具有差异的档案策略继续执行。

Result: 在ThreeDWorld transport基准上的实验表明，该框架相较代表性基线规划器同时提升了任务成功率和交互效率，说明多样化策略档案能够有效支持规划适应与在线恢复。摘要未提供具体提升幅度、统计显著性或消融实验结果。

Conclusion: 通过质量多样性搜索维护规划策略的行为多样性，可以缓解单一规划模式失效导致的长期停滞问题。该方法为多模态具身任务提供了结合离线策略发现、在线停滞检测和检查点恢复的有效规划范式。

Abstract: Multimodal embodied agents are increasingly required to solve long-horizon tasks by integrating visual observations, textual goals, and interaction history into closed-loop decision making. However, state-of-the-art large-model-based planners often rely on a single dominant planning style during execution. Once this execution mode becomes ineffective, the agent may remain stalled for many steps, repeatedly interacting with the environment without making meaningful progress. We address this limitation by proposing a Quality-Diversity (QD) framework for discovering diverse planning policies for multimodal embodied agents. The proposed method treats planning-policy templates as evolvable individuals and organizes them into a behavior-indexed archive rather than collapsing search to a single prompt style. In the offline stage, rollout trajectories are summarized into structured success and failure experiences, which guide policy variation through recombination and experience-guided mutation. The resulting policies are mapped into a behavior space defined by interaction intensity and goal-directedness, and the highest-quality policy in each niche is retained in the archive. In the online stage, the agent executes one policy at a time while monitoring task progress. When persistent stall is detected, the system rolls back to the latest checkpoint and switches to a behaviorally distinct archive policy to resume execution. Experiments on the ThreeDWorld transport benchmark show that the proposed framework improves both task success and interaction efficiency over representative baseline planners. These results suggest that discovering diverse policy repertoires is an effective way to support adaptive multimodal planning and online failure recovery.

</details>


### [90] [TrustRoboReward: Preference-Ordered Isotonic Score Editing for Multi-Paradigm Robot Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08491)
*Yidong Wang,Yan Zhan,Ziteng Feng,Zhenyu Cui,Ziyi Zhou,Renzhao Liang,Jiaxuan Zhu,Zilei Yang,Yiran Zhao,Zhongkuan Mao,Bo Jia,Hanchu Ni,Chenggang Xie,Biao Liu,Yi Zhang,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju,Wei Ye,Shikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustRoboReward及POISE校准方法，解决具身智能长时程机器人操作中点式评分与成对偏好不一致的问题。Qwen3-VL-4B经训练后总体奖励得分达77.96%，接近GPT-5-mini，并显著优于RoboReward基线；结合TrustJudge推理聚合后得分提升至78.57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型奖励模型主要依赖1—5分轨迹进度评分，缺乏适用于RLHF、DPO和Bradley-Terry框架的成对偏好数据，也未充分优化视频场景理解。直接加入成对比较和视频问答监督会造成点式评分与偏好标签冲突，增加训练噪声并损害下游性能。

Method: 提出TrustRoboReward多范式奖励建模框架，并构建包含四类监督的数据集：轨迹进度点式评分Score-A、视频问答质量点式评分Score-B，以及对应的Pair-A和Pair-B成对偏好标签。核心方法POISE通过偏好有序保序回归式的分数编辑，校准点式评分，使其与成对偏好保持一致，消除跨范式的评分反转冲突；推理阶段可进一步结合TrustJudge进行聚合。

Result: 理论分析表明，POISE将评分对反转冲突率从20.15%降至0%，而TrustJudge仍保留20.46%的冲突。Qwen3-VL-4B结合POISE后总体奖励得分为77.96%，较最强RoboReward-4B基线提升10.13%，与GPT-5-mini的差距仅0.13个百分点。测试时评分对一致性达到71.90%，高于RoboReward-4B的57.26%和GPT-5-mini的68.09%；加入TrustJudge聚合后总体得分升至78.57%。

Conclusion: POISE有效解决了多种奖励监督之间的排序不一致问题，提升了奖励模型的偏好一致性与总体性能。TrustRoboReward在较小规模视觉语言模型上接近或超过强教师模型，表明偏好约束下的点式评分校准是构建可扩展具身智能奖励模型的有效路径。

Abstract: Reward models are a bottleneck for reinforcement learning in embodied AI. Long-horizon robotic manipulation requires scalable vision feedback beyond handcrafted rewards or task-specific annotations. Existing open-source VLM reward judges like RoboReward adopt simple 1--5 trajectory progress scoring, lacking pairwise preferences for RLHF, DPO and Bradley-Terry frameworks, while failing to optimize video scene understanding. Augmenting RoboReward with pairwise comparison and video-QA supervision causes inconsistency between pairwise preferences and pointwise scores, introducing training noise and hurting downstream performance---an issue aggregation methods such as TrustJudge cannot resolve. To address this, we propose TrustRoboReward, a multi-paradigm reward modeling framework equipped with Preference-Ordered Isotonic Score Editing (POISE). We construct a unified four-paradigm dataset with trajectory progress scoring (Score-A), video-QA answer quality scoring (Score-B), and their pairwise counterparts (Pair-A, Pair-B). Pairwise labels align better with human judgment than pointwise scores, inspiring us to calibrate pointwise scores to avoid score-pair reversals against pairwise preferences. POISE rectifies pointwise scores and eliminates cross-paradigm reversal conflicts unresolved by TrustJudge. Theoretically, POISE reduces score-pair reversal conflicts from 20.15% to 0%, whereas TrustJudge retains 20.46% conflicts on the same corpus. Evaluated on our benchmark, Qwen3-VL-4B trained with POISE achieves an overall reward score of 77.96%, nearly matching GPT-5-mini (78.09%, gap 0.13%) and outperforming the strongest RoboReward-4B baseline by 10.13%. It also lifts test-time score-pair consistency to 71.90%, exceeding RoboReward-4B (57.26%) and GPT-5-mini (68.09%). Integrating TrustJudge aggregation during inference boosts the overall score to 78.57%, surpassing the GPT-5-mini teacher model.

</details>


### [91] [Exploring LLM Capabilities for Situational Understanding and COLREG compliance on real-world maritime navigation scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08281)
*Julius Wirbel,P. Nicholas Hansen,Line K. H. Clemmensen,Roberto Galeazzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究利用大语言模型辅助 maritime navigation，涵盖《国际海上避碰规则》（COLREGs）及“良好船艺”实践，并评估其场景理解与推理能力。结果表明，未经微调时，即使是较大的在线模型也难以可靠解决该任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在情境理解、推理和决策方面表现出较强能力，研究者希望检验其能否应用于海事导航这一同时包含成文规则与非成文操作经验的复杂领域。

Method: 基于自动识别系统（AIS）数据构建包含50个多样化真实导航场景的数据集，并为每个场景标注适用的COLREGs规则、推荐行动及行动理由。随后比较不同架构和规模的大语言模型，评估其海事导航理解与推理能力。

Result: 实验显示，海事导航任务对大语言模型仍具有较高难度。未经领域微调时，模型在规则适用判断、行动建议和理由推导方面的整体表现不足，模型规模增大也未能充分解决问题。

Conclusion: 通用大语言模型目前尚不能在海事导航中稳定替代专业决策系统。未来需要引入领域微调、结构化规则约束以及更大规模和更具代表性的数据，以提升其可靠性、可解释性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown considerable capability for situational understanding, reasoning, and decision making in different domains, most notable in the automotive sector. Therefore, we explore current state-of-the-art LLMs as a tool for maritime navigation, which includes both codified rules in the Collision Regulations (COLREGs) and uncodified best practices summarized in the concept of ``Good Seamanship''. We construct a dataset consisting of 50 diverse, real-world navigation scenarios from AIS data, label scenarios with applicable COLREG rules, recommended actions, and the reasoning for the action. We explore a variety of different LLM architectures and sizes to determine their understanding of maritime navigation tasks as well as evaluate their reasoning capabilities in this domain. The results obtained indicate that the maritime navigation task remains difficult to solve without fine-tuning, even for larger online models.

</details>


### [92] [Large Multimodal Agents for Intelligent Transportation Systems: Architectures, Evidence, and Deployment Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08184)
*Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Shaohong Zheng,Zhiyu Qu,Fatima Ashraf,Junbiao Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述系统评估了2023年1月至2026年8月3日期间91个来源中的42个大型多模态智能体研究家族，发现其在交通语义理解与任务编排方面证据较强，但部署成熟度和可审计性不足，现阶段更适合受约束的协同编排，而非替代专业系统或人工决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能交通系统研究常将多模态能力、智能体自主性、实证性能和部署准备度混为一谈，导致研究能力与实际可用性之间缺乏清晰区分。因此，该综述旨在建立可审计的证据地图和统一评估框架。

Method: 研究对42个主要研究家族进行分类与证据评估，区分模型级、系统级和混合多模态，按照系统架构与行动权限进行归类；同时采用功能能力等级C0-C3、验证环境等级E0-E4、证据命题P1-P3及八个方法学问题域Q1-Q8开展独立评估，并提出匹配式比较协议和分阶段部署路线图。
