Result: 23个研究家族直接评估交通语义理解，24个评估多维度整合，其中19个同时覆盖两者。证据链完整性命题P2尚未得到解决，因为没有研究完整展示来源、质疑、处理、比较到结果的可追溯链条。14个家族达到C3，但其中13个仅达到E2；只有1个达到E3，没有达到E4的研究。LMAs在语义解释、意图转换、证据组织、场景生成、解释和专业工具协调方面支持最充分，而在数值预测、优化、仿真保真度、硬约束、底层控制和安全回退方面证据不足。

Conclusion: 当前大型多模态智能体在智能交通领域应定位为受约束的编排与辅助系统，而非独立替代者。数值计算、约束满足、低层控制和最终决策权应继续由可独立验证的专业系统或负责任的人类承担；未来需要加强证据溯源、可复现验证、跨场景评测和分阶段问责式部署。

Abstract: Large multimodal agents (LMAs) are increasingly proposed for intelligent transportation systems (ITS), but existing studies often conflate multimodality, agency, empirical performance, and deployment readiness. This review provides an auditable evidence map of 42 primary study families released between January 2023 and 3 August 2026 within a corpus of 91 mapped sources. It distinguishes model-level, system-level, and hybrid multimodality and classifies each family by system architecture and action authority. Evidence is assessed independently through functional capability (C0-C3), validation setting (E0-E4), three evidence propositions (P1-P3), and eight methodological-concern domains (Q1-Q8). Transportation semantics (P1) are directly evaluated in 23 families and multidimensional integration (P3) in 24; 19 families directly evaluate both. Evidence reconciliation (P2) remains unresolved because no family demonstrates the complete provenance-challenge-handling-comparison-outcome chain. Fourteen families reach C3, but 13 remain at E2; only one reaches E3 and none reaches E4. Across ITS domains, LMAs are best supported for semantic interpretation, intent translation, evidence organisation, scenario authoring, explanation, and specialist-tool coordination. Numerical forecasting, optimisation, simulation fidelity, hard constraints, low-level control, safety fallback, and final authority should remain with independently verifiable specialist systems or accountable humans. The review therefore supports bounded orchestration rather than replacement and provides a matched comparative evaluation protocol and staged roadmap for accountable deployment. The living evidence repository is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [93] [Explore, Map, Remember, Decide: Are Embodied VLMs Ready for Safety-Critical Scenarios?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08077)
*Gabriele La Malfa,Nitay Alon,Emanuele La Malfa,Reuth Mirsky,Stefan Sarkadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EMRD框架，将Theory of Space扩展至安全关键、目标驱动场景，用于系统评估视觉语言模型在部分可观测环境中的探索、空间建图、记忆与决策能力。结果显示，模型常受文本先验驱动而非物理证据驱动，空间推理在低光照下明显退化，且其记忆机制与人类认知存在根本差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉语言模型应用于安全关键任务，有必要检验其是否具备可靠的空间记忆、基于环境证据进行决策的能力，以及在环境干扰和潜在风险下的稳健性。研究重点是识别视觉—语言偏差、空间失配和不可预测的决策风险。

Method: 研究将Theory of Space框架扩展为EMRD流程：Explore评估环境覆盖率与时间效率；Map测量空间表征的保真度；Remember采用多项心理学指标评估记忆持久性；Decide利用焦点指标分析认知决策。实验还比较了低光照、纹理篡改和颜色篡改等环境条件下的表现。

Result: 视觉语言模型经常依据预训练文本先验选择疏散点，缺乏足够的空间证据支持。其空间推理能力在低光照条件下下降，但对纹理和颜色篡改相对不敏感。模型记忆表现与人类认知模式存在明显差异。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型的空间理解、记忆和决策尚不足以支持高度可靠的安全关键应用。文本先验偏差、空间 grounding不足以及与人类认知不一致的记忆机制，可能导致难以预测的失配风险；EMRD可作为评估和诊断此类问题的系统化工具。

Abstract: Theory of Space framework (ToS) assesses the spatial understanding of curiosity-driven Vision-Language Models (VLMs) under partial observability. As AI techniques are increasingly applied to safety-critical scenarios, it is crucial to understand whether VLMs possess robust spatial memory and make reliable decisions. In this paper, we assess whether VLMs' decisions are based on physical evidence or are corrupted by visual-language biases, if their memory processes align with human cognitive patterns, and how they respond to environmental hazards. We extend the ToS framework into a safety-critical, goal-driven pipeline, named Explore, Map, Remember, and Decide (EMRD). We then quantify Exploration Competence (Explore) through metrics of environmental coverage and temporal efficiency, assess Spatial Fidelity (Map), evaluate, with a suite of psychological metrics, Memory Persistence (Remember), and measure, using focal-point metrics, Cognitive Decision-Making (Decide). Our results show that in terms of decision-making capabilities, VLMs frequently select evacuation points based on pre-trained textual priors while lacking the spatial grounding to justify their choices. We also show that spatial reasoning degrades in low-light conditions, but it is not affected by texture and colour tampering. Our findings suggest that VLM memory fundamentally diverges from human cognition, creating unpredictable risks of misalignment.

</details>


### [94] [Lingjing: A Simulation Testbed for Multi-Agent Embodied Tasks in Open-Ended Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08045)
*Xiaohe Li,Yiru Wang,Junhao Fan,Mingyuan Liu,Jie Huang,Kaixin Zhang,Jiahao Li,Chen Qian,Zide Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lingjing是面向开放城市环境的异构多智能体具身智能仿真平台，统一支持城市重建、多物理引擎、自然语言任务、通信约束、资源管理、端到端评测与可归因失败诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真平台通常将不同类型的智能体彼此隔离，并将智能体、任务设计与评测流程分离，难以系统研究无人机、地面机器人和自动驾驶车辆在动态城市中的协同能力，也缺乏可复现的失败分析机制。

Method: 平台基于地理数据重建并渲染动态城市，同步多个物理引擎，并向智能体提供共享的物理状态与结构化城市状态。其Gym风格接口支持用户定义的ReAct智能体、单智能体和多智能体自然语言任务，以及星型或广播式通信和资源约束。每个任务回合均生成可归因回放，将智能体轨迹与通信记录关联到关系图变化、资源消耗和引擎评测结果。实验采用统一的引擎在环协议，在九类城市任务上评估十二个视觉语言模型，并开展通信、可扩展性、鲁棒性和失败来源分析。

Result: 实验发现，模型在环境状态 grounding 和长时程执行方面存在持续瓶颈。协同效果具有明显的任务依赖性，通信与协调机制之间存在权衡；增加模型容量的收益逐渐递减，而更高的工作负载会进一步降低任务成功率。平台能够追踪轨迹、通信、资源和关系图变化，从而支持系统化失败诊断。

Conclusion: Lingjing构建了一个统一、可复现且可扩展的城市异构多智能体具身智能测试平台，弥合了环境仿真、任务交互、通信协同和评测诊断之间的割裂，为端到端能力比较及失败机制研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Urban embodied intelligence requires coordination among heterogeneous agents (e.g., UAVs, ground robots, and autonomous vehicles) in dynamic cities. Simulators therefore provide a scalable foundation for developing and evaluating such coordination. Existing platforms nevertheless isolate different embodiments and decouple them from task design and evaluation. We present \textbf{Lingjing}, a simulation platform for heterogeneous multi-agent embodied intelligence in open-ended urban environments. Lingjing reconstructs and renders evolving cities from geographic data, synchronizes multiple physics engines, and exposes shared physical and structured urban state to agents. Its Gym-like interface supports user-defined ReAct agents and single- or multi-agent natural-language missions with configurable star or broadcast communication and resource constraints. Each episode becomes an attribution-ready replay that links agent trajectories and communication to relation-graph changes, resource consumption, and engine-based evaluations for systematic diagnosis. We evaluate twelve vision-language models on nine urban tasks under a shared engine-in-the-loop protocol. Controlled studies further examine communication, scalability, robustness, and failure provenance. Results expose persistent bottlenecks in grounding and long-horizon execution. They also show task-dependent coordination trade-offs and diminishing returns from added capacity, while heavier workloads further reduce success. Lingjing provides a unified testbed that enables reproducible end-to-end evaluation and systematic failure diagnosis in urban multi-agent embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [95] [GraphThink: Graph-Enhanced LLM Thinking for Long-Horizon Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07905)
*Chen Li,Sijie Cheng,Yuelin Zhang,Junxi Li,Maozhi Huang,Yang Liu,Wenbing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphThink通过任务图与场景图协同增强基于大语言模型的具身智能规划：任务图负责结构化任务知识、引导推理并改进奖励设计，场景图负责环境记忆与事件驱动重规划，从而缓解物理幻觉、长时域规划困难和环境感知不足。在ALFRED基准上取得了当前最佳表现，并展现出较强的零样本、少样本及分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的具身智能体存在三类主要问题：容易产生不符合物理环境的规划幻觉；面对长时域任务时规划稳定性和泛化能力不足；缺乏持续的环境记忆与闭环纠错机制。因此，需要同时提升任务规划的结构化程度和执行过程中的环境感知能力。

Method: GraphThink包含两个核心图结构与一个训练机制。任务图提供结构化任务知识，通过上下文提示和迭代 refinement 引导大语言模型进行规划，并在GRPO框架下支持更细粒度的奖励设计，以提升长时域规划和泛化能力。场景图用于维护环境状态与历史事件，并通过事件驱动的重规划实现闭环环境感知和执行错误修正。

Result: 在ALFRED基准测试中，GraphThink取得了当前最佳性能。其高层规划器在验证集和未见过的长时域任务上均超过领先的基于API的大语言模型，显示出较强的零样本和少样本能力。额外实验表明，该方法对新任务和新环境具有良好的分布外泛化能力。摘要未提供具体数值、基线名称或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: 将任务图用于结构化规划与奖励塑形、将场景图用于环境记忆和事件驱动重规划，能够有效增强大语言模型具身智能体的规划可靠性、长时域能力及环境适应性。GraphThink的实验结果支持其作为通用具身规划框架的有效性，但仍需通过更详细的消融实验、定量指标和真实机器人验证进一步确认各模块的独立贡献与实际可迁移性。

Abstract: Embodied agents using LLM-based planners often struggle with physical hallucinations, poor generalization to long-horizon tasks, and lack of environmental awareness. We propose GraphThink, a novel framework that integrates a task graph to provide structured knowledge for robust planning and a scene graph to maintain environmental memory for event-driven replanning. Specifically, the task graph guides LLM thinking through contextual prompting and iterative refinement, effectively mitigating planning hallucinations. Furthermore, within the GRPO framework, the task graph offers delicate reward design to train the LLM planner, enhancing long-horizon planning capabilities and improving generalization. Finally, an event-driven replanning module, powered by the scene graph, enables closed-loop environment awareness and error correction. GraphThink achieves state-of-the-art performance on the ALFRED benchmark. In particular, our high-level planner surpasses leading API-based LLMs on both the validation set and held-out long-horizon tasks, underscoring its robust zero-shot and few-shot capabilities. Additional evaluations further demonstrate strong out-of-distribution generalization to novel tasks and environments.

</details>


### [96] [SurgLAT: Surgical Latent Attention Tracking for Depth-Aware Robotic Laparoscope Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07876)
*Rulin Zhou,Qiujie Song,Yujie Ma,An Wang,Wanhao Liu,Guoheng Ma,Yidu Wang,Guankun Wang,Xingrong Diao,Jiankun Wang,Chaowei Zhu,Xianming Liu,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SurgLAT，一个面向自主腹腔镜视野控制的因果在线框架，通过建模随时间演化的潜在手术注意状态，实现手术区域跟踪与机器人镜头调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹腔镜手术中的目标区域并非稳定的实体，而是受术者意图、遮挡、快速运动和操作阶段影响的动态注意状态。传统基于固定目标或单帧视觉的方法难以持续理解手术意图并稳定控制镜头，因此需要联合建模短期运动连续性与长期意图演化。

Method: SurgLAT采用冻结的DINOv3编码器提取视觉特征，并利用状态条件空间token混合器和记忆引导的空间先验聚合手术证据。选择性因果潜在记忆模块动态检索当前、近期及历史潜在状态，以建模多时间尺度的注意演化。潜在注意状态进一步解码为概率注意力热图和手术区域，用于内窥镜引导。机器人部署方面，系统采用基于虚拟轴的腹腔镜远程运动中心约束控制，并结合冗余感知的零空间初始化，以提高机械臂运动的稳定性和平滑性。

Result: 在真实腹腔镜手术视频和实体机器人腹腔镜平台上的实验表明，系统能够在遮挡、快速运动及目标切换场景下进行稳健的在线手术区域跟踪，并实现稳定的自主内窥镜视野调整。

Conclusion: 该工作将潜在手术意图建模与满足RCM约束的机器人控制结合起来，形成了从视觉感知到自主镜头操控的完整方案。结果表明，多时间尺度潜在记忆有助于提升动态手术场景中的跟踪鲁棒性和自主内窥镜控制能力，但摘要未提供具体数据、基线比较或统计显著性信息。

Abstract: Autonomous laparoscopic camera control requires continuous understanding of the surgeon's operative intent in dynamic surgical scenes, where the target operative region is not a stable physical object but a latent and temporally evolving attention state. In this work, we present Surgical Latent Attention Tracking (SurgLAT), a causal online framework for latent surgical attention modeling and autonomous laparoscopic view control. SurgLAT uses a frozen DINOv3 encoder and a state-conditioned spatial token mixer to extract operative evidence under a memory-guided spatial prior, while a selective causal latent memory module jointly models short-term motion continuity and long-horizon surgical intent evolution through dynamic retrieval of current, recent, and historical latent states. The learned latent surgical attention state is decoded into a probabilistic attention heatmap and operative region for downstream endoscope guidance. Beyond perception, we further introduce a robotic deployment framework with explicit laparoscopic Remote Center of Motion (RCM) constrained control based on virtual-axis formulation, together with redundancy-aware null-space initialization for stable and smooth manipulator motion. We validate the full system on real laparoscopic surgical videos and a physical robotic laparoscope platform. Experimental results demonstrate robust online operative-region tracking and stable autonomous endoscopy adjustment under occlusion, rapid motion, and target transitions, highlighting the effectiveness of latent surgical intent modeling for surgical autonomy.

</details>


### [97] [CausalNav: Reliability-Certified Causal World Models for Control under Physical-Parameter Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07809)
*Yiyao Zhang,Diksha Goel,Hussain Ahmad,Shixun Huang,Jun Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CausalNav是一种带有可靠性认证和拒答机制的因果世界模型控制器。在CartPole-v1和离散化Pendulum-v1上的实验表明，模型结构恢复能力并不等同于控制收益；安全拒答机制比单纯提升预测精度更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理智能中的世界模型必须同时满足两点：能够改变智能体行为，并在预测不可靠时避免误导控制。现有研究通常强调模型保真度，但较少验证结构准确性是否真正转化为控制效用及部署安全性。

Method: CausalNav基于带符号、动作条件化的状态转移图构建控制器。部署时，它模拟少量干预序列，将目标误差转换为策略logit建议；只有在无量纲预测可靠性证书、策略间隔门控和动作最大值一致性门控同时通过时，才采用该建议，否则严格回退到自身的基于模型的基础控制器。实验在CartPole-v1和离散化Pendulum-v1上进行，包含物理参数变化；使用统一PPO训练器、相同交互预算、9个受控基线和10个留出随机种子，共200次运行。

Result: CausalNav取得十个方法中最佳平均排名，平均名次为1.25。学习到的图在CartPole上的结构恢复能力明显高于随机水平，F1为0.59±0.09；但逐种子的结构保真度与控制收益无显著相关性，相关系数r=-0.15，p=0.67。在Pendulum的10个种子上，可靠性证书全部拒绝规划建议；强制启用规划器反而降低回报。

Conclusion: 在该实验设置下，世界模型的结构或预测保真度不能可靠预测其下游控制效用。CausalNav的主要安全价值来自经过认证的选择性采用与拒答机制，而不是单纯获得更高的预测精度；因此，世界模型控制器应将可靠性校验、策略门控和安全回退作为核心设计。

Abstract: A world model is only useful for physical AI if it changes what the agent does, and only safe if it declines to do so when it is wrong. We study both halves of that requirement with CausalNav, a controller built around a signed, action-conditioned transition graph over identified state coordinates. At deployment CausalNav simulates a small library of intervention sequences, converts their objective error into policy-logit advice, and admits that advice only when a scale-free predictive-reliability certificate, a policy-margin gate, and an argmax-agreement gate all pass; otherwise it falls back exactly to its own model-based base controller. We evaluate against nine controlled baselines (transformer, recurrent, split-latent, graph, causal-induction, and three recent model-based reasoning modules) on CartPole-v1 and discretized Pendulum-v1 with physical-parameter shifts, under one shared PPO trainer, one interaction budget, and ten held-out seeds (200 runs). CausalNav attains the best average rank (1.25 of ten). The diagnostic result is more informative than the ranking: the learned graph recovers structure well above chance (CartPole F1 = 0.59 +/- 0.09), yet per-seed structural fidelity is uncorrelated with per-seed control benefit (r = -0.15, p = 0.67), and the certificate abstains on 10/10 Pendulum seeds, where forcing the planner on costs return. Model fidelity did not predict downstream control utility in our setting; certified abstention, not better prediction, is what made the world model safe to deploy.

</details>


### [98] [The Field Knows: Cross-Dimensional Geometry from Navigation to Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07566)
*Chenghao Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由单一因果对比损失训练的连续度量场框架，将场景编码为固定对称矩阵基的系数，构造李代数元素并通过矩阵指数映射生成黎曼或洛伦兹度量。该框架据称可在机器人导航和时空建模中零样本发现多种几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在以统一的表示、损失函数和训练流程学习跨维度、可迁移的几何结构，避免针对特定配置或任务进行专门建模，并探索因果约束是否能够诱导出具有物理意义的度量结构。

Method: 将输入场景映射为固定对称矩阵基下的系数，组合为李代数元素，再通过指数映射生成连续的黎曼或洛伦兹度量场。模型采用单一因果对比损失进行训练，并在平面空间、机械臂配置空间及洛伦兹时空等不同维度和任务上进行评估，同时开展零样本泛化实验。

Result: 在机器人导航中，模型生成了可用于绕开障碍物的测地线；在黑洞相关设置中，据称自发形成具有正确洛伦兹签名的类黑洞结构，包括类似事件视界的几何现象。零样本实验表明，该模型能够迁移部分几何结构，而非仅记忆具体配置。摘要还声称同一架构、损失和训练流程可覆盖多类几何现象。

Conclusion: 研究表明，因果对比学习结合李代数参数化度量的方式，可能提供统一的几何表示与跨任务泛化机制。不过，摘要中的“完整几何谱系”“真实黑洞结构”等表述较强，仍需通过严格基线、定量误差、物理一致性检验、消融实验及独立复现实验证实其有效性与物理解释。

Abstract: We introduce a continuous metric field framework trained by a single causal contrastive loss. The framework encodes a scene into coefficients of a fixed symmetric matrix basis, assembles them into a Lie algebra element, and exponentiates the result to a Riemannian or Lorentzian metric. Across dimensions, this field discovers the full spectrum of geometric structures: from obstacle-avoiding geodesics in robot navigation across planar and manipulator configuration spaces, to event horizons of black holes in Lorentzian spacetime. Extensive zero-shot generalization studies demonstrate that the field captures transferable geometric structure rather than memorizing specific configurations. In the black hole setting, the causal loss spontaneously evolves genuine black-hole-like structures with the correct Lorentzian signature. The same loss, the same architecture, and the same training protocol produce the full range of geometric phenomena across dimensions. The field knows geometry, and geometry knows physics.

</details>


### [99] [MetaSpace: Metamorphic Testing for Spatial Cognition in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07533)
*Gengyang Xu,Dongwei Xiao,Yiteng Peng,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaSpace，一种基于变形测试的具身智能体空间认知评估框架，通过从真实执行轨迹中提取时空多模态状态，并利用以Prolog编码的变形关系自动生成测试用例和检测认知错误。实验显示，现有MLLM驱动智能体的空间认知能力明显低于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体评估主要依赖人工标注的VQA问答或任务是否完成，前者成本高且存在标注偏差，后者可能掩盖不安全、低效或不合理的执行过程。由于空间认知是导航、操作等具身任务的基础，需要一种能够在任务执行过程中识别空间认知缺陷的自动化评估方法。

Method: MetaSpace借鉴软件工程中的变形测试思想，基于真实执行轨迹构建时空多模态状态，并依据逻辑规则和物理定律定义变形关系。研究将这些变形关系编码为Prolog中的可执行规则，自动生成测试案例；当智能体行为或状态违反相关关系时，即判定存在空间认知错误。此外，研究提出Spatial Cognition（SC）评分，用于量化智能体的空间认知表现。

Result: 在三个具身场景中，MetaSpace共检测出90,422个空间认知错误。现有最先进的MLLM驱动智能体平均SC分数介于0.44和0.52之间，显著低于人类基准0.96，表明这些智能体在空间关系理解和执行一致性方面仍存在明显不足。

Conclusion: MetaSpace能够突破单纯任务成功率评估的局限，从执行过程和时空一致性角度揭示具身智能体的隐性空间认知缺陷。实验结果证明，该框架具有自动化、可解释和可扩展的评估价值，但其效果仍取决于变形关系的覆盖范围、规则质量以及测试场景的代表性。

Abstract: An embodied agent is an intelligent entity that interacts with its environment through a physical body. Currently, the evaluation of embodied agents primarily relies on two paradigms: (1) manually annotated Visual Question Answering (VQA) pairs and (2) high-level task completion metrics, such as success in navigation or manipulation. The former is labor-intensive and subject to variability in annotation quality. The latter may obscure critical vulnerabilities, allowing agents to complete tasks through suboptimal means or safety violations, thereby concealing safety risks and inefficiencies. Given that spatial cognition is the cornerstone for executing embodied tasks, there is a pressing need to assess whether embodied agents possess robust spatial cognition during task execution. Inspired by metamorphic testing principles in software engineering, we propose MetaSpace, a novel framework designed to evaluate the spatial cognition of agents. By leveraging spatiotemporal multimodal states derived from real execution trajectories, MetaSpace automatically generates test cases based on predefined metamorphic relations (MRs) grounded in logical rules and physical laws. Crucially, we encode these MRs as executable rules in a logic programming language (Prolog). Violations of these relations indicate failures in spatial cognition. Our empirical evaluation across three embodied scenarios demonstrates that MetaSpace successfully detects 90,422 spatial cognition errors in state-of-the-art (SOTA) MLLM-driven agents. We introduce the Spatial Cognition (SC) score to quantify performance. Results indicate that all SOTA agents achieve average scores between 0.44 and 0.52, significantly lower than the human benchmark of 0.96.

</details>


### [100] [CMU-Drive and V2V-VLA: Cooperative Multi-agent Unified Driving with Reasoning Benchmark and Vehicle-to-Vehicle Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07621)
*Hsu-kuang Chiu,Stephen F. Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CMU-Drive闭环端到端协作驾驶基准，以及V2V-VLA协作视觉-语言-动作模型，用于评估和实现多辆联网自动驾驶车辆在复杂交通场景中的协同感知、推理、规划与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型主要面向单一自动驾驶智能体，对多车协作感知、联合推理、协同规划和通信支持不足，缺少统一的闭环评测基准与模型基线。

Method: 构建CMU-Drive多智能体闭环端到端基准，包含多辆联网自动驾驶车辆及交通参与者的复杂驾驶场景；提出V2V-VLA模型，在单次前向推理中联合生成驾驶动作、未来路径点、语言推理结果和通信策略，从而统一支持车辆间协作。

Result: 实验表明，CMU-Drive提供了协作式VLA驾驶的首个基准与基线，并验证了V2V-VLA在多车协同驾驶任务中的可行性。论文还计划公开代码、基准数据和模型检查点，以促进后续研究。摘要未提供具体性能指标或与现有方法的定量对比。

Conclusion: 该工作将VLA模型从单车驾驶扩展到多车闭环协作驾驶，贡献了统一的评测平台和协作模型，为多智能体端到端自动驾驶中的通信、联合决策与规划研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently achieved impressive performance for end-to-end autonomous driving, yet existing approaches are primarily designed for an individual single autonomous driving agent with limited support for cooperative perception, reasoning, and planning. We present Cooperative Multi-agent Unified Driving with Reasoning (CMU-Drive), a closed-loop end-to-end benchmark for evaluating cooperative autonomous driving with multiple connected autonomous vehicles (CAVs) operating in safety-critical driving scenarios with background traffic participants. We further propose Vehicle-to-Vehicle Vision-Language-Action (V2V-VLA), a cooperative VLA model that integrates cooperative driving into a single forward pass by jointly generating driving actions, future waypoints, language reasoning, and communication policies. Experiments on CMU-Drive establish the first benchmark and baseline for cooperative VLA driving and provide a foundation for future research on multi-agent, closed-loop, end-to-end cooperative autonomous driving. Our code, benchmark, and model checkpoint will be publicly released to facilitate open-source research.

</details>


### [101] [An AI Scientist that Doesn't Drift: Taste, Structure, and Falsifiable Findings in a Quadruped Navigation Research Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07542)
*Yiwen Zhang,Eloise Zeng,Jaeha Lee,Tony Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向四足机器人导航策略泛化研究的自主科研循环，通过固定实验记录、角色分工子智能体和偏好预言机 kkanbu，减少大语言模型科研过程中的目标漂移与假设改写。结果表明，kkanbu主要改变研究方向和知识积累，而非直接提升最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有由大语言模型驱动的自主实验系统容易围绕可优化指标进行局部改进，偏离最初研究假设，且可能在结果产生后重新解释或修改假设。本文旨在建立更诚实、可追踪，并能体现研究者偏好的自动化科研框架。

Method: 基于 autoresearch 范式构建自主科研循环，加入三项机制：一是不可变实验卡片，以固定格式记录每轮预测及结果；二是职责受限的专业子智能体，分别执行机械化任务；三是 kkanbu 偏好预言机，将用户研究偏好表示为类型化知识图谱，并作为唯一能够进行主观判断的组件。研究在四足机器人导航仿真环境中开展，并在11条研究流上分别运行带有和不带 kkanbu 的相同循环。

Result: 两种实验条件均未表现出明显的目标漂移，约四分之三的自身假设被证伪，说明框架能够保留真实的失败反馈。无 kkanbu 条件下训练出的最佳策略反而性能更高。kkanbu 的主要影响体现在探索方向：只有其所在实验组探索了测试时适应；在该组领先的研究流中，获胜设计均由其提出；此外，kkanbu 能在不同研究流之间迁移经验，而另一组经常重复发现相同结论。

Conclusion: 不可变实验记录和结构化角色分工能够维护自主科研循环的可验证性与诚实性；偏好预言机则主要负责决定研究搜索空间、探索方向和跨任务知识复用，而不是保证更高的最终指标。该工作表明，自动化科研系统中的评价与方向选择应与实验执行机制相互分离。

Abstract: Autonomous research loops driven by large language models can run machine-learning experiments at scale but tend to drift toward local refinements of whichever metric they optimise rather than testing the hypotheses that motivate the experiments. We address this structurally and present an AI Scientist for studying generalisation in quadruped robot navigation policies in simulation. Building on the autoresearch paradigm of Karpathy, our loop adds three components: an immutable experiment card that pairs each iteration's prediction with its outcome under a fixed schema, so a falsified hypothesis cannot be retconned; specialised subagents restricted to mechanical roles; and kkanbu, a preference oracle that holds the user's research taste as a typed knowledge graph and is the only component permitted to make subjective judgements. To isolate the oracle we run the identical loop twice across eleven research streams, with and without kkanbu. Neither arm drifts: both falsify roughly three quarters of their own hypotheses, and the best trained policy comes from the oracle-less arm. What the oracle changes is direction, not score: it alone explores test-time adaptation, it authored the winning designs where its arm led, and it carried lessons across streams that the other arm repeatedly re-derived. The scaffold keeps the loop honest; kkanbu decides where it looks.

</details>


### [102] [Emotion in an active inference model of human driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07480)
*Julian F. Schumann,Johan Engström,Ran Wei,Jens Kober,Martijn Wisse,Arkady Zgonnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将基于效价—唤醒度环形模型的情感状态引入连续状态主动推理驾驶模型，并结合当前状态与未来预测结果估计驾驶情绪；在两个交互式驾驶场景中的实验表明，生成的情绪信号与既有研究报告的情感模式一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动推理驾驶模型主要关注目标导向行为与不确定性降低，尚未充分建模会显著影响交通决策的情感状态。既有情感主动推理研究多限于离散状态和简化环境，难以适用于具有连续状态与未来结果预测的复杂驾驶场景。

Method: 构建一种扩展的效价与唤醒度估计方法，将其嵌入连续状态主动推理驾驶模型中。情感估计不仅依赖当前驾驶状态，还依据模型对未来结果的预测，并在两个交互式驾驶场景中进行评估。

Result: 所生成的效价和唤醒度信号呈现出与相似驾驶情境既有研究相符的情感变化模式，说明该方法能够从复杂连续状态的主动推理模型中提取具有解释性的情绪表征。

Conclusion: 研究证明，结合当前状态和未来预测结果，可以将效价—唤醒度情感建模扩展到连续状态驾驶任务，为构建能够表达情感影响的主动推理驾驶模型提供了可行基础。

Abstract: Active inference has emerged as a principled framework for modeling adaptive behavior by balancing goal-directed action with uncertainty reduction. It has been successfully applied across biological and artificial systems, including recent work on human driving. However, existing active inference models of driving have yet to address an important determinant of behavior in traffic: affective state, which significantly influences decision-making. Prior work in non-traffic domains has explored active inference agents in which emotions are represented along the axes of valence and arousal in the circumplex model. However, this work has been limited to simplified settings with discrete state spaces. In this work, we propose an expanded formulation of valence and arousal that can be extracted from a more complex active inference model of driving with continuous states. In particular, we condition affective estimates not only on the current state but also on predicted future outcomes. We evaluate the proposed approach in two interactive driving scenarios and show that the resulting emotion signals correspond to affective patterns reported in similar scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [V-Simba: Unleashing the Architectural Potential of RL in Visual Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07870)
*Donghu Kim,Youngdo Lee,Hojoon Lee,Johan Obando-Ceron,Byungkun Lee,Aaron Courville,Pablo Samuel Castro,Jaegul Choo,Clare Lyle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出V-Simba，一种将状态型强化学习中的Simba架构迁移到视觉强化学习的简洁架构，在多个基准上取得具有竞争力或更优的性能，并降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉强化学习面临高维输入和数据采集成本高等问题，导致样本效率较低。现有研究主要依赖动力学模型和探索策略改进，而状态型强化学习表明，合理的网络架构设计本身也能显著提升样本效率，因此本文探讨这些架构原则能否有效迁移至视觉强化学习。

Method: V-Simba构建于带数据增强的软演员-评论家算法（SAC）之上，借鉴Simba架构，引入归一化层以稳定训练，并使用逐点卷积降低计算成本。该方法在DMC、Adroit和Meta-World基准上进行评估，并与现有视觉强化学习方法，尤其是DrQ-v2，进行比较。

Result: V-Simba在DMC、Adroit和Meta-World基准上达到或超过现有先进方法的性能，同时相比DrQ-v2具有更高的计算效率。摘要未提供具体的样本效率提升幅度、统计显著性或消融实验结果。

Conclusion: 简单的架构改进能够有效提升视觉强化学习的性能与计算效率，说明状态型强化学习中的架构设计原则具有迁移到视觉场景的潜力。V-Simba为高效视觉强化学习提供了一种简洁且实用的方案，作者同时公开了代码以促进复现。

Abstract: Improving sample efficiency remains a core challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), especially in real-world settings like robotics, where data collection is costly. This challenge is pronounced in visual RL, where high-dimensional inputs often obscure learning signals. While prior work in visual RL has focused on algorithmic solutions, such as better dynamics models or exploration strategies, recent advances in state-based RL show that architectural design alone can lead to significant gains in sample efficiency. This raises an important question: Can these architectural principles transfer to visual RL? In response, we introduce V-Simba, a simple yet effective visual RL architecture inspired by the Simba architecture from state-based RL. Built on top of Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) with data augmentation, V-Simba modifies the architecture by adding normalization layers to stabilize training and using pointwise convolutions to reduce computation. Despite its simplicity, V-Simba matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art methods across the DMC, Adroit, and Meta-World benchmarks, while being more computationally efficient than DrQ-v2. We make our code publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [104] [Satellite Trajectory Optimization via Proximal Policy Optimization for Space Debris Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09628)
*Logan Luna,Juan Ortiz Couder,Raul Alejandro Vargas-Acosta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于近端策略优化（PPO）的强化学习自主碰撞规避策略，并结合高保真开源轨道动力学仿真器，用于应对低地球轨道和地球同步轨道中日益严重的交会与碰撞风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着巨型星座部署和轨道拥堵加剧，碰撞预警数量与规避需求持续增长。传统人工操作或规则驱动方法难以适应高动态、大规模场景，因此需要具备自主决策和可扩展性的碰撞规避方法。

Method: 构建包含牛顿二体动力学、太阳/月球第三体摄动、燃料相关推力和可配置碎片场的轨道仿真器；采用PPO训练强化学习策略，并结合课程学习与奖励塑形，重点鼓励存活、满足预测最小错距和节约速度增量（delta-v）。评估流程采用确定性设置，包括共享随机种子、逐回合日志和遥测数据导出，并与规则控制器及脉冲式delta-v规划器进行对比。

Result: 在1000个确定性的地球同步轨道测试回合中，强化学习智能体的碰撞规避成功率达到97.5%；规则基线和脉冲式delta-v规划器的成功率分别为20.7%和27.5%。结果表明，该方法在所设定的仿真环境中显著优于两类传统基线。

Conclusion: 研究验证了PPO强化学习策略用于自主轨道碰撞规避的潜力，并提供了公开的仿真、训练与评估框架。不过，结论主要基于确定性仿真和特定场景，仍需通过不确定轨道状态、真实运行约束及更广泛轨道环境下的测试，进一步验证其实际部署可靠性。

Abstract: Collision avoidance systems are commonly used to avoid fragmentation events occurring in Low-Earth Orbit (LEO) and Geosynchronous Equatorial Orbit (GEO). However, these events have been growing in frequency as orbital congestion worsens with the launch of megaconstellations. Consequently, conjunction alerts and collision risks are becoming increasingly common. Current practices, which are commonly manual or rule-based, have difficulty scaling to these worsening dynamic environments. To address this intensifying situation, we propose a reinforcement-learning policy for autonomous collision avoidance, trained via Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) along with an open-source, high-fidelity astrodynamics simulator for training and evaluation. In 1,000 deterministic GEO episodes, our agent achieves a 97.5% collision avoidance success rate, outperforming traditional controllers such as a rule-based baseline (20.7% success) and an impulsive delta-v planner baseline (27.5% success). To achieve these results, we designed a simulator to train and evaluate our agent, using real-world and simulated debris. We simulate Newtonian two-body dynamics using Sun/Moon third-body perturbations, fuel-dependent thrust, and configurable debris fields. The agent is trained with curriculum learning and shaped rewards oriented toward encouraging survival, adequate projected miss distance, and delta-v conservation. Finally, our evaluation consisted of a fully deterministic pipeline, including shared seeds, per-episode logs, and telemetry exports. Our work is a publicly available framework at this https URL

</details>


### [105] [Distilling Vision-Language Models for Robust Traffic Sign Perception in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08815)
*Pedram MohajerAnsari,Amir Salarpour,Mert D. Pesé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAMDA，一种利用语言先验提升交通标志识别模型物理对抗鲁棒性的训练框架；无需对抗样本和推理阶段额外开销，即可在多种数据集、骨干网络及攻击类型下稳定提升鲁棒性，并基本保持干净数据准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通标志识别防御方法通常只针对单一物理扰动有效，且可能损害其他攻击场景下的性能与干净数据准确率。研究旨在构建一种统一、低成本且不依赖对抗样本的鲁棒训练方法。

Method: LAMDA使用冻结的OpenCLIP文本编码器，根据视觉语言模型生成的交通标志描述和类别名称构建两组固定原型库。训练阶段通过两个互补的辅助损失，将视觉特征与语言锚定的类别结构及语义描述对齐；推理阶段移除适配器和原型库，仅保留标准骨干网络与分类器，因此不增加推理开销。

Result: 在GTSRB和LISA数据集、4种骨干网络及阴影、自然光干扰、打印补丁3类物理扰动上进行评估。LAMDA在10种对比方法中唯一实现所有攻击—骨干网络—数据集组合下的持续鲁棒性提升；阴影攻击最高提升12.5个百分点，自然光攻击最高提升13.2个百分点，并在几乎所有设置中保持或提高干净数据准确率。

Conclusion: LAMDA表明，借助语言描述与类别名称提供的语义结构，可以有效改善交通标志识别模型对多类型物理对抗扰动的泛化能力。该方法具有训练期增强、推理零额外开销和较少依赖攻击样本等优势，但摘要尚未说明其计算成本、跨数据集迁移能力及对更复杂现实环境的适用性。

Abstract: Traffic sign recognition (TSR) models based on deep neural networks achieve strong clean-data performance but remain vulnerable to physically realizable adversarial attacks, including shadow perturbations, natural-light interference, and printed patches. Existing defenses often improve robustness against one attack type while degrading performance on others, and can reduce clean accuracy. We propose LAMDA (Language-Anchored Model for Direction Alignment), a training framework that transfers language-grounded structure into TSR models without using adversarial examples or adding inference-time overhead. LAMDA builds two fixed prototype banks from VLM-generated sign descriptions and class names using a frozen OpenCLIP text encoder, and uses them to supervise visual features through two complementary auxiliary losses during training. At inference, the adapter and prototype banks are discarded, leaving a standard backbone and classifier. Evaluated on GTSRB and LISA across four backbones and three physical attack types, LAMDA is the only method among ten evaluated that consistently improves robustness across all attack-backbone-dataset combinations, with gains of up to +12.5 pp under shadow attacks and +13.2 pp under natural-light attacks, while preserving or improving clean accuracy in nearly all cases.

</details>


### [106] [Second Order Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07924)
*Drake Brown,Yuhao Huang,Shih-Hsin Wang,Bao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出二阶漂移模型，通过引入人工速度变量将一阶漂移动力学扩展到相空间，使密度扰动具有加速的二阶频域动力学，在保持单步推理的同时缓解细粒度结构学习缓慢的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶漂移模型虽可避免迭代推理，但其基于核的漂移场会产生频率依赖的训练速度：高频密度残差衰减较慢，导致细节恢复困难。论文旨在降低这种谱刚性并改善训练收敛性。

Method: 在生成样本中加入人工速度变量，构造二阶漂移动力学；在线性化频域分析中刻画密度扰动的加速衰减，并将其与优化中的Nesterov加速建立联系。进一步提出半隐式训练算法，以提升数值稳定性和实际可用性。

Result: 在合成分布匹配、序列数据生成和机器人控制任务中，二阶漂移模型均表现出更好的收敛行为，并相较一阶漂移基线取得具有竞争力或更优的性能，同时保持单步推理特性。

Conclusion: 引入速度变量为缓解一阶漂移模型的频谱刚性提供了具有理论依据的机制。二阶漂移结合半隐式训练能够加速训练并改善细节学习，具有跨任务应用潜力，但摘要未报告具体数值增益及计算开销。

Abstract: Drifting models are a recent class of one-step generative models that evolve the model distribution during training using a predefined sample-based drift field. Although they avoid iterative inference, their kernel-based drift fields induce frequency-dependent training dynamics: In the linearized regime, each Fourier mode of the density residual decays at a rate determined by the kernel spectrum, leading to slow recovery of fine-scale structure. We propose Second-Order Drifting Models, which lift drifting dynamics into phase space by augmenting generated samples with artificial velocity variables. We show that the resulting density perturbations obey accelerated second-order dynamics in Fourier space, connecting drifting models to the celebrated Nesterov acceleration from optimization theory. This provides a principled mechanism for mitigating the spectral stiffness of first-order drifting while preserving one-step inference. We derive a practical semi-implicit training algorithm and evaluate it on synthetic distribution matching, sequential data generation, and robotic control. Across these settings, the second-order drifting model improves convergence behavior and achieves competitive or superior performance over first-order drifting baselines.

</details>


### [107] [LUCID: Latent-Skill Unified Control via Imagined Dynamics for Long-Horizon Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07746)
*Cheng Guo,Mingzhe Ni,Angelo Cangelosi,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LUCID是一种分层模型强化学习框架，通过学习世界模型并在潜在技能空间中进行想象 rollout，实现 humanoid 机器人长时程全身运动与操作任务的规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖预训练技能与脚本规划器、有限状态机或针对单任务的无模型策略，难以灵活组合技能并处理复杂、多阶段任务序列。因此，需要一种能够在技能层面进行长期规划且减少真实环境交互的方法。

Method: 首先通过对抗模仿学习训练结构化、潜变量条件化的低层全身控制策略，并将其冻结为可复用技能库。随后联合训练高层策略与宏观动力学世界模型，其中世界模型预测潜变量决策所引起的时间扩展状态转移。高层策略利用世界模型进行想象 rollout，并据此优化技能选择与任务规划。

Result: 在多种模拟多物体重排场景中，LUCID相较于既有基线方法提升了完整任务成功率和部分任务完成率，表明其能够更有效地处理复杂的连续运动与操作任务。摘要未提供具体数值、实验规模或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: LUCID验证了将可复用低层技能、宏观世界模型与高层模型式强化学习相结合的有效性，为长时程 humanoid 全身运动操作提供了一种更具扩展性的规划框架。不过，其性能在真实机器人环境中的泛化能力以及对世界模型误差的敏感性仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation requires composing versatile whole-body skills and reliable high-level decision making. Existing methods often coordinate pretrained skills with scripted planners, finite-state machines or task-specific model-free policies, restricting their ability to handle complex task sequences. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{LUCID}, a hierarchical model-based reinforcement learning framework that plans over reusable skills through imagined rollouts of a learned dynamics model. LUCID first trains a structured latent-conditioned low-level policy via adversarial imitation and then freezes it while jointly learning a high-level policy and macro-dynamics world model. The world model predicts the temporally extended state transitions induced by latent decisions, enabling high-level policy optimization through imagined rollouts. We evaluate our framework across various simulated multi-object rearrangement scenarios. Experimental results show that LUCID improves the full-task success and partial-completion rates compared to prior baseline methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in complex sequential loco-manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [108] [Neural Operators for Immersed-Boundary Soft Swimmers Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07722)
*Mohammad Sadegh Eshaghi,Yizheng Wang,Navid Valizadeh,Xiaoying Zhuang,Timon Rabczuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了用于预测平面和三维鳗鱼游动流场的神经算子代理模型，以降低高保真浸入边界流固耦合模拟的计算成本。模型在速度、涡量和压力场预测上取得较低误差，但压力精度和物理一致性仍需改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真浸入边界模拟能够解析变形游动体与周围流体的耦合运动，但计算成本高，难以支持工程设计、参数研究和控制中的重复评估。因此，需要能够快速预测时变流场的代理模型。

Method: 基于自适应流固耦合模拟导出的规则网格场数据，构建条件神经算子，用于时间推进预测。模型以游动体几何和雷诺数为条件。平面模型联合预测两个速度分量、标量涡量和压力；三维模型采用三个具有共同多通道输入的目标特定模型，分别预测三维速度、涡量和压力。

Result: 平面模型在5条留出的高雷诺数轨迹上取得3.51%的全域相对L2误差。三维模型在5条范围内留出轨迹上的速度、涡量和压力误差分别为3.44%、5.58%和19.2%。速度和涡量预测总体较准确，而压力误差明显较高。

Conclusion: 研究证明了神经算子用于移动边界游动体流场全场代理预测的可行性，并可显著减少重复高保真模拟的需求。后续工作应重点提升压力预测精度，加强预测结果的物理约束与一致性，并进一步验证模型的外推能力。

Abstract: High-fidelity immersed-boundary simulation resolves the coupled motion of a deforming swimmer and its surrounding flow, but the resulting cost limits repeated evaluations for engineering design, parameter studies, and control. We develop neural-operator surrogates for temporal prediction of the hydrodynamic fields generated by planar and volumetric eel swimmers. The surrogates are trained on regular-grid fields exported from adaptive fluid--structure simulations and are conditioned on swimmer geometry and Reynolds number. The planar model jointly predicts two velocity components, scalar vorticity, and pressure. On five held-out high-Reynolds-number trajectories, its full-domain global relative L^2 error is 3.51 %. The volumetric formulation uses three target-specific models with a common multichannel input: one model predicts three-dimensional velocity, one predicts vorticity, and one predicts pressure. Their full-domain global relative L^2 errors on five held-out within-range trajectories are 3.44 %, 5.58 %, and 19.2 %. Together, the results demonstrate the feasibility of field-resolved neural surrogates for moving-boundary swimmer flows while identifying pressure accuracy and physical consistency as priorities for further development.

</details>


### [109] [CODS: Iterative Bellman-Residual Data Selection for Reusable Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07719)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Sanjeda Akter,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Md Najmus Swaqeeb,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出CODS，一种由评论家引导、可复用的离线强化学习数据子集选择方法。在仅保留10%数据的情况下，CODS在20个有效的D4RL任务—算法组合中保持了候选池性能的96.6%，并整体优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习通常需要在固定转移数据池上针对不同随机种子和超参数重复训练，导致数据与计算成本高昂。简单随机下采样又可能删除对长期信用分配和稀疏奖励学习至关重要的罕见转移，因此需要一种能够保留高价值样本且可跨实验复用的静态子集选择方法。

Method: CODS交替执行两步：首先拟合与目标算法匹配的评论家模型，然后依据评论家预测残差获取高价值转移，并在多轮采集中动态刷新评分，最终冻结为可复用的数据子集。该方法不同于优先经验回放，因为其输出是静态数据产物；也不同于一次性残差筛选，因为它会随着评论家更新而重新评估样本。论文还进行机制分析、异常值扰动实验，以及基于整条轨迹的扩展评估。

Result: 在10%数据预算下，CODS保留了候选池96.6%的性能；在20个有效D4RL任务—算法组合中，超过ReDOR和OPER的19个，并超过其他所有子集基线的20个组合。六项子集优势在预先声明的分层推断和Holm校正后仍具有显著性。在总选择器更新次数固定时，五轮采集相较一轮在四个代表性组合上平均提升11.23个百分点，之后趋于饱和。等遍数和等耗时实验表明，主要计算优势来自子集复用，而非单次训练加速。机制与数据污染实验显示，CODS能够富集稀疏奖励相关样本，但对异常值较敏感。整轨迹扩展在ALFWorld上保留了95.4%的汇总成功率，在GSM8K上保留了96.5%的汇总精确匹配率。

Conclusion: CODS是一种有效且可复用的离线强化学习数据选择程序，能够以较小数据预算维持接近完整数据池的性能，并在多任务、多算法场景下带来计算节省。不过，论文并未提供形式化的核心集保证，其效果可能受到异常值和任务分布的影响。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning repeatedly trains policies from a fixed transition pool, making redundant data costly across seeds and hyperparameters, while naive subsampling can remove rare transitions needed for long-horizon credit assignment. We introduce CODS, a critic-guided selector that alternates between fitting an algorithm-matched critic and acquiring high-residual transitions before freezing a reusable subset. Unlike prioritized replay, CODS produces a static artifact; unlike one-shot residual selection, it refreshes scores as the critic changes. At a 10\% budget, CODS retains 96.6\% of eligible-pool performance across 20 valid D4RL task--algorithm cells. It exceeds ReDOR and OPER on 19/20 cells and every other subset baseline on 20/20; all six subset advantages remain significant under predeclared hierarchical inference with Holm correction. Holding total selector updates fixed, five acquisition rounds improve four representative cells by 11.23 points over one round and saturate thereafter. Equal-pass and equal-hour evaluations clarify that reuse, rather than a single-run speedup, creates the compute advantage. Mechanism and corruption interventions expose both useful sparse-reward enrichment and sensitivity to outliers. Finally, a whole-trace extension retains 95.4\% of pooled ALFWorld success and 96.5\% of pooled GSM8K exact match. CODS is therefore a reusable selection procedure, not a formal coreset guarantee.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [DAP-Pose: Deep Temporal Alignment and Physics-aware Cross-modal Sensor Fusion for Robust Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23755)
*Jianhan Lin,Yuchu Qin,Jiateng Yuan,Wenbo Zhang,Shuai Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAP-Pose，一种面向复杂环境的端到端多模态位姿估计模型，融合视觉、惯性和GNSS信息，并通过时序对齐与物理约束提升精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶和移动机器人在复杂环境中需要准确且鲁棒的位姿估计，但多传感器之间存在信息异构、时间不同步、尺度不确定和累积漂移等问题。

Method: 模型包含双层跨模态融合模块（BCF），用于提取视觉、惯性与GNSS中的互补语义和几何运动信息；深度时序对齐模块（DTA）在潜空间显式对齐异步数据流；同时引入基于流形几何的物理约束和GNSS引导的绝对尺度约束，以增强运动一致性并抑制漂移。

Result: 在KITTI公开数据集上的实验表明，DAP-Pose取得当前最佳性能，平均平移误差（t_rel）为1.31%，旋转误差（r_rel）为0.46°。在人工注入严重时间错位的条件下，模型仍能保持准确且稳定的位姿估计性能。

Conclusion: DAP-Pose有效解决了多模态位姿估计中的跨模态融合、异步时序对齐、尺度恢复和漂移抑制问题，具有较强的精度与时间不同步鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robust and accurate pose estimation with multi-modal sensors is fundamental for autonomous vehicles and mobile robotic systems in complex environments. In this paper, we propose DAP-Pose, a unified end-to-end model for robust multi-modal pose estimation. DAP-Pose introduces a Bi-level Cross-modal Fusion (BCF) module that captures complementary semantic and geometric motion cues from visual, inertial, and GNSS measurements. To handle temporal offsets, we designed a Deep Temporal Alignment (DTA) module that explicitly aligns asynchronous streams in latent space, enabling coherent motion modeling without strict hardware synchronization. Furthermore, we incorporate physics-aware constraints via manifold geometry and GNSS-guided absolute metric scale, enforcing motion consistency and mitigating drift. Experiments upon the public KITTI benchmark dataset were conducted to evaluate the performance of DAP-Pose against existing methods. DAP-Pose achieved the state-of-the-art performance, with the lowest average translation error ($t_{rel}$) of 1.31% and rotation error ($r_{rel}$) of 0.46$^{\circ}$. Furthermore, it accurately estimates poses and maintains robust performance under severe artificially injected temporal misalignment.

</details>


### [111] [World Tokens: Enhancing Embodied Policies with Training-Time World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09730)
*Qu Tang,Benhui Zhuang,Bo Yuan,Xue Yu,Longteng Guo,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出World Tokens，一种利用世界模型训练增强动作策略、但在部署时无需运行视频世界模型的视觉-语言-动作架构，在多个仿真和真实机器人基准上取得有竞争力的性能，同时保持VLA级推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLA模型擅长闭环控制，但缺乏对任务过程中物理场景演化的显式建模；WAM虽然引入视频世界模型以捕获时空动态，却因未来视频生成和大型视频骨干网络增加控制环路的推理成本。本文旨在兼顾世界动态建模能力与高效部署。

Method: 架构核心是World Adapter。它将视觉语言模型特征转换为固定数量的world tokens，并以此共同条件化联合微调的未来视频去噪器和动作专家。未来视频去噪产生的梯度可直接优化动作预测所使用的表示；同时，动作专家只能接收world tokens作为视觉语言上下文，避免绕过世界建模表征。部署时移除世界模型分支，仅保留VLM、World Adapter和动作专家。

Result: 在采用2B骨干网络且不进行具身动作预训练的条件下，World Tokens在LIBERO上表现具有竞争力，在SIMPLER上取得报告中的最佳平均成绩，并相较匹配的仅动作基线显著提升真实世界R1 Pro成功率。每个动作块的生成延迟达到VLA级别。

Conclusion: 该方法证明，世界模型可以作为训练期的表征学习与辅助监督模块，而不必参与在线控制推理。通过共享world tokens和专属信息路由，World Tokens在增强物理动态理解的同时维持了较低部署成本，体现出较好的性能与效率平衡。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are a widely adopted paradigm for embodied policies. They excel at efficient closed-loop control but do not explicitly model how physical scenes evolve as a task unfolds. Recently emerging world-action models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video world models to capture spatiotemporal evolution, yet retaining future generation or a large video backbone in the control loop substantially increases inference cost. We introduce World Tokens, an embodied policy architecture built around a World Adapter that bridges visual-language understanding, world-dynamics modeling, and action generation. It uses world modeling during training to enhance the action policy while preserving efficient deployment. Specifically, the World Adapter transforms VLM features into a fixed set of world tokens, which condition a jointly fine-tuned future-video denoiser and simultaneously serve as the action expert's sole visual-language context. This shared conditioning allows gradients from future-video denoising to directly shape the representation used for action prediction, while exclusive routing prevents the policy from bypassing that representation. At deployment, the world-model branch is removed, leaving only the VLM, World Adapter, and action expert, with no online video-model inference. With a 2B backbone and no embodied action pretraining, World Tokens is highly competitive on LIBERO, attains the best reported averages on SIMPLER, substantially improves real-world R1 Pro success over a matched action-only baseline, and generates each action chunk at VLA-level latency.

</details>


### [112] [From Semantic Grounding to Decision Optimization: A Unified Framework for Long-Horizon UAV Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09564)
*Zeyuan Ma,Jiaxin Chen,Di Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向无人机视觉-语言导航的统一语义到决策框架，通过语义增强、动态历史聚合和拓扑感知决策，提升复杂开放三维环境中的导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN方法存在三类耦合问题：难以将指令相关地标与视觉观测准确对齐，长时历史信息利用不足，以及在局部最优、重复探索等情形下决策不稳定。

Method: 首先，设计指令驱动的语义增强模块，将目标级语义和相对空间线索注入当前观测状态。其次，提出相关性感知的动态时间聚合策略，对完整历史缓存进行重加权，并将少量高相关帧转换为结构化地标提示输入解码器。最后，构建拓扑感知决策方法，将局部最优认知与群体相对策略优化结合，并依据进度、目标、语义和路径合规性奖励进行训练。

Result: 在AerialVLN和OpenFly两个常用基准上的实验表明，该方法取得了当前最优性能，说明其在视觉语义对齐、长时信息利用和复杂决策稳定性方面具有优势。

Conclusion: 该研究通过统一连接语义理解、历史建模与拓扑决策，有效缓解了UAV-VLN中的关键瓶颈，为开放三维环境下更可靠的语言指令导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: UAV vision-language navigation (UAV-VLN) focuses on enabling an aerial agent to follow natural-language instructions in open 3D environments from egocentric visual observations. Current approaches suffer from three coupled issues: weak grounding of instruction-relevant landmarks in visual observations, insufficient exploitation of long-horizon history, and unstable decisions under local traps or repeated exploration. To address these issues, we propose a unified semantic-to-decision framework. First, we present an instruction-grounded semantic enhancement module that injects object-level semantics and relative spatial cues into the current observation state. Subsequently, we develop a relevance-aware dynamic temporal aggregation strategy that reweights the full history buffer while converting a few high-relevance frames into structured landmark prompts for the decoder. Finally, we devise a topology-aware decision method that combines local-optimum cognition with group-relative policy optimization under progress, goal, semantic, and path-compliance rewards. Experiments on the widely used AerialVLN and OpenFly benchmarks clearly demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [113] [Towards Collaborative Joint Perception and Prediction: Framework, Baseline Evaluation, and Deployment Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09541)
*Lei Wan,Hannan Ejaz Keen,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出协同联合感知与预测（Co-P&P）框架，将多车协同感知与运动预测统一起来，以缓解感知误差累积和视觉遮挡问题。实验表明，检测级或跟踪级融合优于预测级融合；端到端原型在约降低34倍通信带宽的同时仍能提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统协同感知主要关注目标检测，难以充分应对感知误差累积、遮挡以及复杂动态交通环境中的未来状态推断问题。本文旨在通过联合利用多源传感器信息和运动预测，提升周围交通参与者的态势理解与预测能力。

Method: 构建模块化Co-P&P概念框架，对检测级、跟踪级和预测级融合策略进行比较，并建立感知与预测模块的基线性能。进一步实现端到端原型：使用RENO神经编解码器进行协同点云共享，并结合FutureDet完成联合检测与预测。

Result: 预测级融合导致整体系统性能下降，相比之下检测级和跟踪级融合表现更优。端到端原型实验显示，协同信息能够提高运动预测准确率；采用神经压缩后，在通信带宽降低约34倍的情况下仍基本保持协同收益。

Conclusion: Co-P&P证明了将协同感知与运动预测联合建模的可行性。融合层级对系统性能具有显著影响，检测级或跟踪级融合更适合作为模块化方案；神经压缩为在有限通信资源下部署端到端协同系统提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Connected Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) increasingly exploit Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communication to exchange multi-source sensor information, enabling advanced Collaborative Perception (CP) capabilities. Extending beyond these capabilities, this work focuses on Collaborative Joint Perception and Prediction (Co-P&P), a paradigm that unifies CP with motion prediction to mitigate two persistent challenges: the accumulation of perception errors and visual occlusions. We present a conceptual framework for Collaborative Joint Perception and Prediction (Co-P&P) that improves motion prediction of surrounding road users, thereby enhancing situational awareness in complex and dynamic traffic environments. Building upon our preliminary study, this extended version compares the performance of different fusion strategies and establishes baseline performance for a modular design of perception and prediction. Experimental results show that prediction-level fusion leads to a decline in overall system performance compared to detection-level or tracking-level fusion. We further implement a minimal end-to-end Co-P&P prototype that couples collaborative point-cloud sharing via the RENO neural codec with joint detection-forecasting via FutureDet, showing that collaboration improves forecasting accuracy while neural compression preserves this benefit at roughly 34x lower communication bandwidth.

</details>


### [114] [A Height-Constrained 2-Point Minimal Solver for Pose Estimation from Active LED Markers with Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09520)
*Runze Yuan,Alexander Kappler,Jun Zhang,Kuangyi Chen,Fabio Morbidi,Pascal Vasseur,Cédric Demonceaux,Friedrich Fraundorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用两个LED标记、已知相机倾角与高度信息进行实时相机位姿估计的最小求解器，适用于事件相机主动标记定位系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动标记定位方法通常依赖PnP求解器，并要求较规则或数量较多的标记布局，在空间受限场景中部署困难。与此同时，IMU或高度计可提供重力方向和相机高度等部分自运动信息，因此有必要研究仅使用两个LED标记并融合辅助传感器信息的低延迟位姿估计方法。

Method: 建立基于两个LED标记、已知相机倾角和相机高度的相机位姿估计模型，推导鲁棒的最小求解器。该方法既提供闭式解，也提供线性最小二乘解；同时分析退化构型，并刻画高度信息无法改善旋转估计的条件。作者还构建了事件相机主动标记系统，并使用运动捕获系统获取真实数据和真值。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，该方法的定位精度优于现有P2P求解器，并且相较P3P具有有竞争力的性能。实验验证了其在低延迟、标记数量受限和空间受限场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: 融合倾角和高度先验后，仅依赖两个LED标记即可唯一确定相机位姿，从而降低标记布局要求并保持实时性。该方法适合事件相机主动定位系统，但其性能仍取决于辅助传感器测量精度以及是否避开理论退化构型。

Abstract: In many autonomous applications requiring real-time localization, active marker-based systems are preferred due to their low latency and ease of deployment compared to computationally demanding feature-based methods. Event~\mbox{cameras} offer high temporal resolution and minimal delay and are commonly used with active LED markers for robust real-time localization. Existing methods typically rely on Perspective-n-Point (PnP) solvers for pose estimation. However, structured marker layouts can be challenging to deploy in space-constrained scenarios, while partial self-motion information (e.g., gravity direction and altitude) is readily available from onboard sensors. We derive a robust and accurate minimal solver that estimates camera pose from only two LED markers by incorporating known tilt angle and camera height measured by an onboard sensor, such as an IMU or an altimeter. The proposed formulation uniquely determines the camera pose through both a closed-form and a linear least-squares solution. We further analyze degenerate configurations and characterize the conditions under which height information does not contribute to rotation estimation. For evaluation, we developed an event-based active marker system to collect real-world data with ground truth from a motion capture system. Experiments on both synthetic and real data demonstrate improved accuracy over the state-of-the-art P2P solver and competitive performance relative to P3P.

</details>


### [115] [RecoverFly: A Failure-Aware Reinforcement Learning Post-Training Framework for Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09467)
*Boxiong Wang,Hui Kang,Geng Sun,Jiahui Li,Chao Yu,Daxin Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RecoverFly，一种面向端到端无人机视觉-语言-动作策略的失败感知强化学习后训练框架，通过改进动作级优化、失败样本重访、长尾场景课程学习和参考策略正则化，提升闭环导航性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于行为克隆的无人机视觉-语言-动作策略缺乏交互式闭环执行中的纠错监督；同时，强化学习又面临样本利用率低、场景分布长尾以及优化过程中的策略分布偏移等问题。因此，需要一种能够高效利用失败经验、稳定训练并保持已有能力的后训练方法。

Method: RecoverFly包含四个核心机制：一是采用token级强化学习，以稳定优化受语法约束的自回归无人机动作；二是重访尚未解决的失败案例，增强纠错学习和样本利用率；三是设计两阶段长尾场景课程学习，逐步提升对复杂场景的适应能力；四是引入参考策略正则化，缓解策略漂移并保留预训练策略已有能力。

Result: 在TravelUAV基准上的已见场景、未见地图和未见目标划分中，RecoverFly均取得最佳性能。相较于AerialVLA初始化模型，在约为训练集规模30%的总轨迹采样预算下，成功率提升了3.12至8.37个百分点，表明该方法具有较高的样本效率、鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: RecoverFly有效弥补了行为克隆在闭环纠错方面的不足，并缓解了强化学习中的样本效率、长尾分布和策略偏移问题。实验结果支持其作为端到端无人机视觉-语言-动作策略后训练方案的有效性，但摘要未报告各组件的独立消融结果及更广泛基准上的验证。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle vision-language navigation (UAV-VLN) requires agents to translate visual observations and language instructions into reliable flight actions in complex environments. Although recent end-to-end UAV vision-language-action (UAV-VLA) policies reduce reliance on separately designed perception, planning, and control modules, their behavior-cloning objectives provide limited corrective supervision for interactive closed-loop execution. Reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising solution, while its effectiveness is constrained by inefficient use of samples, long-tailed scene distributions, and policy distribution shift during optimization. To this end, we propose RecoverFly, a failure-aware RL post-training framework for end-to-end UAV-VLA policies. Specifically, RecoverFly adapts token-level RL for stable optimization of grammar-constrained autoregressive UAV actions, revisits unresolved failure cases to strengthen corrective learning and sample utilization, and combines a two-stage long-tail scene curriculum with reference-policy regularization to improve scene adaptation while preserving acquired capabilities. Experiments on the TravelUAV benchmark demonstrate that RecoverFly achieves the best performance on the seen, unseen-map, and unseen-object splits. Moreover, compared to the AerialVLA initialization, RecoverFly improves success rate by 3.12 to 8.37 percentage points under a total rollout budget of about 30\% of the training-set size, validating its effectiveness, robustness, and generalization capabilities.

</details>


### [116] [Multi-Submap Implicit Neural SLAM with Local-to-Global Loop Closure for Large-Scale Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09146)
*Tianchen Deng,Chongdi Wang,Nailin Wang,Lei Zhao,Ziqi Ma,Tianjun Zhang,Zhe Liu,Danwei Wang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向大规模复杂环境的神经SLAM系统，通过多子图架构、双层回环闭合、光流跟踪和跨子图在线蒸馏，提升重建质量、定位鲁棒性与系统可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于NeRF的SLAM主要适用于小规模场景，在大范围环境中容易出现灾难性遗忘、轨迹累积漂移、显存或内存开销过大，以及剧烈运动和视角变化下跟踪与重定位不稳定等问题。

Method: 系统采用渐进式建图策略，动态分配神经子图以避免内存爆炸；引入基于光流的跟踪模块以增强激烈运动下的位姿估计；构建局部到全局的双层回环闭合框架，并利用基础模型提取高性能全局描述子以提升跨视角重定位；在后端优化阶段执行跨子图在线蒸馏，约束重叠边界处的几何与外观一致性。此外，研究使用定制手持机电平台，并在公开基准和自建大规模室内外数据集上进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在重建质量、定位鲁棒性和大规模场景适应性方面优于现有先进神经SLAM方法，并能够直接部署于机载计算单元。摘要还声明将公开发布代码。

Conclusion: 该系统通过子图化表示、增强型跟踪与回环闭合以及跨子图一致性优化，有效缓解了大规模神经SLAM中的遗忘、漂移和资源扩展问题，为真实机器人感知与数字孪生应用提供了具有可扩展性的解决方案。

Abstract: Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF)-based SLAM has demonstrated impressive results in small-scale scene reconstruction, yet scaling these methods to extensive, complex environments remains challenging due to catastrophic forgetting and accumulated trajectory drift. This paper presents a robust, large-scale neural SLAM system featuring a multi-submap architecture and a dual-tier loop closure mechanism. Specifically, we propose a progressive mapping strategy that dynamically allocates neural submaps to maintain high-fidelity representations without memory explosion. For robust pose estimation, an optical-flow-based tracking module is integrated to handle aggressive motions. To address global consistency, we introduce a local-to-global loop closure framework leveraging the foundation model for high-performance global descriptor extraction, significantly enhancing relocalization accuracy under varying viewpoints. Furthermore, an inter-submap online distillation algorithm is designed during back-end optimization to enforce geometric and appearance consistency across overlapping submap boundaries. To validate the system, we developed a customized handheld mechatronic platform and conducted extensive evaluations on both public benchmarks and our large-scale indoor-outdoor datasets. Experimental results, including direct deployment on an onboard computing unit, demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art neural SLAM methods in reconstruction quality and localization robustness, providing a scalable solution for real-world robotic perception and digital twinning. We will release the code publicly on \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL } .

</details>


### [117] [EndoMD-SLAM: Endoscopic Gaussian Splatting SLAM under Optical Degradation with Memory and Static-Transient Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08949)
*Nuo Chen,Kangqi Ni,Lulin Liu,Joga Ivatury,Ying Ding,Farshid Alambeigi,Tianlong Chen,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EndoMD-SLAM，用于在内窥镜视觉受到运动碎屑、水冲洗等间歇性光学退化时，保持3D重建与SLAM的稳定性。该方法通过异常观测门控、历史关键帧重定位以及静态—瞬态分解，显著降低跟踪漂移和地图污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯泼溅SLAM依赖严格的多视角光度一致性，但临床内窥镜操作中常出现运动碎屑和水冲洗等动态视觉干扰。传统方法会将附着于相机视野中的伪影错误融合进持久三维几何，导致跟踪漂移、地图不可逆损坏，并影响导航与术后记录。

Method: EndoMD-SLAM包含两个核心机制：1）跟踪阶段采用记忆驱动的门控机制识别不可靠观测，暂停受污染区域的地图更新，并利用历史关键帧进行具有漂移感知能力的重定位；2）建图阶段采用自监督静态—瞬态分解，将视觉污染因素分离到专用瞬态场中，从而避免其与持久解剖结构发生几何纠缠。研究还基于结肠镜视频构建了针对光学退化的评测基准。

Result: 在严重光学退化条件下，标准基线方法出现明显失效，而EndoMD-SLAM能够保持地图几何完整性。实验显示，其绝对轨迹误差降低91%，渲染保真度提升9.9 dB PSNR，表明跟踪稳定性和重建质量均得到显著改善。

Conclusion: EndoMD-SLAM有效解决了内窥镜SLAM中动态光学污染破坏光度一致性的问题。通过在跟踪和建图阶段显式识别、抑制并隔离瞬态伪影，该框架提升了退化场景下的定位、重建和渲染可靠性，具有临床内窥镜导航与记录应用潜力。

Abstract: Dense 3D reconstruction is critical for clinical endoscopic navigation and documentation. While Gaussian Splatting SLAM systems show promise in this domain, they fundamentally rely on strict multi-view photometric consistency. In routine procedures, this assumption is severely violated by intermittent optical degradations like moving debris and water flushing. Standard systems erroneously fuse these cameraattached artifacts into the persistent 3D geometry, causing severe tracking drift and irreversible map corruption. To address this limitation, we propose EndoMD-SLAM, a framework designed to maintain stability under optical degradation through specialized tracking and mapping mechanisms. On the tracking side, a memory-driven gating mechanism detects unreliable observations to suspend map updates and utilizes historical keyframes for drift-aware relocalization. On the mapping side, a self-supervised static-transient decomposition isolates visual contaminants into a dedicated transient field. This explicit separation prevents artifacts from structurally entangling with the persistent anatomical map. We curate a degradationfocused benchmark from colonoscopy videos to systematically evaluate these failure modes. Extensive experiments show that while standard baselines fail under severe optical degradation, EndoMD-SLAM preserves geometric integrity, reducing absolute trajectory error by 91% and improving rendering fidelity by 9.9 dB PSNR.

</details>


### [118] [Zero-shot 2D Grounding with Novel Affordance Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08929)
*Haomeng Zhang,Raymond A. Yeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向新型可供性类别的零样本二维可供性定位任务，并构建NAT基准；同时提出无需训练的AffordAnything及可训练增强版本AffordAnything+，后者在AGD20K-NAT上较OOAL在IoU@0.4上绝对提升12.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二维可供性定位研究主要识别训练阶段已见的可供性类型，缺乏对新型可供性类别泛化能力的研究，而该能力对于真实场景中的开放世界交互至关重要。因此，本文旨在评估并提升模型对未见可供性类型的零样本定位能力。

Method: 作者定义了新型可供性类别零样本二维定位任务（NAT），并提出相应基准数据集。AffordAnything是一种无需训练的方法，利用分割线索进行定位，其核心动机是可供性区域与物体局部子部件之间存在较强相关性。AffordAnything+进一步采用可训练模型学习不同线索的组合，以提升定位效果。

Result: 在AGD20K-NAT基准上，AffordAnything+取得最佳性能，相较现有最优可供性定位方法OOAL，在IoU@0.4指标上实现12.3%的绝对提升，表明该方法对新型可供性类别具有更强的定位能力。

Conclusion: 本文将二维可供性定位从已知类别识别拓展到新型类别零样本泛化，并通过基准、分割线索和可训练融合策略推动该方向发展。实验结果显示，利用物体子部件与可供性区域之间的关联能够显著提升未见可供性类型的定位性能。

Abstract: 2D affordance grounding aims to locate the region of an object that a human can interact with. Existing research focuses on recognizing affordance types seen during training and does not study models' ability to generalize to novel affordances, which is crucial for real-world applications. We propose the task of zero-shot 2D grounding with novel affordance types (NAT) and introduce the NAT benchmarks. We then propose AffordAnything, a training-free method that leverages segmentation cues, motivated by the strong correlation between affordance regions and object subparts. To further improve performance, we develop AffordAnything+, a trainable variant that learns to combine these cues. On the proposed AGD20K-NAT benchmark, our best model AffordAnything+ achieves a substantial improvement of 12.3% (absolute) in IoU@0.4 over the SOTA affordance grounding method, OOAL.

</details>


### [119] [Goal-oriented Navigation Instruction Generation with Tour Video Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08596)
*Fangdi Li,Juncheng Liao,Changxu Cheng,Jiazhi Wang,Senda Chen,Tao Wang,Wuyue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoNIG：一种基于自我中心游览视频、初始观察和文本或视觉目标生成导航指令的目标导向任务，并配套构建数据集、诊断评测协议及两阶段课程学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航指令生成主要服务于视觉语言导航中的数据增强或多任务学习，通常依赖图结构、地图等中间表示。当目标路线偏离示范轨迹时，模型需要从紧凑环境先验中进行精细空间推理，现有多模态大语言模型在此方面能力不足。

Method: 在连续室内环境模拟器中构建包含6万段游览视频和3.7万个多模态提示的数据集，并设置渐进式难度。评测结合文本相似度、基于选项的空间一致性测试和下游导航执行效果。模型采用两阶段课程学习：首先通过Action Warmup学习空间动作与视角的对齐关系，再通过Complexity Progression使用探索难度逐步增加的轨迹，训练长时程导航推理能力。

Result: 实验表明，现有多模态大语言模型在VideoNIG任务上表现较弱；所提出的方法在文本质量、空间一致性和导航执行等互补指标上均取得显著提升。将生成的指令接入视觉语言导航代理后，代理能够执行相应导航，验证了该任务设定的可行性。

Conclusion: VideoNIG将导航指令生成从辅助性任务拓展为不依赖地图或图结构的目标导向视频推理任务。其数据集、诊断评测和课程学习框架有效提升了指令的空间推理能力与可执行性，为端到端视频导航和多模态空间智能研究提供了新的基准。

Abstract: Navigation Instruction Generation (NIG) aims to produce step-by-step natural language instructions for navigation guidance. Existing studies primarily treat NIG as an auxiliary task for vision-andlanguage navigation (VLN), focusing on data augmentation or multi-task learning. However, generating navigation instructions from compact environmental priors requires meticulous spatial reasoning, especially when the target route does not simply follow the demonstrated tour, and remains challenging for current multimodal models. In this work, we introduce VideoNIG, a goal-oriented video-grounded NIG task that generates navigation instructions from ego-centric tour videos, an initial observation, and a textual or visual goal, without relying on intermediate representations such as graphs and maps. We instantiate VideoNIG in a controlled simulator benchmark with 60K tour videos across continuous indoor environments and 37K multimodal prompts with progressive difficulty levels. We further introduce a diagnostic evaluation protocol that combines text similarity, choice-based spatial consistency tests, and downstream navigation execution. To address this task, we propose a two-stage Curriculum Learning framework that decomposes the learning into foundational motion perception and long-horizon navigation reasoning. Specifically, we first employ Action Warmup for spatial action-view alignment, followed by Complexity Progression using trajectories with increasing exploratory difficulty. Extensive experiments show that existing MLLMs struggle with VideoNIG, while our approach significantly improves instruction quality across complementary diagnostic metrics. Finally, integrating VideoNIG-generated instructions with a VLN agent demonstrates the executability of this task formulation for end-to-end navigation.

</details>


### [120] [RayLift: Lifting Complementary Ray-Wise Evidence with 3D Geometry Priors for Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08476)
*Meng Wang,Hongxia Yu,Wenzhe He,Xingdong Song,Huilong Pi,Jiapeng Zhang,Ruihui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RayLift是一种面向相机三维语义场景补全的框架，通过融合立体几何、射线证据与空间不确定性，提升三维结构恢复和体素语义预测的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将立体深度估计视为确定性的几何约束，导致深度不确定性和局部对应错误直接传播至体素表示，进而影响场景补全质量。

Method: 首先，利用冻结的三维视觉基础模型构建互补上下文编码器，提取具有几何感知能力的场景先验；其次，通过深度射线证据提升模块联合建模几何差异、深度置信度和空间不确定性，沿相机射线自适应采样并加权候选表面位置；最后，利用语义感知体素集成器显式建模空间支持范围，将射线证据注入体素特征。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和SSCBench-KITTI-360数据集上的大量实验表明，RayLift取得了具有竞争力的性能，并持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将立体几何作为度量参考，同时引入互补射线证据和不确定性建模，RayLift能够更稳健地恢复三维结构并改善语义场景补全效果。

Abstract: Camera-based 3D semantic scene completion (SSC) provides comprehensive scene understanding for autonomous driving and robotics. However, existing methods often treat stereo depth estimates as deterministic geometric constraints, causing depth uncertainty and local correspondence errors to propagate directly into voxel representations. To address this issue, we propose RayLift, a framework that uses stereo geometry as a metric reference while incorporating complementary ray evidence to recover reliable 3D structures adaptively. RayLift first employs a Complementary Context Encoder that extracts geometry-aware priors from a frozen 3D vision foundation model, thereby enriching the scene context. It then introduces a Depth Ray Evidence Lifter module that jointly models geometric dissimilarity, depth confidence, and spatial uncertainty to adaptively sample and weight candidate surface locations along each camera ray. Finally, a Semantic-Aware Voxel Integrator injects the resulting ray evidence into voxel features by explicitly modeling their spatial support. Extensive experiments on SemanticKITTI and SSCBench-KITTI-360 demonstrate that RayLift achieves competitive performance and consistently outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [121] [EgoTrack3D: A Modular Framework for Egocentric 3D Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08016)
*Jan Kulik,Bjarni Dagur Thor Karason,Yung-Hsu Yang,Boyang Sun,Marc Pollefeys,Xi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoTrack3D提出一个面向自中心RGB视频的模块化动态三维场景表示与持续跟踪框架，通过将二维分割提升至全局三维坐标系，并结合点级运动评分和体素级合并实现目标关联，在静态与动态目标上均支持持久化三维跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维跟踪和场景图构建方法通常聚焦显式交互或静态场景，难以应对自中心视频中的快速视角变化、局部遮挡及复杂动态。研究旨在构建能够长期维护、适应真实部署噪声的结构化三维场景表示。

Method: 首先将二维实例分割掩码提升到全局三维坐标系；随后利用点基运动评分机制评估候选目标关联关系，并采用体素基合并启发式维护和更新目标轨迹。在退化条件实验中，以稀疏三维边界框估计替代稠密深度图，并引入交互引导的动态关联策略，以增强噪声观测下的跟踪能力。

Result: 在Aria Digital Twin数据集上，相较于最强基线，EgoTrack3D的正确位置百分比（PCL）提升了11%。实验表明，该方法能够同时处理静态和动态目标，并在稀疏、噪声较大的观测条件下维持较准确的空间表示。摘要未报告绝对PCL数值、统计显著性或不同模块的独立消融结果。

Conclusion: EgoTrack3D为自中心视频中的持久化三维目标跟踪和动态场景表示提供了有效方案。其模块化设计兼顾了常规稠密深度条件与受限部署场景，具有较好的鲁棒性和应用潜力，但仍需通过更全面的消融、跨数据集测试及长期跟踪指标验证泛化能力。

Abstract: Understanding 3D scenes from egocentric video is fundamental for robotics and autonomous navigation, yet rapid viewpoint changes and partial occlusions make building structured representations challenging. Existing 3D tracking and scene graph construction methods primarily address explicit interactions or assume static scenes, limiting their ability to capture complex dynamics. We introduce EgoTrack3D, a modular framework that reconstructs and maintains a dynamic 3D scene representation directly from egocentric RGB video. The framework lifts 2D segmentation masks into a global 3D coordinate frame, using a point-based motion scoring mechanism alongside a voxel-based merging heuristic to associate object tracks. EgoTrack3D maintains accurate representations over time, achieving an 11% improvement in percentage of correct locations (PCL) relative to the strongest baseline on the Aria Digital Twin (ADT) dataset, while addressing the more general setting of persistent 3D tracking for both static and dynamic objects. Furthermore, to demonstrate the system's robustness under degraded conditions that simulate real-world deployment constraints, we replace dense depth maps with sparse 3D bounding box estimation and integrate interaction-guided dynamic association, enabling EgoTrack3D to maintain accurate spatial representations despite noisy observations.

</details>


### [122] [SpikeWorld: Fast-State Adaptation for Frozen Spiking World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07712)
*Ziqiao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpikeWorld是一种冻结参数的稀疏脉冲多模态模型，通过部署后的外部状态通路利用延迟预测残差，实现无需标签、奖励或反向传播的在线适应，并提升预测、跟踪及策略表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署后环境动力学和语义分布可能发生变化。若冻结模型则难以适应，若更新权重则需要优化器状态并可能破坏已学习的表征，因此需要一种不改动模型参数的自监督适应机制。

Method: 构建一个约145万参数的稀疏脉冲模型，联合训练异构感知预测、语义理解、图文绑定和动作条件动力学。部署时冻结全部参数，仅利用延迟的下一状态残差更新两条外部通路：累计固定损失库选择有界动作修正，路由特定残差矩阵修正下一状态预测。该过程不使用标签、教师输出、奖励、成功信号或真实分布偏移量，并以16字节RLS估计器作为对照。

Result: 联合训练使动作到下一状态的MSE提升17.10%，同时改善多模态预测、语义准确率和图文检索。在留出的剪切与衰减数据流上，组合外部状态分别提升总体预测5.48%和30.01%；固定损失库动作通路分别提升跟踪24.20%和3.94%。在包含450条新轨迹的六臂实验中，冻结策略奖励提高7.90，95%置信区间为[2.48,14.06]；成功率差异为13.33个百分点，但其区间为[0,40]，证据较不确定。相同输入下，模型参数和继承的语义输出保持逐位不变。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，外部残差状态与冻结的多模态脉冲模型结合，可以在不修改模型内部参数的情况下实现部署后适应，并带来预测和策略收益。其核心贡献不是优于线性系统辨识，而是将轻量在线估计与冻结多模态检查点整合。主要局限是部分性能区间较宽，实验规模有限，且泛化效果依赖于外部状态通路和测试分布。

Abstract: A predictive model receives a self-supervised signal whenever the consequence of an action is observed. Using that signal after deployment is difficult when dynamics and semantics share parameters: freezing prevents adaptation, whereas weight updates require optimizer state and may alter the learned representation. Here we introduce SpikeWorld, a 1.45M-parameter sparse spiking model jointly trained for heterogeneous sensory prediction, semantics, image-text binding and action-conditioned dynamics. At deployment, all trained parameters are frozen. Delayed next-state residuals update two external paths: cumulative fixed-bank losses select the bounded action correction, while route-specific residual matrices refine next-state prediction. Neither path uses labels, teacher outputs, rewards, success signals or the true shift value. Joint optimization improves action next-state MSE by 17.10\% while also improving multimodal prediction, semantic accuracy and image-text retrieval. On held-out shear and attenuation streams, the combined external state improves aggregate prediction by 5.48\% and 30.01\%; its fixed-bank action path improves tracking by 24.20\% and 3.94\%, respectively. In a six-arm study comprising 450 new Meta-World trajectories (75 per arm), SpikeWorld raises frozen-policy reward by 7.90 (95\% CI [2.48, 14.06]); the 13.33-point success difference is descriptive (CI [0, 40]). For identical sensory inputs, model parameters and inherited semantic outputs remain bitwise unchanged. A 16-byte RLS estimator obtains the highest non-oracle reward on linear attenuation, showing that the contribution is not superior linear identification, but its integration with a frozen multimodal spiking checkpoint. Reference code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [123] [Open-World Hierarchical Perception: Taxonomic Abstraction over Class-Agnostic Proposals for the Safe Handling of Out-of-Vocabulary Road Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07577)
*Felix Schaller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向自动驾驶的开放世界感知层：在类无关区域提议之上进行层级分类与运行时抽象，使系统能够处理闭集检测器未识别的目标，并避免对未知目标作出高置信度错误分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭集检测器只能将所有目标强行归入固定类别，遇到类别外目标时容易产生高置信度的具体错误标签，或直接漏检。此前方法仅在已有检测框上进行层级抽象，无法处理闭集检测器完全未框出的目标，因此需要面向开放世界、基于候选区域的评估框架。

Method: 将分类层级抽象机制置于类无关区域提议之上，并研究三类开放世界信号：类无关分割、基于外观的分布外评分和单目深度。论文通过留出类别评测，在真实标注目标上隐藏COCO中的7个类别，使用235个真实目标裁剪区域比较扁平闭集分类头与层级分类层的表现。

Result: 扁平闭集分类头对全部235个未知目标均输出了高置信度的错误具体标签，其中37%的目标甚至被错误归入不同的上层类别。层级分类层没有输出任何高置信度错误具体标签，并安全处理了94%的目标：其中26%被正确归入上层类别，69%被保守地标记为UNKNOWN OBSTACLE。三种二维线索均不足以单独解决问题，但组合使用具有可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了开放世界感知层在安全性上的价值：对超出词汇表的目标不作高置信度具体误判。不过，其优势主要体现在安全而非分类具体性，正确恢复上层类别的比例仅为26%，大部分目标需要以未知障碍物形式保守处理，体现了降低错误风险所付出的识别精度代价。

Abstract: A closed-set detector for autonomous driving must assign every object one of a fixed set of labels. On an object outside that set (a horse-drawn carriage, road debris, livestock on a rural road) it can only force a confident but wrong specific label or drop the object. Prior work in this series replaced the flat label set with a hierarchical taxonomy and a runtime abstraction rule, but evaluated it only on the boxes a closed detector already produces. This paper takes the layer open-world: we place taxonomic abstraction on top of class-agnostic region proposals so objects the closed detector never boxes can still be classified or flagged; we report a feasibility study of three open-world signals (class-agnostic segmentation, appearance-based out-of-distribution scoring, monocular depth) that shows why no single 2D cue suffices and how they compose; and we run the evaluation the earlier papers could not, a ground-truth leave-classes-out benchmark on real annotated objects. Holding out seven COCO classes and classifying their 235 ground-truth crops, a flat closed head emits a confident wrong specific label 100% of the time (37% of them in the wrong super-category, e.g. an animal named as a vehicle), whereas the hierarchical layer emits zero confident wrong specific labels and safely handles 94% of the objects (a correct super-category, or an explicit UNKNOWN OBSTACLE). We are explicit that this is a safety result, not a specificity one: the correct super-category is recovered only 26% of the time and the remaining 69% are conservatively flagged unknown. The contribution is an open-world perception layer that never makes a confident categorical mistake on an out-of-vocabulary object, together with an honest account of its cost.

</details>


### [124] [Impact of Dataset Composition on Embedded Real-Time UAV Wildfire Detection Using Compact YOLO Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07554)
*Eduardo de los Santos,Andre S. Kelbouscas,Ricardo B. Grando,Bruna V. Guterres*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究不同数据集构成对嵌入式无人机实时野火检测性能的影响。结果表明，真实且未增强的数据集取得了最佳综合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实野火图像数量有限，限制了视觉检测模型的训练效果；论文旨在评估合成数据与图像增强能否在资源受限的无人机部署环境中提升检测性能。

Method: 以紧凑型YOLO模型作为受控验证模型，比较四种训练配置：真实未增强、真实增强、混合未增强和混合增强。混合数据集由真实野火图像与AI生成样本组成，并根据召回率和平均精度均值评估检测效果。

Result: 真实未增强数据集取得了最佳整体运行点，在召回率与平均精度均值之间实现了最优平衡。加入合成数据或图像增强均未带来更优的最终部署方案。

Conclusion: 对于嵌入式无人机野火检测，真实数据的质量与领域一致性比单纯扩大训练集规模更重要。合成数据扩充和增强策略不一定能改善实际部署性能。

Abstract: The development of vision-based wildfire detection systems for unmanned aerial vehicles is constrained by the limited availability of diverse real-world training images. This paper investigates the impact of dataset composition on embedded real-time UAV wildfire detection using compact YOLO models as a controlled validation family. Four training configurations were evaluated: real non-augmented, real augmented, hybrid non-augmented, and hybrid augmented, where the hybrid sets combine real wildfire images with AI-generated samples. The objective is to determine whether synthetic data mixing and image augmentation improve practical detection performance under resource-constrained deployment conditions. Experimental results show that the best overall operating point was obtained with the real non-augmented dataset, which achieved the strongest balance between recall and mean average precision for UAV-based wildfire detection. The results also show that neither hybridization with synthetic data nor augmentation produced a better final deployment choice. These findings suggest that, for embedded UAV wildfire detection, dataset realism and domain alignment are more valuable than increasing training set size through synthetic expansion.

</details>


### [125] [Can Webcam Gaze Constrain Mesa-Objectives in Driving Models? An Instrument Precision Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08947)
*Lennox Anderson,Ahmed Boutar,Jonah Mulcrone,Tal Erez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究检验了基于摄像头的眼动信息能否帮助自动驾驶系统减少“mesa objective”现象，但在不同校准协议、模型架构和随机种子下均未发现显著改进。主要原因是眼动估计误差远大于危险目标尺寸，无法进行可靠的目标级注视归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶模型可能通过虚假相关性获得较高训练性能，而非真正学习危险识别。研究尝试将人类注视模式作为特权信息，以约束模型形成内部目标并提升危险感知的真实性。

Method: 研究收集了388段真实行车视频中的137663个帧级注视样本，并与危险标注同步。实验比较了两种校准协议（9点/45次点击与11点/440次点击）、两种模型（随机森林与因果Transformer），每种设置使用5个随机种子，并通过配对t检验评估眼动信息的效果。此外，研究将眼动误差与危险目标尺寸进行几何尺度比较。

Result: 加入眼动信息未带来统计显著的性能提升，报告的p值为0.919、0.578和0.667。WebGazer的定位误差约为130—257像素，而检测到的危险目标中位尺寸仅为36像素；该误差超过93%目标的尺寸，使目标级注视归因在当前测量精度下基本不可行。

Conclusion: 在现有摄像头眼动追踪精度和实验设置下，人类注视信息不足以有效抑制mesa objective形成或改善危险识别。失败更可能源于测量分辨率与目标尺度不匹配，而非眼动信息本身缺乏潜在价值。后续研究应采用更高精度的眼动设备、扩大目标空间尺度，或使用区域级而非目标级注视监督。

Abstract: Current hazard detection systems in autonomous driving may develop mesa objectives, learned internal goals that achieve high training performance through spurious correlations rather than genuine hazard recognition. We investigate whether human gaze patterns, captured via webcam-based eye tracking ( this http URL ), can serve as privileged information to constrain mesa-objective formation. We collected 137,663 frame-level gaze samples synchronized with hazard annotations across 388 real dashcam clips, then test this hypothesis across two calibration protocols (9-point/45-click and 11-point/440-click), two model architectures (Random Forest and causal Transformer), and five random seeds per experiment with paired t-tests. No experiment yields a statistically significant improvement from gaze (p = 0.919, 0.578, and 0.667 respectively). A geometric analysis reveals the root cause: WebGazer's reported error (~130-257 px depending on configuration) exceeds 93% of detected hazard object sizes (median 36 px), rendering object-level gaze attribution physically impossible at this instrument precision.

</details>


### [126] [From Recovery to Drop-off: How Action Post-training Reduces a VLM's Late-Layer Depth Decodability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08904)
*Alexander Hackett,Arnaud Denis-Remillard,Axel Cassou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究发现，视觉语言模型经过动作后训练转化为视觉语言动作模型后，其深度感知能力会系统性下降，并呈现“地板”和“悬崖”两阶段退化：前者贯穿所有解码层，后者主要发生在后期层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言动作模型需要同时保留视觉空间理解能力与动作预测能力，但动作后训练是否会损害原有的空间几何表征尚不清楚。研究以深度感知这一基础空间能力为切入点，考察视觉语言模型到视觉语言动作模型的能力迁移与退化机制。

Method: 研究比较了权重匹配的开源基础视觉语言模型Molmo2-ER与视觉语言动作模型MolmoAct2-LIBERO，在每个解码器层探测深度信息的可解码性。随后分别对后期层的MLP和注意力模块进行消融，并通过模块级分解分析累积MLP写入对深度表征的贡献。

Result: 视觉语言动作模型在所有解码层的深度解码性能均低于基础视觉语言模型，形成持续性的“地板”差距。基础模型的深度可解码性随层数增加而提升，而动作模型在后期层出现明显崩塌，形成额外的“悬崖”式下降。消融后期层MLP写入能够恢复大部分末端性能损失，而注意力消融以及在基础模型中的对应干预均未产生类似恢复效果。模块分析表明，基础模型主要通过累积的MLP写入保留深度信息；动作后训练则破坏了后期累积写入中的深度可解码性。

Conclusion: 动作后训练会显著损害视觉语言模型的深度空间理解，其中普遍退化构成“地板”，后期MLP干扰导致的额外退化构成“悬崖”。结果表明，保护或约束后期MLP通路可能是提升视觉语言动作模型空间推理能力的重要方向。

Abstract: How much of a vision-language model's (VLM) spatial understanding remains after the action post-training process of building a vision-language-action model (VLA)? We probe depth perception, a primitive of spatiogeometric understanding, from every decoder layer of a weight-matched open-source base VLM/VLA pair: Molmo2-ER and MolmoAct2-LIBERO. First, the VLA decodes depth worse at every layer, a persistent gap we call the floor. Second, the degradation is not uniform: while the base VLM's depth decodability improves through its final layers, the VLA's collapses, an additional late-layer drop we call the cliff. We causally localize the cliff to late-layer MLP interference: ablating the late-layer MLP writes recovers the majority of the terminal decodability cliff, while matched attention ablations and the same intervention in the weight-matched base VLM produce no comparable recovery. A module-level decomposition explains this dissociation: the base VLM carries depth most accessibly in accumulated MLP writes, whereas action post-training collapses depth decodability in the late accumulated writes.

</details>


### [127] [360CityArena: A Realistic Virtual Urban Navigation Benchmark for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08814)
*Kenta Watanabe,Atsuyuki Miyai,Mizuki Takenawa,Kiyoharu Aizawa,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出360CityArena，一个基于东京秋叶原街区360度视频重建的高真实感城市探索基准，用于评估具身智能体的环境理解、路径推理和空间推理能力。最强模型Gemini 2.5 Flash仅 đạt 17.1%，显著低于人类的77.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有户外具身智能基准在真实感或环境复杂度方面不足，与现实城市探索存在明显差距，因此需要一个覆盖大规模街区、具备高视觉真实性和多样推理任务的评测平台。

Method: 利用602段360度视频重建包含85条街道的城市环境，并由人工设计175项任务。任务分为环境理解、路径推理和空间推理三类，涵盖定位、地标搜索、路径规划及空间关系判断；随后使用当前先进的基于大语言模型的智能体进行评测，并与人类表现比较。

Result: Gemini 2.5 Flash取得17.1%的表现，远低于人类的77.3%，说明现有模型在城市尺度的导航、定位、路径规划和空间推理方面仍存在显著不足。

Conclusion: 360CityArena提供了一个高真实感、任务覆盖全面且具有挑战性的城市街区导航基准，可用于系统评估和推动具身智能体在真实城市环境中的感知、推理与探索能力发展。

Abstract: We present 360CityArena, a benchmark for evaluating the urban exploration capabilities of embodied agents within a photorealistic environment constructed from 360-degree videos. Existing outdoor benchmarks either lack sufficient photorealism or complexity, resulting in a considerable gap from real-world urban environments. 360CityArena is built on a realistic reconstruction of the Akihabara district in Tokyo, Japan, using 602 360-degree video segments covering 85 streets, and consists of 175 meticulously human-crafted tasks. It encompasses three task categories: Environment Understanding, Path Reasoning, and Spatial Reasoning, covering fundamental abilities required for urban exploration, such as localization, landmark search, path planning, and relational spatial reasoning, thereby enabling comprehensive evaluation in realistic urban scenes. Our evaluation using state-of-the-art LMM-based agents shows that even the strongest model, Gemini 2.5 Flash, performs far below human level (human: 77.3% vs. Gemini 2.5 Flash: 17.1%), revealing substantial challenges that remain in city-scale embodied navigation and reasoning. 360CityArena provides a necessary and challenging testbed for photorealistic urban-district navigation and spatial reasoning.

</details>
